[No.1] HiRA: Parameter-Efficient Hadamard High-Rank Adaptation for Large Language Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: HiRA：参数高效哈达玛高秩适应性方法用于大型语言模型
类别: other topics in machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Qiushi Huang;Tom Ko;Zhan Zhuang;Lilian Tang;Yu Zhang;
机构: University of Surrey;ByteDance;City University of Hong Kong;Southern University of Science and Technology;
类型: Oral
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29500

[No.2] SD-LoRA: Scalable Decoupled Low-Rank Adaptation for Class Incremental Learning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: SD-LoRA: 可扩展解耦低秩适应性学习方法用于类别增量学习
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Yichen Wu;Hongming Piao;Long-Kai Huang;Renzhen Wang;Wanhua Li;Hanspeter Pfister;Deyu Meng;Kede Ma;Ying Wei;
机构: City University of Hong Kong;Tencent;Xi'an Jiao Tong University;Harvard University;Zhejiang University;
类型: Oral
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30945
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.13198
英摘: Continual Learning (CL) with foundation models has recently emerged as a promising paradigm to exploit abundant knowledge acquired during pre-training for tackling sequential tasks. However, existing prompt-based and Low-Rank Adaptation-based (LoRA-based) methods often require expanding a prompt/LoRA pool or retaining samples of previous tasks, which poses significant scalability challenges as the number of tasks grows. To address these limitations, we propose Scalable Decoupled LoRA (SD-LoRA) for class incremental learning, which continually separates the learning of the magnitude and direction of LoRA components without rehearsal. Our empirical and theoretical analysis reveals that SD-LoRA tends to follow a low-loss trajectory and converges to an overlapping low-loss region for all learned tasks, resulting in an excellent stability-plasticity trade-off. Building upon these insights, we introduce two variants of SD-LoRA with further improved parameter efficiency. All parameters of SD-LoRAs can be end-to-end optimized for CL objectives. Meanwhile, they support efficient inference by allowing direct evaluation with the finally trained model, obviating the need for component selection. Extensive experiments across multiple CL benchmarks and foundation models consistently validate the effectiveness of SD-LoRA. The code is available at https://github.com/WuYichen-97/SD-Lora-CL.
中摘: 基于基础模型的持续学习（CL）最近已成为一种有前景的范式，用于利用预训练期间获得的丰富知识来应对顺序任务。然而，现有的基于提示和低秩适应（LoRA）的方法往往需要扩展提示/LoRA池或保留先前任务的样本，这在任务数量增加时带来了显著的可扩展性挑战。为了解决这些限制，我们提出了一种名为Scalable Decoupled LoRA（SD-LoRA）的方法，用于类别增量学习，该方法能够在无需回放的情况下持续分离LoRA组件的幅度和方向的学习。我们的实证和理论分析表明，SD-LoRA倾向于遵循一个低损失轨迹，并且能够收敛到所有学习任务的重叠低损失区域，从而实现卓越的稳定性和灵活性权衡。基于这些见解，我们引入了两种进一步提高参数效率的SD-LoRA变体。SD-LoRAs的所有参数都可以端到端地优化以满足持续学习的目标，同时它们还支持高效的推理，允许直接使用最终训练好的模型进行评估，从而省去了组件选择的步骤。在多个持续学习基准和基础模型上的广泛实验一致验证了SD-LoRA的有效性。相关代码可在https://github.com/WuYichen-97/SD-Lora-CL获取。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.3] LoRA3D: Low-Rank Self-Calibration of 3D Geometric Foundation models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: LoRA3D：低秩自我校准的三维几何基础模型
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Ziqi Lu;Heng Yang;Danfei Xu;Boyi Li;Boris Ivanovic;Marco Pavone;Yue Wang;
机构: Amazon;NVIDIA;University of California, Berkeley;Stanford University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29996
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07746
英摘: Emerging 3D geometric foundation models, such as DUSt3R, offer a promising approach for in-the-wild 3D vision tasks. However, due to the high-dimensional nature of the problem space and scarcity of high-quality 3D data, these pre-trained models still struggle to generalize to many challenging circumstances, such as limited view overlap or low lighting. To address this, we propose LoRA3D, an efficient self-calibration pipeline to $\textit{specialize}$ the pre-trained models to target scenes using their own multi-view predictions. Taking sparse RGB images as input, we leverage robust optimization techniques to refine multi-view predictions and align them into a global coordinate frame. In particular, we incorporate prediction confidence into the geometric optimization process, automatically re-weighting the confidence to better reflect point estimation accuracy. We use the calibrated confidence to generate high-quality pseudo labels for the calibrating views and use low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to fine-tune the models on the pseudo-labeled data. Our method does not require any external priors or manual labels. It completes the self-calibration process on a $\textbf{single standard GPU within just 5 minutes}$. Each low-rank adapter requires only $\textbf{18MB}$ of storage. We evaluated our method on $\textbf{more than 160 scenes}$ from the Replica, TUM and Waymo Open datasets, achieving up to $\textbf{88% performance improvement}$ on 3D reconstruction, multi-view pose estimation and novel-view rendering.
中摘: 新兴的3D几何基础模型，如DUSt3R，为野外3D视觉任务提供了一种有前景的方法。然而，由于问题空间的高度维度和高质量3D数据的稀缺性，这些预训练模型仍然难以在许多具有挑战性的场景中泛化，例如视图重叠有限或照明条件差。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种高效的自我校准管道——LoRA3D，用于根据目标场景的多视图预测来优化预训练模型。我们以稀疏的RGB图像作为输入，利用鲁棒优化技术来细化多视图预测，并将其对齐到全局坐标系中。特别地，我们将预测置信度融入到几何优化过程中，自动重新加权置信度以更好地反映点估计准确性。我们使用校准后的置信度生成高质量的伪标签，并通过低秩适应（LoRA）对模型进行微调，使其在伪标签数据上进一步优化。我们的方法不需要任何外部先验或手动标签。它可以在单一标准GPU上仅用5分钟完成自我校准过程。每个低秩适配器只需要18MB的存储空间。我们在Replica、TUM和Waymo Open数据集上的超过160个场景中评估了该方法，在3D重建、多视图姿态估计和新视图渲染方面实现了高达88%的性能提升。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.4] PaRa: Personalizing Text-to-Image Diffusion via Parameter Rank Reduction
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 个性化文本到图像扩散通过参数秩减少
类别: generative models
作者: Shangyu Chen;Zizheng Pan;Jianfei Cai;Dinh Phung;
机构: ;DeepSeek;Monash University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30047
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05641
英摘: Personalizing a large-scale pretrained Text-to-Image (T2I) diffusion model is challenging as it typically struggles to make an appropriate trade-off between its training data distribution and the target distribution, i.e., learning a novel concept with only a few target images to achieve personalization (aligning with the personalized target) while preserving text editability (aligning with diverse text prompts). In this paper, we propose PaRa, an effective and efficient Parameter Rank Reduction approach for T2I model personalization by explicitly controlling the rank of the diffusion model parameters to restrict its initial diverse generation space into a small and well-balanced target space. Our design is motivated by the fact that taming a T2I model toward a novel concept such as a specific art style implies a small generation space. To this end, by reducing the rank of model parameters during finetuning, we can effectively constrain the space of the denoising sampling trajectories towards the target. With comprehensive experiments, we show that PaRa achieves great advantages over existing finetuning approaches on single/multi-subject generation as well as single-image editing. Notably, compared to the prevailing fine-tuning technique LoRA, PaRa achieves better parameter efficiency (2x fewer learnable parameters) and much better target image alignment.
中摘: 个性化大规模预训练文本到图像（T2I）扩散模型具有挑战性，因为它通常难以在训练数据分布和目标分布之间做出适当的权衡，即仅通过少量的目标图像学习一个新颖的概念以实现个性化（与个性化目标对齐），同时保持文本可编辑性（与多样的文本提示对齐）。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为PaRa的有效且高效的参数秩减少方法，通过显式控制扩散模型参数的秩来限制其初始多样生成空间进入一个小而平衡的目标空间。我们的设计动机在于，将T2I模型引导至特定艺术风格等新颖概念意味着生成空间较小。为此，在微调过程中减少模型参数的秩，可以有效地约束去噪采样轨迹的空间朝向目标。通过全面的实验，我们展示了PaRa在单/多主题生成以及单图像编辑方面优于现有微调方法的优势。值得注意的是，与当前流行的微调技术LoRA相比，PaRa实现了更好的参数效率（参数量少2倍）和更佳的目标图像对齐效果。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.5] SVDQuant: Absorbing Outliers by Low-Rank Component for 4-Bit Diffusion Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: SVDQuant：低秩成分吸收异常值的4位扩散模型
类别: generative models
作者: Muyang Li;Yujun Lin;Zhekai Zhang;Tianle Cai;Xiuyu Li;Junxian Guo;Enze Xie;Chenlin Meng;Jun-Yan Zhu;Song Han;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;;Princeton University;University of California, Berkeley;NVIDIA;Stanford University;Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27906
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.05007
英摘: Diffusion models can effectively generate high-quality images. However, as they scale, rising memory demands and higher latency pose substantial deployment challenges. In this work, we aim to accelerate diffusion models by quantizing their weights and activations to 4 bits. At such an aggressive level, both weights and activations are highly sensitive, where existing post-training quantization methods like smoothing become insufficient. To overcome this limitation, we propose SVDQuant, a new 4-bit quantization paradigm. Different from smoothing, which redistributes outliers between weights and activations, our approach absorbs these outliers using a low-rank branch. We first consolidate the outliers by shifting them from activations to weights. Then, we use a high-precision, low-rank branch to take in the weight outliers with Singular Value Decomposition (SVD), while a low-bit quantized branch handles the residuals. This process eases the quantization on both sides. However, naively running the low-rank branch independently incurs significant overhead due to extra data movement of activations, negating the quantization speedup. To address this, we co-design an inference engine Nunchaku that fuses the kernels of the low-rank branch into those of the low-bit branch to cut off redundant memory access. It can also seamlessly support off-the-shelf low-rank adapters (LoRAs) without re-quantization. Extensive experiments on SDXL, PixArt-$Σ$, and FLUX.1 validate the effectiveness of SVDQuant in preserving image quality. We reduce the memory usage for the 12B FLUX.1 models by 3.5$\times$, achieving 3.0$\times$ speedup over the 4-bit weight-only quantization (W4A16) baseline on the 16GB laptop 4090 GPU with INT4 precision. On the latest RTX 5090 desktop with Blackwell architecture, we achieve a 3.1$\times$ speedup compared to the W4A16 model using NVFP4 precision.
中摘: 扩散模型可以有效地生成高质量的图像。然而，随着模型规模的扩大，不断上升的内存需求和更高的延迟带来了重大的部署挑战。在这项工作中，我们旨在通过将权重和激活量化为4位来加速扩散模型。在如此激进的水平下，权重和激活都高度敏感，现有的后训练量化方法如平滑变得不够充分。为了解决这一限制，我们提出了一种新的4位量化范式——SVDQuant。与平滑法不同，后者重新分配权重和激活之间的异常值，我们的方法则是通过一个低秩分支吸收这些异常值。首先，我们通过将异常值从激活转移到权重来聚集这些异常值。然后，我们使用高精度低秩分支结合奇异值分解（SVD）吸收权重异常值，而低位量化分支处理残差。这个过程使得两侧的量化更加轻松。但是，简单地独立运行低秩分支会导致显著的额外数据移动开销，从而抵消了量化带来的速度提升。为解决这个问题，我们设计了一个推理引擎Nunchaku，该引擎将低秩分支的内核融合到低位量化分支的内核中，以减少冗余的内存访问。此外，它还可以无缝支持现成的低秩适配器（LoRA），无需重新量化。我们在SDXL、PixArt-$\Sigma$和FLUX.1上进行了广泛的实验，验证了SVDQuant在保持图像质量方面的有效性。我们通过INT4精度将12B FLUX.1模型的内存使用量减少了3.5倍，在16GB的笔记本电脑4090 GPU上，相比仅权重量化（W4A16）基线实现了3.0倍的速度提升。在最新的具有Blackwell架构的RTX 5090桌面GPU上，我们使用NVFP4精度实现了相比W4A16模型3.1倍的速度提升。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.6] LoRA-Pro: Are Low-Rank Adapters Properly Optimized?
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: LoRA-Pro：低秩适配器是否得到了适当的优化？
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Zhengbo Wang;Jian Liang;Ran He;Zilei Wang;Tieniu Tan;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;Chinese Academy of Sciences;;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28819
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.18242
英摘: Low-rank adaptation, also known as LoRA, has emerged as a prominent method for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of foundation models. Despite its computational efficiency, LoRA still yields inferior performance compared to full fine-tuning. In this paper, we first uncover a fundamental connection between the optimization processes of LoRA and full fine-tuning: using LoRA for optimization is mathematically equivalent to full fine-tuning using a low-rank gradient for parameter updates. And this low-rank gradient can be expressed in terms of the gradients of the two low-rank matrices in LoRA. Leveraging this insight, we introduce LoRA-Pro, a method that enhances LoRA's performance by strategically adjusting the gradients of these low-rank matrices. This adjustment allows the low-rank gradient to more accurately approximate the full fine-tuning gradient, thereby narrowing the performance gap between LoRA and full fine-tuning. Furthermore, we theoretically derive the optimal solutions for adjusting the gradients of the low-rank matrices, applying them during fine-tuning in LoRA-Pro. We conduct extensive experiments across natural language understanding, dialogue generation, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and image classification tasks, demonstrating that LoRA-Pro substantially improves LoRA's performance, effectively narrowing the gap with full fine-tuning. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/mrflogs/LoRA-Pro.
中摘: 低秩适应（Low-rank adaptation），也被称为LoRA，已成为一种参数高效的基础模型微调方法。尽管LoRA在计算效率方面表现出色，但其性能仍然不如全面微调。在本文中，我们首先揭示了LoRA和全面微调优化过程之间的基本联系：使用LoRA进行优化在数学上等同于使用低秩梯度进行全面微调以更新参数。而这个低秩梯度可以用LoRA中的两个低秩矩阵的梯度来表示。借助这一洞见，我们引入了LoRA-Pro方法，通过战略性调整这些低秩矩阵的梯度来提升LoRA的性能。这种调整使得低秩梯度更准确地逼近全面微调梯度，从而缩小了LoRA与全面微调之间的性能差距。此外，我们还从理论上推导出了调整低秩矩阵梯度的最优解，并在LoRA-Pro的微调过程中应用它们。我们在自然语言理解、对话生成、数学推理、代码生成和图像分类等多种任务上进行了广泛的实验，证明LoRA-Pro显著提高了LoRA的性能，有效地缩小了与全面微调之间的差距。LoRA-Pro的相关代码已在https://github.com/mrflogs/LoRA-Pro公开发布。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.7] Enhancing Zeroth-order Fine-tuning for Language Models with Low-rank Structures
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 通过低秩结构增强语言模型的零阶微调
类别: optimization
作者: Yiming Chen;Yuan Zhang;Liyuan Cao;Kun Yuan;Zaiwen Wen;
机构: Peking University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30716
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07698
英摘: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) significantly reduces memory costs when adapting large language models (LLMs) for downstream applications. However, traditional first-order (FO) fine-tuning algorithms incur substantial memory overhead due to the need to store activation values for back-propagation during gradient computation, particularly in long-context fine-tuning tasks. Zeroth-order (ZO) algorithms offer a promising alternative by approximating gradients using finite differences of function values, thus eliminating the need for activation storage. Nevertheless, existing ZO methods struggle to capture the low-rank gradient structure common in LLM fine-tuning, leading to suboptimal performance. This paper proposes a low-rank ZO gradient estimator and introduces a novel low-rank ZO algorithm (LOZO) that effectively captures this structure in LLMs. We provide convergence guarantees for LOZO by framing it as a subspace optimization method. Additionally, its low-rank nature enables LOZO to integrate with momentum techniques while incurring negligible extra memory costs. Extensive experiments across various model sizes and downstream tasks demonstrate that LOZO and its momentum-based variant outperform existing ZO methods and closely approach the performance of FO algorithms.
中摘: 参数高效微调（PEFT）在适应大型语言模型（LLMs）用于下游应用时显著减少了内存成本。然而，传统的第一阶（FO）微调算法由于在梯度计算过程中需要存储反向传播的激活值而产生了大量的内存开销，特别是在长上下文微调任务中。零阶（ZO）算法通过使用函数值的有限差分来近似梯度，从而消除了激活值存储的需求，提供了一种有前景的替代方案。不过，现有的ZO方法难以捕捉LLM微调中常见的低秩梯度结构，导致性能不佳。本文提出了一种低秩ZO梯度估计器，并引入了一种新的低秩ZO算法（LOZO），该算法能够有效捕捉LLM中的这种结构。我们通过将其框架化为子空间优化方法，提供了LOZO的收敛性保证。此外，其低秩特性使得LOZO能够与动量技术结合使用，同时几乎不增加额外的内存成本。在各种模型规模和下游任务上的广泛实验表明，LOZO及其基于动量的变体在性能上优于现有ZO方法，并且接近FO算法的表现。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.8] RaSA: Rank-Sharing Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: RaSA：秩共享低秩适应
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Zhiwei He;Zhaopeng Tu;Xing Wang;Xingyu Chen;Zhijie Wang;Jiahao Xu;Tian Liang;Wenxiang Jiao;Zhuosheng Zhang;Rui Wang;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;Tencent;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30271
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12576
英摘: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has been prominently employed for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). However, the limited expressive capacity of LoRA, stemming from the low-rank constraint, has been recognized as a bottleneck, particularly in rigorous tasks like code generation and mathematical reasoning. To address this limitation, we introduce Rank-Sharing Low-Rank Adaptation (RaSA), an innovative extension that enhances the expressive capacity of LoRA by leveraging partial rank sharing across layers. By forming a shared rank pool and applying layer-specific weighting, RaSA effectively increases the number of ranks without augmenting parameter overhead. Our theoretically grounded and empirically validated approach demonstrates that RaSA not only maintains the core advantages of LoRA but also significantly boosts performance in challenging code and math tasks. Code, data and scripts are available at: https://github.com/zwhe99/RaSA.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）已被广泛应用于大型语言模型（LLMs）的参数高效微调。然而，LoRA 的表达能力受限于低秩约束，在诸如代码生成和数学推理等严格任务中尤为明显。为了解决这一限制，我们引入了秩共享低秩适应（RaSA），这是一种创新性的扩展方法，通过在层间利用部分秩共享来增强 LoRA 的表达能力。通过形成共享的秩池并应用层特定的权重，RaSA 有效地增加了秩的数量而无需增加参数开销。我们的理论依据和实验证明表明，RaSA 不仅保持了 LoRA 的核心优势，还在挑战性代码和数学任务中显著提升了性能。有关代码、数据和脚本，请参阅：https://github.com/zwhe99/RaSA。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.9] Efficient Learning with Sine-Activated Low-Rank Matrices
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于正弦激活低秩矩阵的高效学习
类别: other topics in machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Yiping Ji;Hemanth Saratchandran;Cameron Gordon;Zeyu Zhang;Simon Lucey;
机构: ;University of Adelaide;Australian National University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29043
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19243
英摘: Low-rank decomposition has emerged as a vital tool for enhancing parameter efficiency in neural network architectures, gaining traction across diverse applications in machine learning. These techniques significantly lower the number of parameters, striking a balance between compactness and performance. However, a common challenge has been the compromise between parameter efficiency and the accuracy of the model, where reduced parameters often lead to diminished accuracy compared to their full-rank counterparts. In this work, we propose a novel theoretical framework that integrates a sinusoidal function within the low-rank decomposition process. This approach not only preserves the benefits of the parameter efficiency characteristic of low-rank methods but also increases the decomposition's rank, thereby enhancing model performance. Our method proves to be a plug in enhancement for existing low-rank models, as evidenced by its successful application in Vision Transformers (ViT), Large Language Models (LLMs), Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and 3D shape modelling.
中摘: 低秩分解已成为增强神经网络架构参数效率的重要工具，并在机器学习的各种应用中逐渐受到关注。这些技术显著降低了参数数量，同时在紧凑性和性能之间取得了平衡。然而，一个常见的挑战在于参数效率与模型准确性的权衡，减少的参数往往会导致模型准确性下降，相较于全秩方法而言。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种新的理论框架，该框架将正弦函数整合到低秩分解过程中。这种方法不仅保留了低秩方法参数效率的优点，还提高了分解的秩，从而增强了模型性能。我们的方法证明是对现有低秩模型的一种插件增强，其成功应用于视觉变换器（ViT）、大型语言模型（LLMs）、神经辐射场（NeRF）和三维形状建模等领域。

[No.10] AdaRankGrad: Adaptive Gradient Rank and Moments for Memory-Efficient LLMs Training and Fine-Tuning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: AdaRankGrad：自适应梯度排名和矩方法用于高效内存大型语言模型训练与微调
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Yehonathan Refael;Jonathan Svirsky;Boris Shustin;Wasim Huleihel;Ofir Lindenbaum;
机构: Tel Aviv University;Bar-Ilan University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29966
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.17881
英摘: Training and fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) come with challenges related to memory and computational requirements due to the increasing size of the model weights and the optimizer states. Various techniques have been developed to tackle these challenges, such as low-rank adaptation (LoRA), which involves introducing a parallel trainable low-rank matrix to the fixed pre-trained weights at each layer. However, these methods often fall short compared to the full-rank weight training approach, as they restrict the parameter search to a low-rank subspace. This limitation can disrupt training dynamics and require a full-rank warm start to mitigate the impact. In this paper, we introduce a new method inspired by a phenomenon we formally prove: as training progresses, the rank of the estimated layer gradients gradually decreases, and asymptotically approaches rank one. Leveraging this, our approach involves adaptively reducing the rank of the gradients during Adam optimization steps, using an efficient online-updating low-rank projections rule. We further present a randomized SVD scheme for efficiently finding the projection matrix. Our technique enables full-parameter fine-tuning with adaptive low-rank gradient updates, significantly reducing overall memory requirements during training compared to state-of-the-art methods while improving model performance in both pretraining and fine-tuning. Finally, we provide a convergence analysis of our method and demonstrate its merits for training and fine-tuning language and biological foundation models.
中摘: 训练和调整大型语言模型（LLMs）会面临与模型权重和优化器状态不断增加的规模相关的内存和计算需求挑战。为应对这些挑战，开发了各种技术，例如低秩适应（LoRA），它涉及在每一层中引入一个并行可训练的低秩矩阵，以固定预训练权重为基础。然而，这些方法往往不如完整的高秩权重训练方法有效，因为它们限制了参数搜索的空间为低秩子空间，这可能会干扰训练动态，并需要一个高秩的预热启动来减轻影响。在本文中，我们介绍了一种新的方法，该方法受到我们正式证明的一种现象的启发：随着训练的进行，估计的层梯度的秩逐渐减少，并且渐近地接近秩一。利用这一现象，我们的方法在Adam优化步骤中自适应地降低梯度的秩，使用高效的在线更新低秩投影规则。此外，我们还提出了一种随机化SVD方案，用于高效地找到投影矩阵。我们的技术能够通过自适应低秩梯度更新实现全面参数调整，相比最先进的方法，在训练过程中显著减少了总体内存需求，同时提高了预训练和调整后的模型性能。最后，我们对方法进行了收敛性分析，并展示了其在训练和调整语言及生物基础模型方面的优势。

[No.11] Exploiting Structure in Offline Multi-Agent RL: The Benefits of Low Interaction Rank
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 利用离线多智能体强化学习中的结构优势：低交互秩带来的益处
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Wenhao Zhan;Scott Fujimoto;Zheqing Zhu;Jason D. Lee;Daniel Jiang;Yonathan Efroni;
机构: Princeton University;Mila;Meta;University of Pittsburgh;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30636
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01101
英摘: We study the problem of learning an approximate equilibrium in the offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) setting. We introduce a structural assumption -- the interaction rank -- and establish that functions with low interaction rank are significantly more robust to distribution shift compared to general ones. Leveraging this observation, we demonstrate that utilizing function classes with low interaction rank, when combined with regularization and no-regret learning, admits decentralized, computationally and statistically efficient learning in offline MARL. Our theoretical results are complemented by experiments that showcase the potential of critic architectures with low interaction rank in offline MARL, contrasting with commonly used single-agent value decomposition architectures.
中摘: 我们研究了在离线多智能体强化学习（MARL）设置中学习近似均衡的问题。我们引入了一个结构假设——交互秩，并证明具有低交互秩的函数相较于一般的函数，在分布偏移情况下具有显著更高的鲁棒性。基于这一观察，我们展示了利用低交互秩的功能类，结合正则化和无遗憾学习，可以在离线MARL中实现去中心化、计算和统计效率更高的学习。我们的理论成果得到了实验的支持，这些实验展示了低交互秩的评论器架构在离线MARL中的潜力，这与常用的单智能体价值分解架构形成了对比。

[No.13] LoLCATs: On Low-Rank Linearizing of Large Language Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: LoLCATs：大型语言模型的低秩线性化
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Michael Zhang;Simran Arora;Rahul Chalamala;Benjamin Frederick Spector;Alan Wu;Krithik Ramesh;Aaryan Singhal;Christopher Re;
机构: Stanford University;University of Pennsylvania;California Institute of Technology;Broad Institute;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30763
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10254
英摘: Recent works show we can linearize large language models (LLMs) -- swapping the quadratic attentions of popular Transformer-based LLMs with subquadratic analogs, such as linear attention -- avoiding the expensive pretraining costs. However, linearizing LLMs often significantly degrades model quality, still requires training over billions of tokens, and remains limited to smaller 1.3B to 7B LLMs. We thus propose Low-rank Linear Conversion via Attention Transfer (LoLCATs), a simple two-step method that improves LLM linearizing quality with orders of magnitudes less memory and compute. We base these steps on two findings. First, we can replace an LLM's softmax attentions with closely-approximating linear attentions, simply by training the linear attentions to match their softmax counterparts with an output MSE loss ("attention transfer"). Then, this enables adjusting for approximation errors and recovering LLM quality simply with low-rank adaptation (LoRA). LoLCATs significantly improves linearizing quality, training efficiency, and scalability. We significantly reduce the linearizing quality gap and produce state-of-the-art subquadratic LLMs from Llama 3 8B and Mistral 7B v0.1, leading to 20+ points of improvement on 5-shot MMLU. Furthermore, LoLCATs does so with only 0.2% of past methods' model parameters and 0.4% of their training tokens. Finally, we apply LoLCATs to create the first linearized 70B and 405B LLMs (50x larger than prior work). When compared with prior approaches under the same compute budgets, LoLCATs significantly improves linearizing quality, closing the gap between linearized and original Llama 3.1 70B and 405B LLMs by 77.8% and 78.1% on 5-shot MMLU.
中摘: 近期的研究表明，我们可以线性化大型语言模型（LLMs）——通过用亚二次注意力（如线性注意力）替换流行的基于Transformer的LLMs中的二次注意力来进行预训练，从而避免昂贵的预训练成本。然而，线性化LLMs通常会显著降低模型质量，仍然需要对数十亿个标记进行训练，并且目前仅限于较小的1.3B至7B规模的LLMs。因此，我们提出了一种名为低秩线性转换通过注意力转移（LoLCATs）的简单两步方法，该方法能够以数量级减少的内存和计算资源提高LLM线性化质量。我们基于两个发现构建了这些步骤。首先，我们可以将LLM的softmax注意力替换为近似匹配其softmax对应物的线性注意力，只需通过输出均方误差损失（"注意力转移"）来训练线性注意力。然后，这使得我们可以通过低秩适应（LoRA）调整近似误差并恢复LLM的质量。LoLCATs在提高线性化质量、训练效率和可扩展性方面表现出了显著的优势。我们大幅缩小了线性化质量差距，并从Llama 3 8B和Mistral 7B v0.1生成了最先进的亚二次LLMs，分别在5-shot MMLU上取得了20多点的改进。此外，LoLCATs仅使用过去方法0.2%的模型参数和0.4%的训练标记就实现了这一目标。最后，我们应用LoLCATs创建了首个线性化的70B和405B LLMS（比先前工作大50倍）。与在相同计算预算下比较的先前方法相比，LoLCATs显著提高了线性化质量，在5-shot MMLU上将线性化后的Llama 3.1 70B和405B LLMS与原始LLM之间的差距分别缩小了77.8%和78.1%。

[No.14] Offline Model-Based Optimization by Learning to Rank
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于离线模型的优化通过学习排序
类别: optimization
作者: Rong-Xi Tan;Ke Xue;Shen-Huan Lyu;Haopu Shang;yaowang;Yaoyuan Wang;Fu Sheng;Chao Qian;
机构: Nanjing University;Huawei;Hohai University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28117
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11502
英摘: Offline model-based optimization (MBO) aims to identify a design that maximizes a black-box function using only a fixed, pre-collected dataset of designs and their corresponding scores. A common approach in offline MBO is to train a regression-based surrogate model by minimizing mean squared error (MSE) and then find the best design within this surrogate model by different optimizers (e.g., gradient ascent). However, a critical challenge is the risk of out-of-distribution errors, i.e., the surrogate model may typically overestimate the scores and mislead the optimizers into suboptimal regions. Prior works have attempted to address this issue in various ways, such as using regularization techniques and ensemble learning to enhance the robustness of the model, but it still remains. In this paper, we argue that regression models trained with MSE are not well-aligned with the primary goal of offline MBO, which is to select promising designs rather than to predict their scores precisely. Notably, if a surrogate model can maintain the order of candidate designs based on their relative score relationships, it can produce the best designs even without precise predictions. To validate it, we conduct experiments to compare the relationship between the quality of the final designs and MSE, finding that the correlation is really very weak. In contrast, a metric that measures order-maintaining quality shows a significantly stronger correlation. Based on this observation, we propose learning a ranking-based model that leverages learning to rank techniques to prioritize promising designs based on their relative scores. We show that the generalization error on ranking loss can be well bounded. Empirical results across diverse tasks demonstrate the superior performance of our proposed ranking-based models than twenty existing methods.
中摘: 基于离线模型的优化（MBO）旨在仅使用预先收集的设计及其相应得分的固定数据集来识别最大化黑盒函数的设计。离线MBO中的常见方法是通过最小化均方误差（MSE）训练一个回归型代理模型，然后利用不同的优化器（例如梯度上升）在该代理模型中找到最佳设计。然而，一个关键挑战是分布外错误的风险，即代理模型可能会过度估计得分并误导优化器进入次优区域。先前的研究尝试以各种方式解决这一问题，如使用正则化技术和集成学习来增强模型的鲁棒性，但这一问题仍然存在。在这篇论文中，我们提出观点认为，使用MSE训练的回归模型并未很好地与离线MBO的主要目标——选择有前景的设计而非精确预测其得分——相匹配。值得注意的是，如果代理模型能够根据候选设计之间的相对得分关系保持设计顺序，即使没有精确的预测也能生成最佳设计。为了验证这一点，我们进行了实验比较最终设计质量与MSE的关系，发现两者之间的相关性实际上非常弱。相反，衡量保持顺序质量的指标显示出显著更强的相关性。基于这一观察，我们提出了利用排序技术学习一个基于排名的模型，以根据相对得分优先选择有前景的设计。我们展示了在排序损失上的泛化误差可以很好地被限制。在不同任务上的实证结果表明，我们提出的基于排名的模型在性能上优于二十种现有方法。

[No.15] On the Convergence of No-Regret Dynamics in Information Retrieval Games with Proportional Ranking Functions
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于比例排名函数的信息检索博弈中无悔动态的收敛性研究
类别: learning theory
作者: Omer Madmon;Idan Pipano;Itamar Reinman;Moshe Tennenholtz;
机构: Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28641
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.11517
英摘: Publishers who publish their content on the web act strategically, in a behavior that can be modeled within the online learning framework. Regret, a central concept in machine learning, serves as a canonical measure for assessing the performance of learning agents within this framework. We prove that any proportional content ranking function with a concave activation function induces games in which no-regret learning dynamics converge. Moreover, for proportional ranking functions, we prove the equivalence of the concavity of the activation function, the social concavity of the induced games and the concavity of the induced games. We also study the empirical trade-offs between publishers' and users' welfare, under different choices of the activation function, using a state-of-the-art no-regret dynamics algorithm. Furthermore, we demonstrate how the choice of the ranking function and changes in the ecosystem structure affect these welfare measures, as well as the dynamics' convergence rate.
中摘: 在互联网上发布其内容的出版商会采取战略性行为，并且这种行为可以在在线学习框架内进行建模。遗憾（后悔），机器学习中的一个核心概念，作为在线学习框架内评估学习代理性能的标准度量。我们证明了任何具有凹形激活函数的比例内容排名函数都会诱导出遗憾学习动态收敛的游戏。此外，对于比例排名函数，我们证明了激活函数的凹形性、诱导游戏的社会凹形性和诱导游戏的凹形性之间是等价的。我们还使用最先进的遗憾学习动态算法研究了出版商和用户福利之间的经验权衡，不同激活函数的选择对这些福利指标以及动态收敛速度的影响。

[No.18] MoS: Unleashing Parameter Efficiency of Low-Rank Adaptation with Mixture of Shards
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: MoS：利用碎片混合释放低秩适应性参数效率
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Sheng Wang;Liheng Chen;Pengan CHEN;Jingwei Dong;Boyang XUE;Jiyue Jiang;Lingpeng Kong;Chuan Wu;
机构: University of Hong Kong;Chinese University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/31177
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00938
英摘: The rapid scaling of large language models necessitates more lightweight finetuning methods to reduce the explosive GPU memory overhead when numerous customized models are served simultaneously. Targeting more parameter-efficient low-rank adaptation (LoRA), parameter sharing presents a promising solution. Empirically, our research into high-level sharing principles highlights the indispensable role of differentiation in reversing the detrimental effects of pure sharing. Guided by this finding, we propose Mixture of Shards (MoS), incorporating both inter-layer and intra-layer sharing schemes, and integrating four nearly cost-free differentiation strategies, namely subset selection, pair dissociation, vector sharding, and shard privatization. Briefly, it selects a designated number of shards from global pools with a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-like routing mechanism before sequentially concatenating them to low-rank matrices. Hence, it retains all the advantages of LoRA while offering enhanced parameter efficiency, and effectively circumvents the drawbacks of peer parameter-sharing methods. Our empirical experiments demonstrate approximately 8x parameter savings in a standard LoRA setting. The ablation study confirms the significance of each component. Our insights into parameter sharing and MoS method may illuminate future developments of more parameter-efficient finetuning methods. The code is officially available at https://github.com/Forence1999/MoS.
中摘: 大型语言模型的快速扩展需要更轻量级的微调方法，以减少在同时服务众多自定义模型时出现的爆炸性GPU内存开销。针对低秩适应（LoRA）这一更为参数高效的微调方案，参数共享提供了一种有前景的解决方案。我们的研究表明，高层次的参数共享原则强调了差异化在抵消纯粹共享带来的负面影响中的不可或缺作用。基于这一发现，我们提出了混合分片（Mixture of Shards, MoS）方法，结合了跨层和同层共享方案，并集成了四种几乎无成本的差异化策略，即子集选择、配对解耦、向量分片和分片私有化。具体来说，它使用类似于MoE的路由机制从全局池中选择指定数量的分片，然后依次将其连接到低秩矩阵中。因此，MoS保留了LoRA的所有优点，同时提高了参数效率，并有效避免了同类参数共享方法的缺点。我们的实验证明，在标准LoRA设置下，MoS大约可以节省8倍的参数。消融研究证实了每个组件的重要性。我们对参数共享和MoS方法的理解可能为未来开发更参数高效的微调方法提供启示。相关代码已正式发布在https://github.com/Forence1999/MoS。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.19] SaLoRA: Safety-Alignment Preserved Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: SaLoRA: 安全对齐保留的低秩适应
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Mingjie Li;Wai Man Si;Michael Backes;Yang Zhang;Yisen Wang;
机构: CISPA Helmholtz Center for Information Security;;Peking University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30283
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01765
英摘: As advancements in large language models (LLMs) continue and the demand for personalized models increases, parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods (e.g., LoRA) will become essential due to their efficiency in reducing computation costs. However, recent studies have raised alarming concerns that LoRA fine-tuning could potentially compromise the safety alignment in LLMs, posing significant risks for the model owner. In this paper, we first investigate the underlying mechanism by analyzing the changes in safety alignment related features before and after fine-tuning. Then, we propose a fixed safety module calculated by safety data and a task-specific initialization for trainable parameters in low-rank adaptations, termed Safety-alignment preserved Low-Rank Adaptation (SaLoRA). Unlike previous LoRA methods and their variants, SaLoRA enables targeted modifications to LLMs without disrupting their original alignments. Our experiments show that SaLoRA outperforms various adapters-based approaches across various evaluation metrics in different fine-tuning tasks.
中摘: 随着大型语言模型（LLMs）的发展和个人化模型需求的增加，参数高效微调（PEFT）方法（例如LoRA）将变得至关重要，因为它们能够通过减少计算成本来提高效率。然而，最近的研究提出了令人担忧的问题，即LoRA微调可能会潜在地损害LLMs的安全对齐，给模型的所有者带来重大风险。在本文中，我们首先通过分析微调前后与安全对齐相关的特征变化来研究其背后的工作机制。然后，我们提出了一种由安全数据计算得到的固定安全模块，并为低秩适应中的可训练参数提供了一个特定任务的初始化，将其称为“保持安全对齐的低秩适应”（SaLoRA）。与之前的LoRA方法及其变体不同，SaLoRA能够在不破坏原始对齐的情况下，实现对LLMs的目标修改。我们的实验表明，在各种微调任务的不同评估指标下，SaLoRA优于基于适配器的各种方法。

[No.20] RankSHAP: Shapley Value Based Feature Attributions for Learning to Rank
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: RankSHAP：基于Shapley值的特征归因方法在排序学习中的应用
类别: interpretability and explainable AI
作者: Tanya Chowdhury;Yair Zick;James Allan;
机构: University of Massachusetts Amherst;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/31045
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.01848
英摘: Numerous works propose post-hoc, model-agnostic explanations for learning to rank, focusing on ordering entities by their relevance to a query through feature attribution methods. However, these attributions often weakly correlate or contradict each other, confusing end users. We adopt an axiomatic game-theoretic approach, popular in the feature attribution community, to identify a set of fundamental axioms that every ranking-based feature attribution method should satisfy. We then introduce Rank-SHAP, extending classical Shapley values to ranking. We evaluate the RankSHAP framework through extensive experiments on two datasets, multiple ranking methods and evaluation metrics. Additionally, a user study confirms RankSHAP's alignment with human intuition. We also perform an axiomatic analysis of existing rank attribution algorithms to determine their compliance with our proposed axioms. Ultimately, our aim is to equip practitioners with a set of axiomatically backed feature attribution methods for studying IR ranking models, that ensure generality as well as consistency.
中摘: 众多研究提出了用于学习排序的后验、模型无关的解释方法，这些方法主要通过特征归因方法按查询的相关性对实体进行排序。然而，这些归因往往与彼此弱相关或相互矛盾，使最终用户感到困惑。我们采用了特征归因社区中流行的公理博弈论方法来识别每种基于排名的特征归因方法都应满足的基本公理。随后，我们引入了Rank-SHAP，将其经典Shapley值扩展应用于排名领域。我们通过在两个数据集上进行广泛的实验，以及对多种排名方法和评估指标进行评估，来评价Rank-SHAP框架的效果。此外，一项用户研究表明，Rank-SHAP与人类直觉相一致。我们还对现有的排名归因算法进行了公理分析，以确定它们是否符合我们提出的公理。最终，我们的目标是为研究IR排名模型的实践者提供一套基于公理的特征归因方法，确保这些方法的通用性和一致性。

[No.21] Process Reward Model with Q-value Rankings
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于Q值排名的过程奖励模型
类别: other topics in machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Wendi Li;Yixuan Li;
机构: Huazhong University of Science and Technology;Cornell University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27847
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11287
英摘: Process Reward Modeling (PRM) is critical for complex reasoning and decision-making tasks where the accuracy of intermediate steps significantly influences the overall outcome. Existing PRM approaches, primarily framed as classification problems, employ cross-entropy loss to independently evaluate each step's correctness. This method can lead to suboptimal reward distribution and does not adequately address the interdependencies among steps. To address these limitations, we introduce the Process Q-value Model (PQM), a novel framework that redefines PRM in the context of a Markov Decision Process. PQM optimizes Q-value rankings based on a novel comparative loss function, enhancing the model's ability to capture the intricate dynamics among sequential decisions. This approach provides a more granular and theoretically grounded methodology for process rewards. Our extensive empirical evaluations across various sampling policies, language model backbones, and multi-step reasoning benchmarks show that PQM outperforms classification-based PRMs. The effectiveness of the comparative loss function is highlighted in our comprehensive ablation studies, confirming PQM's practical efficacy and theoretical advantage.
中摘: 过程奖励建模（PRM）对于复杂的推理和决策任务至关重要，因为中间步骤的准确性显著影响整体结果。现有的PRM方法主要以分类问题的形式呈现，使用交叉熵损失独立评估每个步骤的正确性。这种方法可能导致奖励分布不佳，并且不能充分解决各步骤之间的相互依赖关系。为了解决这些局限性，我们引入了过程Q值模型（PQM），这是一种新的框架，将PRM重新定义在马尔可夫决策过程的背景下。PQM基于一种新颖的比较损失函数优化Q值排名，增强了模型捕捉顺序决策之间复杂动态的能力。这种方法提供了一种更细致且理论依据更强的过程奖励计算方法。我们在各种采样策略、语言模型基础架构以及多步推理基准上的广泛实证评估表明，PQM优于基于分类的PRM。我们的全面消融研究突显了比较损失函数的有效性，证实了PQM的实际有效性和理论优势。

[No.22] LoR-VP: Low-Rank Visual Prompting for Efficient Vision Model Adaptation
来源: ICLR 2025

英摘: Visual prompting has gained popularity as a method for adapting pre-trained models to specific tasks, particularly in the realm of parameter-efficient tuning. 来源: ICLR 2025

英摘: The recent paradigm shift to large-scale foundation models has brought about a new era for deep learning that, while has found great success in practice, has also been plagued by prohibitively expensive costs in terms of high memory consumption and compute. To mitigate these issues, there has been a concerted effort in post-hoc neural network pruning techniques that do not require costly retraining. Despite the considerable progress being made, existing methods often exhibit a steady drop in model performance as the compression increases. [No.26] Ranking-aware adapter for text-driven image ordering with CLIP

[No.27] Lambda-Skip Connections: the architectural component that prevents Rank Collapse
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: Lambda-Skip 连接：防止秩塌陷的架构组件
类别: learning theory
作者: Federico Arangath Joseph;Jerome Sieber;Melanie Zeilinger;Carmen Amo Alonso;
机构: ETH Zurich;Stanford University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/31171
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10609
英摘: Rank collapse, a phenomenon where embedding vectors in sequence models rapidly converge to a uniform token or equilibrium state, has recently gained attention in the deep learning literature. This phenomenon leads to reduced expressivity and potential training instabilities due to vanishing gradients. Empirical evidence suggests that architectural components like skip connections, LayerNorm, and MultiLayer Perceptrons (MLPs) play critical roles in mitigating rank collapse. While this issue is well-documented for transformers, alternative sequence models, such as State Space Models (SSMs), which have recently gained prominence, have not been thoroughly examined for similar vulnerabilities. This paper extends the theory of rank collapse from transformers to SSMs using a unifying framework that captures both architectures. We study how a parametrized version of the classic skip connection component, which we call \emph{lambda-skip connections}, provides guarantees for rank collapse prevention. Through analytical results, we present a sufficient condition to guarantee prevention of rank collapse across all the aforementioned architectures. We also study the necessity of this condition via ablation studies and analytical examples. To our knowledge, this is the first study that provides a general guarantee to prevent rank collapse, and that investigates rank collapse in the context of SSMs, offering valuable understanding for both theoreticians and practitioners. Finally, we validate our findings with experiments demonstrating the crucial role of architectural components such as skip connections and gating mechanisms in preventing rank collapse.
中摘: 嵌入向量在序列模型中迅速收敛到统一标记或平衡状态的现象被称为排名坍缩，这一现象最近在深度学习文献中引起了广泛关注。排名坍缩会导致表达能力降低，并可能由于梯度消失而引发训练不稳定。实证证据表明，跳跃连接、LayerNorm 和多层感知机（MLPs）等架构组件在缓解排名坍缩方面发挥着关键作用。尽管这一问题在变压器中得到了充分记录，但其他替代序列模型，如近期备受关注的状态空间模型（SSMs），尚未被彻底研究以发现类似的脆弱性。本文利用一个统一框架将排名坍缩的理论从变压器扩展到SSMs，研究了参数化的经典跳跃连接组件——称为\emph{lambda-跳跃连接}——如何提供防止排名坍缩的保证。通过分析结果，我们提出了一个充分条件，以确保上述所有架构中排名坍缩的预防。我们还通过消融研究和分析示例研究了该条件的必要性。据我们所知，这是首次提供了一般性的保证来预防排名坍缩，并且探讨了排名坍缩在状态空间模型中的情况，为理论研究者和实践者提供了宝贵的见解。最后，我们通过实验验证了这些发现，证明了跳跃连接和门控机制等架构组件在预防排名坍缩中的关键作用。

[No.28] SaRA: High-Efficient Diffusion Model Fine-tuning with Progressive Sparse Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: SaRA: 高效扩散模型渐进稀疏低秩适应微调
类别: generative models
作者: Teng Hu;Jiangning Zhang;Ran Yi;Hongrui Huang;Yabiao Wang;Lizhuang Ma;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;Tencent;Harbin Institute of Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27860
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.06633
英摘: In recent years, the development of diffusion models has led to significant progress in image and video generation tasks, with pre-trained models like the Stable Diffusion series playing a crucial role. Inspired by model pruning which lightens large pre-trained models by removing unimportant parameters, we propose a novel model fine-tuning method to make full use of these ineffective parameters and enable the pre-trained model with new task-specified capabilities. In this work, we first investigate the importance of parameters in pre-trained diffusion models, and discover that the smallest 10% to 20% of parameters by absolute values do not contribute to the generation process. Based on this observation, we propose a method termed SaRA that re-utilizes these temporarily ineffective parameters, equating to optimizing a sparse weight matrix to learn the task-specific knowledge. To mitigate overfitting, we propose a nuclear-norm-based low-rank sparse training scheme for efficient fine-tuning. Furthermore, we design a new progressive parameter adjustment strategy to make full use of the re-trained/finetuned parameters. Finally, we propose a novel unstructural backpropagation strategy, which significantly reduces memory costs during fine-tuning. Our method enhances the generative capabilities of pre-trained models in downstream applications and outperforms traditional fine-tuning methods like LoRA in maintaining model's generalization ability. We validate our approach through fine-tuning experiments on SD models, demonstrating significant improvements. SaRA also offers a practical advantage that requires only a single line of code modification for efficient implementation and is seamlessly compatible with existing methods.
中摘: 近年来，扩散模型的发展在图像和视频生成任务上取得了显著进展，预训练模型如Stable Diffusion系列发挥了关键作用。受模型剪枝的启发，该方法通过移除不重要参数来减轻大型预训练模型的负担，我们提出了一种新的模型微调方法，以充分利用这些无效参数，并赋予预训练模型新的任务特定能力。在这项工作中，我们首先研究了预训练扩散模型中参数的重要性，并发现绝对值最小的10%到20%的参数对生成过程没有贡献。基于这一观察，我们提出了一种名为SaRA的方法，重新利用这些暂时无效的参数，相当于优化一个稀疏权重矩阵以学习任务特定的知识。为了缓解过拟合问题，我们提出了一种基于核范数的低秩稀疏训练方案，以实现高效的微调。此外，我们设计了一种新的逐步参数调整策略，以充分利用重新训练/微调后的参数。最后，我们提出了一种新颖的无结构反向传播策略，这在微调过程中显著降低了内存成本。我们的方法增强了预训练模型在下游应用中的生成能力，并且在保持模型泛化能力方面优于传统的微调方法如LoRA。我们通过在SD模型上的微调实验验证了这种方法，显示出显著的改进效果。SaRA还提供了一个实用的优势，只需一行代码修改即可实现高效实施，并且与现有方法无缝兼容。

[No.29] RandLoRA: Full rank parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: RandLoRA: 高秩参数高效微调大模型
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Paul Albert;Frederic Z. Zhang;Hemanth Saratchandran;Cristian Rodriguez-Opazo;Anton van den Hengel;Ehsan Abbasnejad;
机构: University of Adelaide;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30212
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00987
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and its variants have shown impressive results in reducing the number of trainable parameters and memory requirements of large transformer networks while maintaining fine-tuning performance. The low-rank nature of the weight update inherently limits the representation power of fine-tuned models, however, thus potentially compromising performance on complex tasks. This raises a critical question: when a performance gap between LoRA and standard fine-tuning is observed, is it due to the reduced number of trainable parameters or the rank deficiency? This paper aims to answer this question by introducing RandLoRA, a parameter-efficient method that performs full-rank updates using a learned linear combinations of low-rank, non-trainable random matrices. Our method limits the number of trainable parameters by restricting optimization to diagonal scaling matrices applied to the fixed random matrices. This allows us to effectively overcome the low-rank limitations while maintaining parameter and memory efficiency during training. Through extensive experimentation across vision, language, and vision-language benchmarks, we systematically evaluate the limitations of LoRA and existing random basis methods. Our findings reveal that full-rank updates are beneficial across vision and language tasks individually, and even more so for vision-language tasks, where RandLoRA significantly reduces -- and sometimes eliminates -- the performance gap between standard fine-tuning and LoRA, demonstrating its efficacy.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）及其变体在减少大型变压器网络中的可训练参数数量和内存需求的同时，保持了微调性能。然而，权重更新的低秩性质本身固有限制了微调模型的表现能力，从而可能在复杂任务上影响性能。这引发了一个关键问题：当观察到LoRA与标准微调之间的性能差距时，这种差距是由于可训练参数数量减少还是低秩不足造成的？本文旨在通过引入RandLoRA方法来回答这个问题，RandLoRA是一种参数高效的方法，使用学习到的线性组合非可训练随机矩阵进行全秩更新。我们的方法通过限制优化仅应用于固定随机矩阵上的对角缩放矩阵，来限制可训练参数的数量。这使得我们能够有效地克服低秩限制，同时在训练过程中保持参数和内存效率。通过对视觉、语言和跨模态基准的广泛实验，我们系统地评估了LoRA及其现有随机基底方法的局限性。研究发现，在单独的语言和视觉任务中，全秩更新是有益的，而在跨模态任务中，RandLoRA显著减少了——有时甚至消除了——标准微调与LoRA之间的性能差距，证明了其有效性。

[No.31] Selective Aggregation for Low-Rank Adaptation in Federated Learning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 联邦学习中选择性聚合的低秩适应机制
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Pengxin Guo;Shuang Zeng;Yanran Wang;Huijie Fan;Feifei Wang;Liangqiong Qu;
机构: University of Hong Kong;;Shenyang Institute of Automation;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28693
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01463
英摘: We investigate LoRA in federated learning through the lens of the asymmetry analysis of the learned $A$ and $B$ matrices. In doing so, we uncover that $A$ matrices are responsible for learning general knowledge, while $B$ matrices focus on capturing client-specific knowledge. Based on this finding, we introduce Federated Share-A Low-Rank Adaptation (FedSA-LoRA), which employs two low-rank trainable matrices $A$ and $B$ to model the weight update, but only $A$ matrices are shared with the server for aggregation. Moreover, we delve into the relationship between the learned $A$ and $B$ matrices in other LoRA variants, such as rsLoRA and VeRA, revealing a consistent pattern. Consequently, we extend our FedSA-LoRA method to these LoRA variants, resulting in FedSA-rsLoRA and FedSA-VeRA. In this way, we establish a general paradigm for integrating LoRA with FL, offering guidance for future work on subsequent LoRA variants combined with FL. Extensive experimental results on natural language understanding and generation tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. Our code is available at https://github.com/Pengxin-Guo/FedSA-LoRA.
中摘: 我们通过不对称分析的角度研究了联邦学习中的LoRA，发现学到的$A$矩阵负责学习通用知识，而$B$矩阵则专注于捕捉客户端特定的知识。基于这一发现，我们引入了Federated Share-A Low-Rank Adaptation（FedSA-LoRA）方法，该方法使用两个低秩可训练矩阵$A$和$B$来建模权重更新，但只有$A$矩阵被客户端共享给服务器进行聚合。此外，我们探讨了其他LoRA变体（如rsLoRA和VeRA）中学习到的$A$和$B$矩阵之间的关系，揭示了一致的模式。因此，我们将FedSA-LoRA方法扩展到这些LoRA变体中，形成了FedSA-rsLoRA和FedSA-VeRA。这样，我们建立了一个将LoRA与联邦学习（FL）结合的一般范式，为后续将LoRA与其他FL变体相结合的工作提供了指导。在自然语言理解与生成任务上的大量实验结果证明了所提出方法的有效性。我们的代码可在https://github.com/Pengxin-Guo/FedSA-LoRA获取。

[No.32] Adaptive Rank Allocation: Speeding Up Modern Transformers with RaNA Adapters
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 自适应秩分配：通过RaNA适配器加速现代变压器模型
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Roberto Garcia;Jerry Weihong Liu;Daniel Sorvisto;Sabri Eyuboglu;
机构: Stanford University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27996
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18216
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) are computationally intensive, particularly during inference. Neuron-adaptive techniques, which selectively activate neurons in Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) layers, offer some speedups but suffer from limitations in modern Transformers. These include reliance on sparse activations, incompatibility with attention layers, and the use of costly neuron masking techniques. To address these issues, we propose the Adaptive Rank Allocation framework and introduce the Rank and Neuron Allocator (RaNA) adapter. RaNA adapters leverage rank adapters, which operate on linear layers by applying both low-rank matrix decompositions and adaptive masking to efficiently allocate compute without depending on activation sparsity. This enables RaNA to be generally applied to MLPs and linear components of attention modules, while eliminating the need for expensive maskers found in neuron-adaptive methods. Notably, when compared to neuron adapters, RaNA improves perplexity by up to 7 points and increases accuracy by up to 8 percentage-points when reducing FLOPs by $\sim$44% in state-of-the-art Transformer architectures. These results position RaNA as a robust solution for improving inference efficiency in modern Transformer architectures.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在推理过程中计算量很大。神经元自适应技术可以在多层感知器（MLP）层中选择性地激活神经元，从而提供一些加速效果，但在现代Transformer中却存在一些局限性。这些局限性包括依赖稀疏激活、与注意力层不兼容以及使用昂贵的神经元遮罩技术。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了自适应秩分配框架，并引入了秩和神经元分配器（RaNA）适配器。RaNA适配器利用秩适配器，在线性层上通过应用低秩矩阵分解和自适应遮罩来高效地分配计算资源，而不依赖于激活的稀疏性。这使得RaNA可以普遍应用于MLP和注意力模块的线性组件，同时消除了神经元自适应方法中昂贵遮罩器的需求。值得注意的是，当与最先进的Transformer架构相比时，与神经元适配器相比，RaNA在减少FLOPs约44%的情况下，改善困惑度最多可达7点，并提高准确性最多可达8个百分点。这些结果将RaNA定位为改进现代Transformer架构推理效率的稳健解决方案。

[No.33] Merging LoRAs like Playing LEGO: Pushing the Modularity of LoRA to Extremes Through Rank-Wise Clustering
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 像玩乐高一样合并LoRA：通过按秩聚类来极致推进LoRA的模块化
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Ziyu Zhao;Tao Shen;Didi Zhu;Zexi Li;Jing Su;Xuwu Wang;Fei Wu;
机构: Zhejiang University;;ByteDance;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28655
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16167
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a popular technique for fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) to various domains due to its modular design and widespread availability on platforms like Huggingface. This modularity has sparked interest in combining multiple LoRAs to enhance LLM capabilities. However, existing methods for LoRA composition primarily focus on task-specific adaptations that require additional training, and current model merging techniques often fail to fully leverage LoRA's modular nature, leading to parameter interference and performance degradation. In this paper, we investigate the feasibility of disassembling and reassembling multiple LoRAs at a finer granularity, analogous to assembling LEGO blocks. We introduce the concept of Minimal Semantic Units (MSUs), where the parameters corresponding to each rank in LoRA function as independent units. These MSUs demonstrate permutation invariance and concatenation-summation equivalence properties, enabling flexible combinations to create new LoRAs. Building on these insights, we propose the LoRA-LEGO framework. This framework conducts rank-wise parameter clustering by grouping MSUs from different LoRAs into $k$ clusters. The centroid of each cluster serves as a representative MSU, enabling the assembly of a merged LoRA with an adjusted rank of $k$. Additionally, we apply a dual reweighting strategy to optimize the scale of the merged LoRA. Experiments across various benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in LoRA merging.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）已经成为一种流行的大型语言模型（LLM）微调技术，尤其是在Huggingface等平台上广泛可用的情况下。这种模块化设计激发了将多个LoRA组合起来以增强LLM能力的兴趣。然而，现有的LoRA组合方法主要集中在需要额外训练的任务特定适应上，而当前的模型合并技术往往未能充分利用LoRA的模块化特性，导致参数干扰和性能下降。在本文中，我们研究了在更细粒度级别拆解和重组多个LoRA的可能性，类似于拼装乐高积木。我们提出了最小语义单元（MSUs）的概念，其中LoRA中的每个秩对应的参数作为独立的单元发挥功能。这些MSUs表现出置换不变性和串联求和等价性性质，能够灵活组合以创建新的LoRA。基于这些洞察，我们提出了LoRA-LEGO框架。该框架通过将来自不同LoRA的MSU按秩分组到$k$个簇中来进行参数聚类。每个簇的中心点作为代表性的MSU，使我们可以组装一个调整秩为$k$的合并LoRA。此外，我们还应用了一种双重权重优化策略来调整合并LoRA的比例。我们在各种基准测试中的实验表明，我们的方法在LoRA合并方面优于现有方法。

[No.35] A Differentiable Rank-Based Objective for Better Feature Learning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 一种可微分的基于秩的目标函数，以提高更好的特征学习
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Krunoslav Lehman Pavasovic;Giulio Biroli;Levent Sagun;
机构: ;Ecole Normale Superieure;Meta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30039
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09445
英摘: In this paper, we leverage existing statistical methods to better understand feature learning from data. We tackle this by modifying the model-free variable selection method, Feature Ordering by Conditional Independence (FOCI), which is introduced in \cite{azadkia2021simple}. While FOCI is based on a non-parametric coefficient of conditional dependence, we introduce its parametric, differentiable approximation. With this approximate coefficient of correlation, we present a new algorithm called difFOCI, which is applicable to a wider range of machine learning problems thanks to its differentiable nature and learnable parameters. We present difFOCI in three contexts: (1) as a variable selection method with baseline comparisons to FOCI, (2) as a trainable model parametrized with a neural network, and (3) as a generic, widely applicable neural network regularizer, one that improves feature learning with better management of spurious correlations. We evaluate difFOCI on increasingly complex problems ranging from basic variable selection in toy examples to saliency map comparisons in convolutional networks. We then show how difFOCI can be incorporated in the context of fairness to facilitate classifications without relying on sensitive data.
中摘: 在本文中，我们利用现有的统计方法来更好地理解从数据中进行特征学习。我们通过修改无模型变量选择方法Feature Ordering by Conditional Independence（FOCI），来解决这一问题，FOCI方法在文献\cite{azadkia2021simple}中被介绍。虽然FOCI基于非参数条件依赖系数，但我们引入了其可训练的、可微分的近似版本。借助这一可微分的相关性系数，我们提出了一种新的算法difFOCI，由于其可微分的性质和可学习的参数，该算法适用于更广泛的机器学习问题。我们从三个角度介绍了difFOCI：（1）作为一种与FOCI基线比较的变量选择方法；（2）作为一种使用神经网络参数化的可训练模型；（3）作为一种通用且广泛适用的神经网络正则化器，它可以更好地管理虚假相关性从而提高特征学习的效果。我们在从基本变量选择的玩具示例到卷积网络中的重要性图比较等日益复杂的问题上评估了difFOCI的表现。最后，我们展示了如何将difFOCI融入公平性分类的背景下，以在不依赖敏感数据的情况下实现分类。

[No.36] Ensembles of Low-Rank Expert Adapters
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩专家适配器集合
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Yinghao Li;Vianne R. Gao;Chao Zhang;MohamadAli Torkamani;
机构: Georgia Institute of Technology;Amazon;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28544
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00089
英摘: The training and fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) often involve diverse textual data from multiple sources, which poses challenges due to conflicting gradient directions, hindering optimization and specialization. These challenges can undermine model generalization across tasks, resulting in reduced downstream performance. Recent research suggests that fine-tuning LLMs on carefully selected, task-specific subsets of data can match or even surpass the performance of using the entire dataset. Building on these insights, we propose the Ensembles of Low-Rank Expert Adapters (ELREA) framework to improve the model's capability to handle diverse tasks. ELREA clusters the training instructions based on their gradient directions, representing different areas of expertise and thereby reducing conflicts during optimization. Expert adapters are then trained on these clusters, utilizing the low-rank adaptation (LoRA) technique to ensure training efficiency and model scalability. During inference, ELREA combines predictions from the most relevant expert adapters based on the input data's gradient similarity to the training clusters, ensuring optimal adapter selection for each task. Experiments show that our method outperforms baseline LoRA adapters trained on the full dataset and other ensemble approaches with similar training and inference complexity across a range of domain-specific tasks.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）的训练和微调通常涉及来自多个来源的多样化文本数据，这会由于相互冲突的梯度方向而带来挑战，从而妨碍优化和专业化。这些挑战可能会削弱模型在不同任务上的泛化能力，导致下游性能下降。近期的研究表明，在精心选择的任务特定子集上微调LLMs可以达到甚至超过使用整个数据集的效果。基于这些见解，我们提出了一种低秩专家适配器集合（ELREA）框架，以提高模型处理多样化任务的能力。ELREA根据梯度方向对训练指令进行聚类，代表不同的专业领域，从而在优化过程中减少冲突。然后，利用低秩适应（LoRA）技术训练专家适配器，确保训练效率和模型可扩展性。在推理阶段，ELREA根据输入数据与训练集群的梯度相似性结合最相关的专家适配器的预测，确保为每个任务选择最佳适配器。实验结果显示，我们的方法在多种特定领域任务上优于在完整数据集上训练的标准LoRA适配器，并且与其他具有相似训练和推理复杂度的集成方法相比也表现出更好的性能。

[No.39] Efficient Alternating Minimization with Applications to Weighted Low Rank Approximation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 高效的交替最小化方法及其在加权低秩逼近中的应用
类别: optimization
作者: Zhao Song;Mingquan Ye;Junze Yin;Lichen Zhang;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;;Rice University;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28163
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.04169
英摘: Weighted low rank approximation is a fundamental problem in numerical linear algebra, and it has many applications in machine learning. Given a matrix $M \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times n}$, a non-negative weight matrix $W \in \mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}^{n \times n}$, a parameter $k$, the goal is to output two matrices $X,Y\in \mathbb{R}^{n \times k}$ such that $\| W \circ (M - X Y^\top) \|_F$ is minimized, where $\circ$ denotes the Hadamard product. It naturally generalizes the well-studied low rank matrix completion problem. Such a problem is known to be NP-hard and even hard to approximate assuming the Exponential Time Hypothesis [GG11, RSW16]. Meanwhile, alternating minimization is a good heuristic solution for weighted low rank approximation. In particular, [LLR16] shows that, under mild assumptions, alternating minimization does provide provable guarantees. In this work, we develop an efficient and robust framework for alternating minimization that allows the alternating updates to be computed approximately. For weighted low rank approximation, this improves the runtime of [LLR16] from $\|W\|_0k^2$ to $\|W\|_0 k$ where $\|W\|_0$ denotes the number of nonzero entries of the weight matrix. At the heart of our framework is a high-accuracy multiple response regression solver together with a robust analysis of alternating minimization.
中摘: 加权低秩逼近是数值线性代数中的一个基本问题，并且在机器学习中有许多应用。给定一个矩阵 \(M \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times n}\)，一个非负权重矩阵 \(W \in \mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}^{n \times n}\)，以及一个参数 \(k\)，目标是输出两个矩阵 \(X, Y \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times k}\)，使得 \( \| W \circ (M - X Y^\top) \|_F \) 最小化，其中 \(\circ\) 表示哈达玛积。这个问题自然地推广了已研究得很好的低秩矩阵补全问题。已知该问题在最坏情况下是 NP 难的，并且即使假设指数时间假设，也难以近似解决 [GG11, RSW16]。同时，交替最小化是加权低秩逼近的一个良好的启发式解决方案。特别是，[LLR16] 显示，在温和假设下，交替最小化确实提供了可证明的保证。在这项工作中，我们开发了一个高效的稳健框架，允许交替更新被近似计算。对于加权低秩逼近，这将 [LLR16] 的运行时间从 \(\|W\|_0 k^2\) 改进到 \(\|W\|_0 k\)，其中 \(\|W\|_0\) 表示权重矩阵的非零元素数量。我们框架的核心是一个高精度的多重响应回归求解器，以及交替最小化的稳健分析。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.40] Computational Limits of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) Fine-Tuning for Transformer Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩适应（LoRA）微调对变换器模型的计算限制
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Jerry Yao-Chieh Hu;Maojiang Su;En-jui kuo;Zhao Song;Han Liu;
机构: ;National Yang Ming Chiao Tung University;University of California, Berkeley;Northwestern University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29984
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03136
英摘: We study the computational limits of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for finetuning transformer-based models using fine-grained complexity theory. Our key observation is that the existence of low-rank decompositions within the gradient computation of LoRA adaptation leads to possible algorithmic speedup. This allows us to (i) identify a phase transition behavior of efficiency assuming the Strong Exponential Time Hypothesis (SETH), and (ii) prove the existence of almost linear algorithms by controlling the LoRA update computation term by term. For the former, we identify a sharp transition in the efficiency of all possible rank-$r$ LoRA update algorithms for transformers, based on specific norms resulting from the multiplications of the input sequence $X$, pretrained weights ${W^\star}$, and adapter matrices $αB A/r$. Specifically, we derive a shared upper bound threshold for such norms, and show that efficient (sub-quadratic) approximation algorithms of LoRA exist only below this threshold. For the latter, we prove the existence of almost linear approximation algorithms for LoRA adaptation by utilizing the hierarchical low-rank structures of LoRA gradients and approximating the gradients with a series of chained low-rank approximations. To showcase our theory, we consider two practical scenarios: partial (e.g., only $W_V$ and $W_Q$) and full adaptations (e.g., $W_Q$, $W_V$, and $W_K$) of weights in attention heads.
中摘: 我们使用细粒度复杂性理论研究了低秩适应（LoRA）在微调基于变换器模型时的计算限制。我们的主要观察是，在LoRA适应过程中的梯度计算中存在低秩分解，这可能导致算法加速。这使我们能够（i）假设强指数时间假设（SETH），识别所有可能的秩-$r$ LoRA更新算法效率的相变行为；（ii）通过逐项控制LoRA更新计算来证明几乎线性算法的存在。对于前者，我们根据输入序列$X$、预训练权重${W^\star}$和适配矩阵$αB A/r$的乘积所得到的具体范数，识别出了所有可能的秩-$r$ LoRA更新算法效率的尖锐转变，并推导出这些范数的一个共享上限阈值，表明只有在该阈值以下，高效的（次平方阶）LoRA近似算法才可能存在。对于后者，我们通过利用LoRA梯度的分层低秩结构，并用一系列链式低秩近似来逼近梯度，证明了LoRA适应几乎线性近似算法的存在。为了展示我们的理论，我们考虑了两种实际场景：部分适应（例如，仅$W_V$和$W_Q$）和完全适应（例如，$W_Q$、$W_V$和$W_K$）注意力头中的权重。

[No.42] SEBRA : Debiasing through Self-Guided Bias Ranking
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: SEBRA : 自我引导偏见排序去偏化
类别: alignment, fairness, safety, privacy, and societal considerations
作者: Adarsh Kappiyath;Abhra Chaudhuri;AJAY KUMAR JAISWAL;Ziquan Liu;Yunpeng Li;Xiatian Zhu;Lu Yin;
机构: ;Fujitsu;University of Texas at Austin;Queen Mary, University of London;Beijing University of Posts and Telecommunications;University of Surrey;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29907
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18277
英摘: Ranking samples by fine-grained estimates of spuriosity (the degree to which spurious cues are present) has recently been shown to significantly benefit bias mitigation, over the traditional binary biased-\textit{vs}-unbiased partitioning of train sets. However, this spuriosity ranking comes with the requirement of human supervision. In this paper, we propose a debiasing framework based on our novel \ul{Se}lf-Guided \ul{B}ias \ul{Ra}nking (\emph{Sebra}), that mitigates biases (spurious correlations) via an automatic ranking of data points by spuriosity within their respective classes. Sebra leverages a key local symmetry in Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) training -- the ease of learning a sample via ERM inversely correlates with its spuriousity; the fewer spurious correlations a sample exhibits, the harder it is to learn, and vice versa. However, globally across iterations, ERM tends to deviate from this symmetry. Sebra dynamically steers ERM to correct this deviation, facilitating the sequential learning of attributes in increasing order of difficulty, \ie, decreasing order of spuriosity. As a result, the sequence in which Sebra learns samples naturally provides spuriousity rankings. We use the resulting fine-grained bias characterization in a contrastive learning framework to mitigate biases from multiple sources. Extensive experiments show that Sebra consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art unsupervised debiasing techniques across multiple standard benchmarks, including UrbanCars, BAR, CelebA, and ImageNet-1K. Code, pre-trained models, and training logs are available at https://kadarsh22.github.io/sebra_iclr25/.
中摘: 通过精细估计伪相关程度（伪线索存在的程度）对样本进行排名已被证明能显著提高偏见缓解的效果，优于传统的二元有偏-无偏训练集划分方法。然而，这种伪相关性排名需要人工监督。在本文中，我们提出了一种基于我们新颖的“自引导伪相关性排名”（Sebra）的去偏框架，该框架通过自动按伪相关性对数据点在其各自类别内的排名来缓解偏见（伪相关）。Sebra 利用了经验风险最小化（ERM）训练中的关键局部对称性——通过 ERM 学习一个样本的容易程度与其伪相关性成反比；样本展示的伪相关性越少，学习就越困难，反之亦然。然而，在迭代过程中，ERM 趋势上会偏离这种对称性。Sebra 动态引导 ERM 修正这种偏差，促进按从易到难的顺序逐步学习属性，即按从低到高的伪相关性顺序学习。因此，Sebra 学习样本的顺序自然提供了伪相关性排名。我们在对比学习框架中使用由此产生的精细粒度的偏见表征来从多个来源缓解偏见。广泛的实验表明，Sebra 在多个标准基准测试中（包括UrbanCars、BAR、CelebA和ImageNet-1K）始终优于之前的无监督去偏技术。相关代码、预训练模型和训练日志可在https://kadarsh22.github.io/sebra_iclr25/获取。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.43] A Statistical Framework for Ranking LLM-based Chatbots
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于统计框架的大型语言模型聊天机器人排名方法
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Siavash Ameli;Siyuan Zhuang;Ion Stoica;Michael W. Mahoney;
机构: International Computer Science Institute;;University of California, Berkeley;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18407
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) have transformed natural language processing, with frameworks like Chatbot Arena providing pioneering platforms for evaluating these models. By facilitating millions of pairwise comparisons based on human judgments, Chatbot Arena has become a cornerstone in LLM evaluation, offering rich datasets for ranking models in open-ended conversational tasks. Building upon this foundation, we propose a statistical framework that incorporates key advancements to address specific challenges in pairwise comparison analysis. First, we introduce a factored tie model that enhances the ability to handle ties -- an integral aspect of human-judged comparisons -- significantly improving the model's fit to observed data. Second, we extend the framework to model covariance between competitors, enabling deeper insights into performance relationships and facilitating intuitive groupings into performance tiers. Third, we resolve optimization challenges arising from parameter non-uniqueness by introducing novel constraints, ensuring stable and interpretable parameter estimation. Through rigorous evaluation and extensive experimentation, our framework demonstrates substantial improvements over existing methods in modeling pairwise comparison data. To support reproducibility and practical adoption, we release leaderbot, an open-source Python package implementing our models and analyses.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）已经改变了自然语言处理领域，而像Chatbot Arena这样的框架提供了开创性的平台来评估这些模型。通过促进基于人类判断的数百万对之间的比较，Chatbot Arena已成为LLM评估的重要基石，为开放性对话任务中的模型排名提供了丰富的数据集。在此基础上，我们提出了一种统计框架，该框架整合了关键进展以解决对等比较分析中的特定挑战。首先，我们引入了一种因子化的平局模型，增强了处理平局的能力——这是人类评判比较中的一个关键方面，显著提高了模型与观察数据的契合度。其次，我们将框架扩展到建模竞争对手之间的协方差，从而深入探讨性能关系，并促进直观的性能等级分组。第三，我们通过引入新的约束条件解决了由于参数非唯一性引起的优化问题，确保了稳定的和可解释的参数估计。通过严格的评估和广泛的实验，我们的框架在建模对等比较数据方面显示出比现有方法显著的改进。为了支持可重复性和实际应用，我们发布了leaderbot，这是一个开源Python包，实现了我们的模型和分析。

[No.44] SLoPe: Double-Pruned Sparse Plus Lazy Low-Rank Adapter Pretraining of LLMs
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: SLoPe：双剪枝稀疏增强和懒惰低秩适配器预训练的大语言模型
类别: other topics in machine learning (i.e., none of the above)
作者: Mohammad Mozaffari;Amir Yazdanbakhsh;Zhao Zhang;Maryam Mehri Dehnavi;
机构: University of Toronto;Google;Rutgers University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28497
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16325
英摘: We propose SLoPe, a Double-Pruned Sparse Plus Lazy Low-rank Adapter Pretraining method for LLMs that improves the accuracy of sparse LLMs while accelerating their pretraining and inference and reducing their memory footprint. Sparse pretraining of LLMs reduces the accuracy of the model, to overcome this, prior work uses dense models during fine-tuning. SLoPe improves the accuracy of sparsely pretrained models by adding low-rank adapters in the final 1% iterations of pretraining without adding significant overheads to the model pretraining and inference. In addition, SLoPe uses a double-pruned backward pass formulation that prunes the transposed weight matrix using N:M sparsity structures to enable an accelerated sparse backward pass. SLoPe accelerates the training and inference of models with billions of parameters up to $1.25\times$ and $1.54\times$ respectively (OPT-33B and OPT-66B) while reducing their memory usage by up to $0.63\times$ and $0.61\times$ for training and inference respectively.
中摘: 我们提出了一种名为SLoPe的双剪枝稀疏加懒惰低秩适配器预训练方法，该方法在加速LLM（大型语言模型）的预训练和推理、减少其内存占用的同时，提高了稀疏LLM的准确性。稀疏预训练会降低模型的准确性，为了克服这一问题，先前的工作在微调阶段使用密集模型。SLoPe通过在预训练的最后一千分之一迭代中添加低秩适配器来提高稀疏预训练模型的准确性，而不会显著增加模型预训练和推理的成本。此外，SLoPe采用了一种双剪枝反向传播公式，利用N:M稀疏结构剪枝转置权重矩阵，从而实现加速的稀疏反向传播。SLoPe能够将具有数十亿参数的模型的训练和推理速度分别加速至1.25倍和1.54倍（如OPT-33B和OPT-66B），同时将训练和推理的内存使用量分别减少至0.63倍和0.61倍。

[No.45] Pareto Low-Rank Adapters: Efficient Multi-Task Learning with Preferences
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 帕累托低秩适配器：具有偏好性的高效多任务学习
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Nikolaos Dimitriadis;Pascal Frossard;François Fleuret;
机构: EPFL;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28684
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08056
英摘: Multi-task trade-offs in machine learning can be addressed via Pareto Front Learning (PFL) methods that parameterize the Pareto Front (PF) with a single model. PFL permits to select the desired operational point during inference, contrary to traditional Multi-Task Learning (MTL) that optimizes for a single trade-off decided prior to training. However, recent PFL methodologies suffer from limited scalability, slow convergence, and excessive memory requirements, while exhibiting inconsistent mappings from preference to objective space. We introduce PaLoRA, a novel parameter-efficient method that addresses these limitations in two ways. First, we augment any neural network architecture with task-specific low-rank adapters and continuously parameterize the PF in their convex hull. Our approach steers the original model and the adapters towards learning general and task-specific features, respectively. Second, we propose a deterministic sampling schedule of preference vectors that reinforces this division of labor, enabling faster convergence and strengthening the validity of the mapping from preference to objective space throughout training. Our experiments show that PaLoRA outperforms state-of-the-art MTL and PFL baselines across various datasets, scales to large networks, reducing the memory overhead $23.8-31.7$ times compared with competing PFL baselines in scene understanding benchmarks.
中摘: 多任务学习中的权衡可以在单个模型参数化帕累托前沿（PFL）的方法中得到解决。PFL方法允许在推理过程中选择所需的运行点，而传统的多任务学习（MTL）则在训练前决定单一的权衡并对其进行优化。然而，最近的PFL方法存在可扩展性有限、收敛速度慢和内存需求过高的问题，并且从偏好空间到目标空间的映射不一致。我们引入了PaLoRA，这是一种新颖的参数高效方法，通过两种方式解决了这些限制。首先，我们将任一神经网络架构与特定任务的低秩适配器相结合，并在其凸包内连续参数化帕累托前沿。我们的方法使原始模型和适配器分别学习通用特征和特定任务特征。其次，我们提出了一种确定性的采样计划，以偏好向量的形式强化这种劳动分工，从而加快收敛速度并在整个训练过程中增强偏好空间到目标空间映射的有效性。我们的实验表明，在各种数据集上，PaLoRA 在场景理解基准测试中比竞争的PFL基线方法在大规模网络上表现更优，将内存开销减少了23.8至31.7倍。

[No.46] R-Sparse: Rank-Aware Activation Sparsity for Efficient LLM Inference
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: R-稀疏：基于秩的激活稀疏性以实现高效的LLM推理
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Zhenyu Zhang;Zechun Liu;Yuandong Tian;Harshit Khaitan;Zhangyang Wang;Steven Li;
机构: University of Texas at Austin;;Meta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30686
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19449
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs), while demonstrating remarkable capabilities across various applications, present significant challenges during inference due to their substantial model size, especially when deployed on edge devices. Activation sparsity offers a promising solution to reduce computation and memory movement, enabling more efficient inference, particularly for small-batch on-device applications. However, current approaches face limitations with non-ReLU activation function, which are foundational to most advanced LLMs, or require heavy continual training. Additionally, the difficulty in predicting active channels and limited achievable sparsity ratios constrain the effectiveness of activation sparsity-based methods. In this paper, we introduce R-Sparse, a training-free activation sparsity approach capable of achieving high sparsity levels in advanced LLMs. We conducted two preliminary investigations into how different components contribute to the output within a single linear layer and found two key observations: (i) the non-sparse components of the input function can be regarded as a few bias terms, and (ii) The full computation can be effectively approximated by an appropriate combination of input channels and weight singular values. Building on this, we replace the linear layers in LLMs with a rank-aware sparse inference method that leverages the sparsity of input channels and singular value components, eliminating the need for active channel prediction like the output sparsity based approaches. Experiments on Llama-2/3 and Mistral models across ten diverse tasks demonstrate that R-Sparse achieves comparable performance at 50% model-level sparsity, resulting in a significant 43% end-to-end efficient improvements with customized kernels.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在各种应用中展示了显著的能力，但在部署于边缘设备时进行推理过程中会面临重大挑战，尤其是由于其庞大的模型规模。激活稀疏性为减少计算和内存移动、实现更高效推理提供了一种有前景的解决方案，特别适用于小型批次的本地应用。然而，当前的方法在处理大多数先进LLMs所依赖的非ReLU激活函数时存在局限性，或者需要大量的持续训练。此外，预测活跃通道的难度以及可实现的稀疏度比率有限，限制了基于激活稀疏性的方法的有效性。在这篇论文中，我们介绍了R-Sparse，这是一种无需训练的激活稀疏性方法，能够实现高级LLMs中的高稀疏度水平。我们对单个线性层内的不同组件如何影响输出进行了两项初步研究，并发现了两个关键观察结果：(i) 输入函数的非稀疏部分可以视为少数几个偏置项；(ii) 全部计算可以通过适当组合输入通道和权重奇异值来有效近似。在此基础上，我们将LLMs中的线性层替换为一种考虑秩感知的稀疏推理方法，该方法利用输入通道和奇异值分量的稀疏性，从而消除了类似于基于输出稀疏性的方法所需的活跃通道预测步骤。实验结果显示，在Llama-2/3和Mistral模型上进行的十项不同任务表明，R-Sparse能够在50%的模型级别稀疏性下达到相当的性能，这带来了高达43%的整体效率提升，得益于定制化的内核。

[No.47] LoRanPAC: Low-rank Random Features and Pre-trained Models for Bridging Theory and Practice in Continual Learning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: LoRanPAC：低秩随机特征与预训练模型在连续学习中理论与实践的桥梁
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Liangzu Peng;Juan Elenter;Joshua Agterberg;Alejandro Ribeiro;Rene Vidal;
机构: University of Pennsylvania;Spotify;University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;Amazon;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29087
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00645
英摘: The goal of continual learning (CL) is to train a model that can solve multiple tasks presented sequentially. Recent CL approaches have achieved strong performance by leveraging large pre-trained models that generalize well to downstream tasks. However, such methods lack theoretical guarantees, making them prone to unexpected failures. Conversely, principled CL approaches often fail to achieve competitive performance. In this work, we aim to bridge this gap between theory and practice by designing a simple CL method that is theoretically sound and highly performant. Specifically, we lift pre-trained features into a higher dimensional space and formulate an over-parametrized minimum-norm least-squares problem. We find that the lifted features are highly ill-conditioned, potentially leading to large training errors (numerical instability) and increased generalization errors. We address these challenges by continually truncating the singular value decomposition of the lifted features. Our approach, termed LoRanPAC, is stable with respect to the choice of hyperparameters, can handle hundreds of tasks, and outperforms state-of-the-art CL methods on multiple datasets. Importantly, our method satisfies a recurrence relation throughout its continual learning process, which allows us to prove it maintains small training and test errors by appropriately truncating a fraction of SVD factors. This results in a stable continual learning method with strong empirical performance and theoretical guarantees. Code available: https://github.com/liangzu/loranpac.
中摘: 持续学习（CL）的目标是训练一个模型以解决按顺序呈现的多个任务。最近的CL方法通过利用泛化能力强的大规模预训练模型取得了强大的性能。然而，这些方法缺乏理论保证，容易出现意外失败。相反，基于原理的CL方法往往难以达到竞争力的性能。在本工作中，我们旨在通过设计一种既理论严谨又高性能的简单CL方法来弥合理论与实践之间的差距。具体来说，我们将预训练特征提升到更高维度的空间，并形式化为过参数化的最小范数最小二乘问题。我们发现提升后的特征条件数非常高，可能导致较大的训练误差（数值不稳定）和增加的泛化误差。为了应对这些挑战，我们不断截断提升特征的奇异值分解。我们的方法称为LoRanPAC，在超参数选择方面具有稳定性，能够处理数百个任务，并在多个数据集上优于最先进的CL方法。重要的是，我们的方法在其持续学习过程中满足递归关系，这使我们能够证明通过适当地截断一部分SVD因子，它能保持较小的训练误差和测试误差。这一成果使得我们的方法成为一种稳定、具有强大实证性能和理论保证的持续学习方法。代码已公开：https://github.com/liangzu/loranpac。

[No.48] Decoupling Angles and Strength in Low-rank Adaptation
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩适应中的角度与强度解耦
类别: transfer learning, meta learning, and lifelong learning
作者: Massimo Bini;Leander Girrbach;Zeynep Akata;
机构: ;Helmholtz Zentrum München;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29327
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18225
英摘: Parameter-Efficient FineTuning (PEFT) methods have recently gained significant popularity thanks to the widespread availability of large-scale pretrained models. These methods allow for quick adaptation to downstream tasks with minimal computational cost. However, popular finetuning methods such as LoRA exhibit limited robustness when it comes to hyperparameter choices or extended training regimes, preventing optimal out-of-the-box performance. In contrast, bounded approaches, such as ETHER, provide greater robustness but are limited to extremely low-rank adaptations and fixed-strength transformations, reducing their adaptation expressive power. In this work, we propose Decoupled Low-rank Adaptation (DeLoRA), a novel finetuning method that normalizes and scales learnable low-rank matrices. By bounding the distance of the transformation, DeLoRA effectively decouples the angular learning from the adaptation strength, enhancing robustness without compromising performance. Through evaluations on subject-driven image generation, natural language understanding, and instruction tuning, we show that DeLoRA matches or surpasses performance of competing PEFT methods, while exhibiting stronger robustness. Code is available at https://github.com/ExplainableML/DeLoRA.
中摘: 参数高效微调（PEFT）方法近年来因其大规模预训练模型的广泛应用而备受关注。这些方法允许以最小的计算成本快速适应下游任务。然而，诸如LoRA等流行的微调方法在超参数选择或扩展训练方案方面表现出有限的鲁棒性，这限制了它们开箱即用的最佳性能。相比之下，ETHER等有界方法提供了更大的鲁棒性，但它们仅限于极低秩的适应和固定强度的转换，从而降低了其适应表达能力。在本工作中，我们提出了一种名为解耦低秩适应（DeLoRA）的新微调方法，该方法通过归一化和缩放可学习的低秩矩阵来进行微调。通过限制转换的距离，DeLoRA有效地解耦了角度学习与适应强度，增强了鲁棒性而不牺牲性能。通过对主题驱动的图像生成、自然语言理解以及指令调优的评估，我们展示了DeLoRA在性能上能够匹配甚至超越竞争对手的PEFT方法，同时表现出更强的鲁棒性。相关代码可在https://github.com/ExplainableML/DeLoRA获取。
