[No.49] Dynamic Low-Rank Sparse Adaptation for Large Language Models
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 动态低秩稀疏适应性调整大型语言模型
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Weizhong Huang;Yuxin Zhang;Xiawu Zheng;Liuyang;Jing Lin;Yiwu Yao;Rongrong Ji;
机构: Xiamen University;Huawei;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28352
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14816
英摘: Despite the efficacy of network sparsity in alleviating the deployment strain of Large Language Models (LLMs), it endures significant performance degradation. Applying Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to fine-tune the sparse LLMs offers an intuitive approach to counter this predicament, while it holds shortcomings include: 1) The inability to integrate LoRA weights into sparse LLMs post-training, and 2) Insufficient performance recovery at high sparsity ratios. In this paper, we introduce dynamic Low-rank Sparse Adaptation (LoSA), a novel method that seamlessly integrates low-rank adaptation into LLM sparsity within a unified framework, thereby enhancing the performance of sparse LLMs without increasing the inference latency. In particular, LoSA dynamically sparsifies the LoRA outcomes based on the corresponding sparse weights during fine-tuning, thus guaranteeing that the LoRA module can be integrated into the sparse LLMs post-training. Besides, LoSA leverages Representation Mutual Information (RMI) as an indicator to determine the importance of layers, thereby efficiently determining the layer-wise sparsity rates during fine-tuning. Predicated on this, LoSA adjusts the rank of the LoRA module based on the variability in layer-wise reconstruction errors, allocating an appropriate fine-tuning for each layer to reduce the output discrepancies between dense and sparse LLMs. Extensive experiments tell that LoSA can efficiently boost the efficacy of sparse LLMs within a few hours, without introducing any additional inferential burden. For example, LoSA reduced the perplexity of sparse LLaMA-2-7B by 68.73 and increased zero-shot accuracy by 16.32$\%$, achieving a 2.60$\times$ speedup on CPU and 2.23$\times$ speedup on GPU, requiring only 45 minutes of fine-tuning on a single NVIDIA A100 80GB GPU. Code is available at https://github.com/wzhuang-xmu/LoSA.
中摘: 尽管网络稀疏性在缓解大型语言模型（LLMs）部署压力方面具有有效性，但它仍然会遭受显著的性能下降。将低秩适应（LoRA）应用于稀疏LLM的微调提供了一种直观的方法来应对这一困境，但这种方法也存在一些缺点，包括：1）训练后无法将LoRA权重整合到稀疏LLM中，以及2）在高稀疏比下性能恢复不足。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为动态低秩稀疏适应（LoSA）的新方法，该方法在统一框架内无缝地将低秩适应整合到LLM稀疏性中，从而在不增加推理延迟的情况下增强稀疏LLM的性能。特别地，LoSA 在微调过程中根据相应的稀疏权重动态稀疏化LoRA结果，从而确保可以在训练后将LoRA模块整合到稀疏LLM中。此外，LoSA 利用表示互信息（RMI）作为指标来确定各层的重要性，从而在微调过程中高效地确定各层的稀疏率。基于此，LoSA 根据各层重构误差的变化调整LoRA模块的秩，为每个层分配适当的微调，以减少密集和稀疏LLM之间的输出差异。广泛的实验表明，LoSA 可以在几小时内有效地提升稀疏LLM的效果，而不引入任何额外的推理负担。例如，LoSA 将稀疏LLaMA-2-7B的困惑度降低了68.73%，零样本准确率提高了16.32%，并在CPU上实现了2.60倍的速度提升，在GPU上实现了2.23倍的速度提升，仅需在单个NVIDIA A100 80GB GPU上进行45分钟的微调。代码可在https://github.com/wzhuang-xmu/LoSA获取。

[No.50] Attention in Large Language Models Yields Efficient Zero-Shot Re-Rankers
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: rank
标题: 大型语言模型中的注意力机制产生了高效的零样本重排序器
类别: applications to computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Shijie Chen;Bernal Jimenez Gutierrez;Yu Su;
机构: Ohio State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/27696
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02642
英摘: Information retrieval (IR) systems have played a vital role in modern digital life and have cemented their continued usefulness in this new era of generative AI via retrieval-augmented generation. With strong language processing capabilities and remarkable versatility, large language models (LLMs) have become popular choices for zero-shot re-ranking in IR systems. So far, LLM-based re-ranking methods rely on strong generative capabilities, which restricts their use to either specialized or powerful proprietary models. Given these restrictions, we ask: is autoregressive generation necessary and optimal for LLMs to perform re-ranking? We hypothesize that there are abundant signals relevant to re-ranking within LLMs that might not be used to their full potential via generation. To more directly leverage such signals, we propose in-context re-ranking (ICR), a novel method that leverages the change in attention pattern caused by the search query for accurate and efficient re-ranking. To mitigate the intrinsic biases in LLMs, we propose a calibration method using a content-free query. Due to the absence of generation, ICR only requires two ($O(1)$) forward passes to re-rank $N$ documents, making it substantially more efficient than generative re-ranking methods that require at least $O(N)$ forward passes. Our novel design also enables ICR to be applied to any LLM without specialized training while guaranteeing a well-formed ranking. Extensive experiments with two popular open-weight LLMs on standard single-hop and multi-hop information retrieval benchmarks show that ICR outperforms RankGPT while cutting the latency by more than 60% in practice. Through detailed analyses, we show that ICR's performance is specially strong on tasks that require more complex re-ranking signals. Our findings call for further exploration on novel ways of utilizing open-weight LLMs beyond text generation.
中摘: 信息检索（IR）系统在现代数字生活中发挥了至关重要的作用，并通过检索增强生成技术巩固了其在生成式AI新时代的持续 usefulness。凭借强大的语言处理能力和显著的灵活性，大规模语言模型（LLMs）已成为零样本重排序IR系统的热门选择。迄今为止，基于LLM的重排序方法依赖于强大的生成能力，这限制了它们仅能用于专门或强大的专有模型。鉴于这些限制，我们提出一个问题：自回归生成对于LLM执行重排序是否必要且最优？我们假设LLM内部存在大量可用于重排序的相关信号，但这些信号可能并未被充分利用。为了更直接地利用这些信号，我们提出了上下文内重排序（ICR），这是一种新颖的方法，通过利用搜索查询引起的注意力模式变化来进行准确高效的重排序。为减轻LLM固有的偏差，我们提出了一种使用内容无关查询的校准方法。由于不涉及生成过程，ICR只需要两次（$O(1)$）前向传递即可对$N$份文档进行重排序，使其比至少需要$O(N)$次前向传递的生成式重排序方法更为高效。我们的新设计还使ICR能够无需专门训练即可应用于任何LLM，同时保证生成一个合理的排名。在标准单跳和多跳信息检索基准测试中，使用两种流行的开源权重LLM与RankGPT进行的广泛实验表明，在实践中，ICR不仅能超越RankGPT，还能将延迟减少超过60%。通过详细的分析，我们展示了ICR在需要更复杂重排序信号的任务上表现尤为出色。我们的研究结果呼吁进一步探索利用开源权重LLM的新方式，而不仅仅是用于文本生成。

[No.51] A Stronger Mixture of Low-Rank Experts for Fine-Tuning Foundation Models
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 面向基础模型微调的一种更强的低秩专家混合方法
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Mengyang Sun;Yihao Wang;Tao Feng;Dan Zhang;Yifan Zhu;Jie Tang;
机构: Tsinghua University;Beijing Infomation Science and Technology University;Beijing University of Posts and Telecommunications;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43508
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15828
英摘: In order to streamline the fine-tuning of foundation models, Low-Rank Adapters (LoRAs) have been substantially adopted across various fields, including instruction tuning and domain adaptation. The underlying concept of LoRA involves decomposing a full-rank matrix into the product of two lower-rank matrices, which reduces storage consumption and accelerates the training process. Furthermore, to address the limited expressive capacity of LoRA, the Mixture-of-Expert (MoE) has been introduced for incorporating multiple LoRA adapters. The integration of LoRA experts leads to a visible improvement across several downstream scenes. However, the mixture of LoRAs (MoE-LoRA) still exhibits its low robustness during tuning and inferring. Inspired by the Riemannian Preconditioners which train LoRA as a sub-space projector, we propose a new training strategy for MoE-LoRA, to stabilize and boost its feature learning procedure by multi-space projections. Examinations on SGD and AdamW optimizers demonstrate the effectiveness of our methodology. Source code is available at https://github.com/THUDM/MoELoRA_Riemannian.
中摘: 为了简化基础模型的微调过程，低秩适配器（LoRAs）已在指令调优和领域适应等多个领域得到了广泛采用。LoRA的基本概念涉及将一个满秩矩阵分解为两个低秩矩阵的乘积，这可以减少存储消耗并加速训练过程。此外，为了应对LoRA表达能力有限的问题，引入了专家混合（Mixture-of-Experts, MoE）来结合多个LoRA适配器。LoRA专家的集成在多个下游场景中带来了显著改进。然而，LoRAs的混合（MoE-LoRA）在调优和推理过程中仍然表现出较低的鲁棒性。受黎曼预条件化训练LoRA作为子空间投影器的启发，我们提出了一种新的MoE-LoRA训练策略，通过多空间投影来稳定并提升其特征学习过程。对SGD和AdamW优化器的测试表明，我们的方法具有有效性。源代码可在https://github.com/THUDM/MoELoRA_Riemannian获取。

[No.52] GL-LowPopArt: A Nearly Instance-Wise Minimax-Optimal Estimator for Generalized Low-Rank Trace Regression
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: GL-LowPopArt：一种针对广义低秩迹回归的几乎实例最优的最小最大优化估计器
类别: theory->learning theory
作者: Junghyun Lee;Kyoungseok Jang;Kwang-Sung Jun;Milan Vojnovic;Se-Young Yun;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;Adobe;Chung-Ang University;University of Milan;University of Arizona;London School of Economics;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45149
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03074
英摘: We present `GL-LowPopArt`, a novel Catoni-style estimator for generalized low-rank trace regression. Building on `LowPopArt` (Jang et al., 2024), it employs a two-stage approach: nuclear norm regularization followed by matrix Catoni estimation. We establish state-of-the-art estimation error bounds, surpassing existing guarantees (Fan et al., 2019; Kang et al., 2022), and reveal a novel experimental design objective, $\mathrm{GL}(π)$. The key technical challenge is controlling bias from the nonlinear inverse link function, which we address by our two-stage approach. We prove a *local* minimax lower bound, showing that our `GL-LowPopArt` enjoys instance-wise optimality up to the condition number of the ground-truth Hessian. Applications include generalized linear matrix completion, where `GL-LowPopArt` achieves a state-of-the-art Frobenius error guarantee, and **bilinear dueling bandits**, a novel setting inspired by general preference learning (Zhang et al., 2024). Our analysis of a `GL-LowPopArt`-based explore-then-commit algorithm reveals a new, potentially interesting problem-dependent quantity, along with improved Borda regret bound than vectorization (Wu et al., 2024).
中摘: 我们提出了一种新的Catoni风格估计器`GL-LowPopArt`，用于广义低秩迹回归。该方法基于`LowPopArt`（Jang等，2024），采用两阶段的方法：核范数正则化随后进行矩阵Catoni估计。我们建立了最先进的估计误差界，超越了现有的保证（Fan等，2019；Kang等，2022），并揭示了一个新颖的实验设计目标，即$\mathrm{GL}(π)$。关键的技术挑战在于控制来自非线性逆链接函数的偏差，我们通过两阶段的方法解决了这一问题。我们证明了一个局部最小最大下界，表明我们的`GL-LowPopArt`在地面真实海森矩阵条件数范围内享受实例最优性。应用包括广义线性矩阵补全，在这里`GL-LowPopArt`实现了最先进的Frobenius误差保证，并且在双线性对战多臂老虎机（bilinear dueling bandits）这一新颖设置中也有应用，该设置受到一般偏好学习（Zhang等，2024）的启发。基于`GL-LowPopArt`的探索然后提交算法的分析揭示了一个新的、潜在有趣的问题依赖量，并且相比向量化方法（Wu等，2024）提供了改进的Borda后悔界。

[No.53] Improving Your Model Ranking on Chatbot Arena by Vote Rigging
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 通过操控投票提升你在聊天机器人竞技场中的模型排名
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Rui Min;Tianyu Pang;Chao Du;Qian Liu;Minhao Cheng;Min Lin;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Sea AI Lab;TikTok;Pennsylvania State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46419
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.17858
英摘: Chatbot Arena is a popular platform for evaluating LLMs by pairwise battles, where users vote for their preferred response from two randomly sampled anonymous models. While Chatbot Arena is widely regarded as a reliable LLM ranking leaderboard, we show that crowdsourced voting can be rigged to improve (or decrease) the ranking of a target model $m_{t}$. We first introduce a straightforward target-only rigging strategy that focuses on new battles involving $m_{t}$, identifying it via watermarking or a binary classifier, and exclusively voting for $m_{t}$ wins. However, this strategy is practically inefficient because there are over $190$ models on Chatbot Arena and on average only about $1\%$ of new battles will involve $m_{t}$. To overcome this, we propose omnipresent rigging strategies, exploiting the Elo rating mechanism of Chatbot Arena that any new vote on a battle can influence the ranking of the target model $m_{t}$, even if $m_{t}$ is not directly involved in the battle. We conduct experiments on around $1.7$ million historical votes from the Chatbot Arena Notebook, showing that omnipresent rigging strategies can improve model rankings by rigging only hundreds of new votes. While we have evaluated several defense mechanisms, our findings highlight the importance of continued efforts to prevent vote rigging. Our code is available at https://github.com/sail-sg/Rigging-ChatbotArena.
中摘: Chatbot Arena 是一个流行的平台，用于通过一对一比拼来评估语言模型（LLMs），用户可以从两个随机抽取的匿名模型中投票选择他们更喜欢的回应。尽管 Chatbot Arena 广泛被认为是可靠的 LLM 排名排行榜，但我们表明，通过操纵投票可以提高（或降低）目标模型 \( m_t \) 的排名。我们首先介绍了一种简单的目标模型专用操纵策略，该策略专注于涉及 \( m_t \) 的新比拼，通过水印或二元分类器识别 \( m_t \)，并仅为其胜利投票。然而，这种策略在实践中效率低下，因为 Chatbot Arena 上有超过 190 个模型，平均而言，只有大约 1% 的新比拼会涉及 \( m_t \)。为克服这一问题，我们提出了无所不在的操纵策略，利用 Chatbot Arena 的 Elo 积分机制，任何新的投票都可以影响目标模型 \( m_t \) 的排名，即使 \( m_t \) 没有直接参与该比拼。我们在 Chatbot Arena 笔记本上进行了约 170 万次历史投票的实验，结果显示，通过操纵数百个新投票即可提高模型排名。虽然我们评估了几种防御机制，但我们的发现强调了继续努力防止投票操纵的重要性。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/sail-sg/Rigging-ChatbotArena 获取。

[No.55] Representative Ranking for Deliberation in the Public Sphere
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 公共领域中的审议代表性排名
类别: social aspects
作者: Manon Revel;Smitha Milli;Tyler Lu;Jamelle Watson-Daniels;Maximilian Nickel;
机构: Harvard University;Meta;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45374
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18962
英摘: Online comment sections, such as those on news sites or social media, have the potential to foster informal public deliberation, However, this potential is often undermined by the frequency of toxic or low-quality exchanges that occur in these settings. To combat this, platforms increasingly leverage algorithmic ranking to facilitate higher-quality discussions, e.g., by using civility classifiers or forms of prosocial ranking. Yet, these interventions may also inadvertently reduce the visibility of legitimate viewpoints, undermining another key aspect of deliberation: representation of diverse views. We seek to remedy this problem by introducing guarantees of representation into these methods. In particular, we adopt the notion of justified representation (JR) from the social choice literature and incorporate a JR constraint into the comment ranking setting. We find that enforcing JR leads to greater inclusion of diverse viewpoints while still being compatible with optimizing for user engagement or other measures of conversational quality.
中摘: 在线评论区，如新闻网站或社交媒体上的评论区，本有可能促进非正式的公众讨论。然而，这些评论区经常充斥着有毒或低质量的交流，从而削弱了这一潜力。为了应对这个问题，平台越来越多地利用算法排名来促进高质量的讨论，例如使用文明度分类器或促进社会性排名的方法。然而，这些干预措施也可能无意中降低了合法观点的可见性，从而削弱了另一个关键的讨论方面：多样观点的代表。我们希望通过在这些方法中引入多样观点的保障来解决这个问题。具体而言，我们从社会选择文献中采用了正当代表（JR）的概念，并将其约束条件融入到评论排名的设置中。我们发现，强制执行JR能够增加多样观点的包容性，同时仍然与优化用户参与度或其他对话质量指标兼容。

[No.56] Low-Rank Adapting Models for Sparse Autoencoders
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩适应模型Sparse Autoencoders的稀疏自编码器
类别: social aspects->accountability transparency and interpretability
作者: Matthew Chen;Joshua Engels;Max Tegmark;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44210
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.19406
英摘: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose language model representations into a sparse set of linear latent vectors. Recent works have improved SAEs using language model gradients, but these techniques require many expensive backward passes during training and still cause a significant increase in cross entropy loss when SAE reconstructions are inserted into the model. In this work, we improve on these limitations by taking a fundamentally different approach: we use low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to finetune the \textit{language model itself} around a previously trained SAE. We analyze our method across SAE sparsity, SAE width, language model size, LoRA rank, and model layer on the Gemma Scope family of SAEs. In these settings, our method reduces the cross entropy loss gap by 30\% to 55\% when SAEs are inserted during the forward pass. We also find that compared to end-to-end (e2e) SAEs, our approach achieves the same downstream cross entropy loss 3$\times$ to 20$\times$ faster on \gemma and 2$\times$ to 10$\times$ faster on \llama. We further show that our technique improves downstream metrics and can adapt multiple SAEs at once without harming general language model capabilities. Our results demonstrate that improving model interpretability is not limited to post-hoc SAE training; Pareto improvements can also be achieved by directly optimizing the model itself.
中摘: 稀疏自编码器（SAEs）将语言模型表示分解为一组稀疏的线性潜在向量。最近的研究通过使用语言模型梯度改进了SAEs，但这些技术在训练过程中需要许多昂贵的反向传播，并且当将SAE重构插入模型时，仍然会导致交叉熵损失显著增加。在这项工作中，我们通过采取一种根本不同的方法来改进这些限制：我们使用低秩适应（LoRA）对语言模型本身进行微调，使其围绕之前训练好的SAE进行调整。我们在Gemma Scope系列SAE的不同稀疏性、SAE宽度、语言模型规模、LoRA秩以及模型层面上分析了我们的方法。在这些设置中，当在前向传播过程中插入SAE时，我们的方法将交叉熵损失差距减少了30%到55%。我们还发现，与端到端（e2e）的SAE相比，我们的方法在处理Gemma和Llama时，速度提高了3倍到20倍。此外，我们展示了我们的技术可以提高下游指标，而且可以在不损害通用语言模型能力的情况下同时适应多个SAE。我们的研究结果表明，提高模型可解释性的方法不仅限于事后SAE训练；通过直接优化模型本身也可以实现帕累托改进。

[No.57] Low-Rank Thinning
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩稀疏化
类别: theory->probabilistic methods
作者: Annabelle Michael Carrell;Albert Gong;Abhishek Shetty;Raaz Dwivedi;Lester Mackey;
机构: University of Cambridge;Cornell University;University of California, Berkeley;Microsoft;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44371
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12063
英摘: The goal in thinning is to summarize a dataset using a small set of representative points. Remarkably, sub-Gaussian thinning algorithms like Kernel Halving and Compress can match the quality of uniform subsampling while substantially reducing the number of summary points. However, existing guarantees cover only a restricted range of distributions and kernel-based quality measures and suffer from pessimistic dimension dependence. To address these deficiencies, we introduce a new low-rank analysis of sub-Gaussian thinning that applies to any distribution and any kernel, guaranteeing high-quality compression whenever the kernel or data matrix is approximately low-rank. To demonstrate the broad applicability of the techniques, we design practical sub-Gaussian thinning approaches that improve upon the best known guarantees for approximating attention in transformers, accelerating stochastic gradient training through reordering, and distinguishing distributions in near-linear time.
中摘: 在稀疏化的目标是使用少量的代表性点来总结一个数据集。令人惊讶的是，像核减半和压缩这样的亚高斯稀疏化算法可以在显著减少总结点数量的同时，达到与均匀子采样相当的质量。然而，现有的保证仅覆盖了有限范围的分布和基于核的质量度量，并且受到悲观维度依赖性的困扰。为了弥补这些缺陷，我们引入了一种新的低秩分析方法来应用于任何分布和任何核，在核或数据矩阵近似低秩的情况下，保证能够实现高质量的压缩。为了展示这些技术的广泛适用性，我们设计了实用的亚高斯稀疏化方法，这些方法在逼近变换器中的注意力机制、通过重新排序加速随机梯度训练以及在接近线性时间内区分分布方面优于现有最佳保证。

[No.58] LapSum - One Method to Differentiate Them All: Ranking, Sorting and Top-k Selection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: LapSum - 一种区分一切的方法：排名、排序和Top-k选择
类别: deep learning->algorithms
作者: Łukasz Struski;Michal B. Bednarczyk;Igor T. Podolak;Jacek Tabor;
机构: Jagiellonian University;University of Illinois;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46060
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06242
英摘: We present a novel technique for constructing differentiable order-type operations, including soft ranking, soft top-k selection, and soft permutations. Our approach leverages an efficient closed-form formula for the inverse of the function LapSum, defined as the sum of Laplace distributions. This formulation ensures low computational and memory complexity in selecting the highest activations, enabling losses and gradients to be computed in $O(n\log{}n)$ time. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art techniques for high-dimensional vectors and large $k$ values. Furthermore, we provide efficient implementations for both CPU and CUDA environments, underscoring the practicality and scalability of our method for large-scale ranking and differentiable ordering problems.
中摘: 我们提出了一种新颖的技术，用于构建可微序类型操作，包括软排名、软Top-k选择和软排列。我们的方法利用了LapSum函数（定义为拉普拉斯分布之和）的逆函数的一种高效闭形式公式。这种公式确保在选择最高激活值时具有较低的计算和内存复杂度，使得损失和梯度可以在$O(n\log{}n)$时间内计算。通过大量的实验，我们证明了我们的方法在高维向量和大$k$值的情况下优于现有的先进技术。此外，我们提供了适用于CPU和CUDA环境的高效实现，突显了我们的方法在大规模排名和可微排序问题中的实用性和可扩展性。

[No.60] Learning Cascade Ranking as One Network
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 学习级联排序作为一个网络
类别: applications->everything else
作者: Yunli Wang;ZhenZhang;Zhiqiang Wang;Zixuan Yang;Yu Li;Jian Yang;Shiyang Wen;Peng Jiang;Kun Gai;
机构: Kuaishou Technology;;Kwai Technology;Beijing University of Posts and Telecommunications;Alibaba Group;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44498
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09492
英摘: Cascade Ranking is a prevalent architecture in large-scale top-k selection systems like recommendation and advertising platforms. Traditional training methods focus on single-stage optimization, neglecting interactions between stages. Recent advances have introduced interaction-aware training paradigms, but still struggle to 1) align training objectives with the goal of the entire cascade ranking (i.e., end-to-end recall of ground-truth items) and 2) learn effective collaboration patterns for different stages. To address these challenges, we propose LCRON, which introduces a novel surrogate loss function derived from the lower bound probability that ground truth items are selected by cascade ranking, ensuring alignment with the overall objective of the system. According to the properties of the derived bound, we further design an auxiliary loss for each stage to drive the reduction of this bound, leading to a more robust and effective top-k selection. LCRON enables end-to-end training of the entire cascade ranking system as a unified network. Experimental results demonstrate that LCRON achieves significant improvement over existing methods on public benchmarks and industrial applications, addressing key limitations in cascade ranking training and significantly enhancing system performance.
中摘: 级联排名是一种在推荐和广告平台等大规模top-k选择系统中常见的架构。传统的训练方法侧重于单阶段优化，忽视了各阶段之间的交互作用。最近的研究引入了交互感知的训练范式，但仍难以做到以下两点：1）将训练目标与整个级联排名系统的整体目标（即端到端的真实项召回率）对齐；2）学习不同阶段的有效协作模式。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了LCRON，它引入了一个新颖的代理损失函数，该函数源自级联排名中真实项被选中的下界概率，确保了与系统整体目标的一致性。根据所推导出的下界的性质，我们进一步为每个阶段设计了一个辅助损失，以驱动降低这个下界，从而实现更稳健和有效的top-k选择。LCRON能够将整个级联排名系统作为一个统一网络进行端到端的训练。实验结果表明，LCRON在公共基准测试和工业应用中显著优于现有方法，解决了级联排名训练的关键限制，并大幅提升了系统性能。

英摘: Vision GNN (ViG) demonstrates superior performance by representing images as graph structures, providing a more natural way to capture irregular semantic patterns beyond traditional grid or sequence-based representations. [No.62] LIFT the Veil for the Truth: Principal Weights Emerge after Rank Reduction for Reasoning-Focused Supervised Fine-Tuning
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 揭开真相的面纱：推理导向的监督微调在秩减少后凸显主要权重
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Zihang Liu;Tianyu Pang;Oleg Balabanov;Chaoqun Yang;Tianjin Huang;Lu Yin;Yaoqing Yang;Shiwei Liu;
机构: University of California, Berkeley;Dartmouth College;Nanjing University;Lawrence Berkeley National Laboratory;Tsinghua University;University of Exeter;University of Surrey;University of Oxford;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44967
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00772
英摘: Recent studies have shown that supervised fine-tuning of LLMs on a small number of high-quality datasets can yield strong reasoning capabilities. However, full fine-tuning (Full FT), while powerful, is computationally expensive and susceptible to overfitting and catastrophic forgetting, particularly when data is limited. Sparse fine-tuning, which previously achieved notable success by updating only a small subset of model parameters, offers a promising trade-off between efficiency and effectiveness. Yet, it has lagged behind in the LLM era due to the difficulty of identifying parameters truly critical for reasoning. In this work, we state that weights with the largest magnitude after low-rank approximation are critical weights for fine-tuning, which we call Principal Weights. Surprisingly, while magnitude-based sparse fine-tuning performs poorly as a baseline on LLM fine-tuning, it becomes highly effective after rank reduction. These insights motivate our method: Low-rank Informed Sparse Fine-Tuning (LIFT). LIFT only updates the top 5% Principal Weights throughout training and consistently achieves better performance on reasoning tasks than Full FT, while maintaining memory efficiency on par with popular parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods. In addition to strong performance on target domains such as arithmetic reasoning, LIFT also retains up to 20% more source-domain knowledge, compared to Full FT and LoRA. Our code is available at: https://github.com/zihanghliu/LIFT.
中摘: 最近的研究表明，在少量高质量数据集上监督微调大型语言模型（LLMs）可以产生强大的推理能力。然而，完全微调（全量微调）虽然强大，但在计算成本方面较高，并且容易过拟合和灾难性遗忘，尤其是在数据有限的情况下。稀疏微调通过仅更新模型参数的小部分子集，在效率与效果之间提供了有前景的权衡，此前已经取得了显著的成功。但由于难以识别真正对推理至关重要的参数，它在LLM时代的发展相对滞后。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种观点：权重在低秩近似后的最大值是关键权重，我们称之为主权重。令人惊讶的是，基于权重大小的稀疏微调在LLM微调中作为基线表现不佳，但在低秩减少后变得非常有效。这些见解促使我们提出了一种方法：低秩引导稀疏微调（LIFT）。在整个训练过程中，LIFT仅更新顶部5%的主权重，并且在推理任务上的表现始终优于全量微调，同时保持与流行参数高效微调方法相当的记忆效率。除了在目标领域如算术推理中表现出色外，LIFT还保留了比全量微调和LoRA多出高达20%的源领域知识。我们的代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/zihanghliu/LIFT。

[No.64] RepLoRA: Reparameterizing Low-rank Adaptation via the Perspective of Mixture of Experts
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: RepLoRA: 基于专家混合视角重构低秩适应参数化
类别: general machine learning->transfer multitask and metalearning
作者: Tuan Truong;Chau Nguyen;Huy Nguyen;Minh Le;Trung Le;Nhat Ho;
机构: University of British Columbia;;University of Texas at Austin;Qualcomm Incorporated;VinAI Research;Monash University;
类型: Poster

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03044
英摘: Low-rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a powerful method for fine-tuning large-scale foundation models. Despite its popularity, the theoretical understanding of LoRA has remained limited. This paper presents a theoretical analysis of LoRA by examining its connection to the Mixture of Experts models. Under this framework, we show that simple reparameterizations of the LoRA matrices can notably accelerate the low-rank matrix estimation process. In particular, we prove that reparameterization can reduce the data needed to achieve a desired estimation error from an exponential to a polynomial scale. Motivated by this insight, we propose Reparameterized Low-Rank Adaptation (RepLoRA), which incorporates lightweight MLPs to reparameterize the LoRA matrices. Extensive experiments across multiple domains demonstrate that RepLoRA consistently outperforms vanilla LoRA. Notably, with limited data, RepLoRA surpasses LoRA by a margin of up to 40.0% and achieves LoRA's performance with only 30.0% of the training data, highlighting both the theoretical and empirical robustness of our PEFT method.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）已成为一种强大的大规模基础模型微调方法。尽管其广受欢迎，但对LoRA的理论理解仍然有限。本文通过探讨LoRA与专家混合模型之间的联系，对其进行了理论分析。在这一框架下，我们展示了LoRA矩阵的简单重新参数化可以显著加速低秩矩阵估计过程。特别是，我们证明了重新参数化能够将实现所需估计误差所需的数据量从指数级减少到多项式级。受到这一洞察的启发，我们提出了重新参数化低秩适应（RepLoRA），该方法结合了轻量级的MLP来重新参数化LoRA矩阵。在多个领域的广泛实验表明，RepLoRA始终优于传统的LoRA。值得注意的是，在有限数据的情况下，RepLoRA能够比LoRA高出40.0%，并且仅使用LoRA训练数据的30.0%就能达到其性能，这突显了我们PEFT方法的理论和实证稳健性。

[No.65] Flat-LoRA: Low-Rank Adaptation over a Flat Loss Landscape
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 平展洛拉：平坦损失景观上的低秩适应
类别: optimization
作者: Tao Li;Zhengbao He;Yujun Li;Yasheng Wang;Lifeng Shang;Xiaolin Huang;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;Huawei;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46534
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14396
英摘: Fine-tuning large-scale pre-trained models is prohibitively expensive in terms of computation and memory costs. Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), a popular Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) method, offers an efficient solution by optimizing only low-rank matrices. Despite recent progress in improving LoRA's performance, the relationship between the LoRA optimization space and the full parameter space is often overlooked. A solution that appears flat in the loss landscape of the LoRA space may still exhibit sharp directions in the full parameter space, potentially compromising generalization. We introduce Flat-LoRA, which aims to identify a low-rank adaptation situated in a flat region of the full parameter space. Instead of adopting the well-established sharpness-aware minimization approach, which incurs significant computation and memory overheads, we employ a Bayesian expectation loss objective to preserve training efficiency. Further, we design a refined random perturbation generation strategy for improved performance and carefully manage memory overhead using random seeds. Experiments across diverse tasks-including mathematical reasoning, coding abilities, dialogue generation, instruction following, and text-to-image generation-demonstrate that Flat-LoRA improves both in-domain and out-of-domain generalization. Code is available at https://github.com/nblt/Flat-LoRA.
中摘: 大规模预训练模型的微调在计算和内存成本上是极其昂贵的。低秩适应（LoRA），一种流行的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法，通过优化低秩矩阵提供了一个高效的解决方案。尽管最近在提高LoRA性能方面取得了一定进展，但LoRA优化空间与完整参数空间之间的关系往往被忽视。在LoRA空间的损失景观中看似平坦的解，在完整参数空间中仍可能表现出尖锐的方向，这可能会损害泛化能力。我们引入了Flat-LoRA，旨在识别一个位于完整参数空间平坦区域的低秩适应。我们没有采用现有的需要大量计算和内存开销的尖锐性感知最小化方法，而是使用了贝叶斯期望损失目标来保持训练效率。此外，我们设计了一种改进的随机扰动生成策略以提高性能，并通过随机种子仔细管理内存开销。实验结果显示，Flat-LoRA 在多种任务中均能提高领域内和领域外的泛化能力。代码可在https://github.com/nblt/Flat-LoRA获取。

[No.66] Mind the Gap: a Spectral Analysis of Rank Collapse and Signal Propagation in Attention Layers
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 注意差距：注意力层中排名崩溃和信号传播的光谱分析
类别: theory->deep learning
作者: Thiziri Nait Saada;Alireza Naderi;Jared Tanner;
机构: University of Oxford;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43837
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07799
英摘: Attention layers are the core component of transformers, the current state-of-the-art neural network architecture. Alternatives to softmax-based attention are being explored due to its tendency to hinder effective information flow. Even at initialisation, it remains poorly understood why the propagation of signals and gradients through these random networks can be pathological, resulting in issues known as (i) vanishing/exploding gradients and (ii) rank collapse $\textit{in depth}$, i.e. when all tokens converge to a single representation along layers. While rank collapse in depth naturally arises from repeated matrix multiplications$\unicode{x2013}$a common pattern across various architectures$\unicode{x2013}$we identify an additional and previously unknown challenge unique to softmax attention layers: (iii) rank collapse $\textit{in width}$, which occurs as the context length increases. Using Random Matrix Theory, we conduct a rigorous analysis that uncovers a spectral gap between the two largest singular values of the attention matrix as the cause of (iii), which in turn exacerbates (i) and (ii). Building on this insight, we propose a novel yet simple practical solution to mitigate rank collapse in width by removing the outlier eigenvalue(s). Our theoretical framework offers a fresh perspective on recent practical studies, such as (Ye et al., 2024; Ali et al., 2023), whose ad hoc solutions can now be interpreted as implicit efforts to address the spectral gap issue. This work provides valuable theoretical support for ongoing large-scale empirical research, bringing theory and practice one step closer in the understanding of transformers.
中摘: 注意力层是变压器这一当前最先进的神经网络架构的核心组件。由于基于softmax的注意力倾向于阻碍有效信息流动，因此正在探索其替代方案。即使在初始化时，这些随机网络中信号和梯度传播为何会出现病态问题仍然不甚明了，这些问题被称为（i）消失/爆炸梯度和（ii）深度秩坍塌，即当所有标记沿层收敛到单一表示时出现的问题。虽然深度秩坍塌自然地源于重复的矩阵乘法——这是各种架构中的常见模式——但我们发现softmax注意力层面临一个额外且未知的独特挑战：（iii）宽度秩坍塌，这在上下文长度增加时发生。利用随机矩阵理论，我们进行了一项严格的分析，揭示了注意力矩阵的两个最大奇异值之间的谱间隙是导致（iii）的原因，从而加剧了（i）和（ii）。基于这一见解，我们提出了一种新颖而简单的实用解决方案来缓解宽度秩坍塌，方法是移除异常特征值。我们的理论框架为最近的一些实际研究提供了新的视角，如（Ye等，2024；Ali等，2023），他们的临时性解决方案现在可以被解释为无意识地尝试解决谱间隙问题的努力。这项工作为正在进行的大规模经验研究提供了宝贵的理论支持，使理论与实践更接近于理解变压器。

[No.68] Permutation-based Rank Test in the Presence of Discretization and Application in Causal Discovery with Mixed Data
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于排列的秩检验在离散化存在情况下的应用及其在混合数据因果发现中的使用
类别: general machine learning->causality
作者: Xinshuai Dong;Ignavier Ng;Boyang Sun;Haoyue Dai;Guang-Yuan Hao;Shunxing Fan;Peter Spirtes;Yumou Qiu;Kun Zhang;
机构: Carnegie Mellon University;Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Iowa State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45077
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18990
英摘: Recent advances have shown that statistical tests for the rank of cross-covariance matrices play an important role in causal discovery. These rank tests include partial correlation tests as special cases and provide further graphical information about latent variables. Existing rank tests typically assume that all the continuous variables can be perfectly measured, and yet, in practice many variables can only be measured after discretization. For example, in psychometric studies, the continuous level of certain personality dimensions of a person can only be measured after being discretized into order-preserving options such as disagree, neutral, and agree. Motivated by this, we propose Mixed data Permutation-based Rank Test (MPRT), which properly controls the statistical errors even when some or all variables are discretized. Theoretically, we establish the exchangeability and estimate the asymptotic null distribution by permutations; as a consequence, MPRT can effectively control the Type I error in the presence of discretization while previous methods cannot. Empirically, our method is validated by extensive experiments on synthetic data and real-world data to demonstrate its effectiveness as well as applicability in causal discovery.
中摘: 近年来的研究表明，交叉协方差矩阵秩的统计检验在因果发现中扮演着重要角色。这些秩检验包括部分相关性检验作为特例，并且提供了关于潜在变量的进一步图形信息。现有的秩检验通常假设所有连续变量都能被完美测量，但在实践中，许多变量只能在离散化后进行测量。例如，在心理测量研究中，某些人格维度的连续水平只能通过有序选项（如不同意、中立和同意）进行离散化测量。受此启发，我们提出了基于排列的混合数据秩检验（MPRT），即使在一些或所有变量经过离散化处理的情况下，也能适当控制统计误差。理论上，我们建立了MPRT的交换性，并通过排列估计其渐近零分布；因此，MPRT能够在存在离散化的情况下有效控制第一类错误，而之前的检验方法则不能。实验上，我们的方法通过大量合成数据和真实世界数据的实验得到了验证，展示了其在因果发现中的有效性和适用性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.69] Bipartite Ranking From Multiple Labels: On Loss Versus Label Aggregation
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于多标签的二部排名：关于损失函数与标签聚合的关系
类别: general machine learning->supervised learning
作者: Michal Lukasik;Lin Chen;Harikrishna Narasimhan;Aditya Krishna Menon;Wittawat Jitkrittum;Felix X. Yu;Sashank J. Reddi;Gang Fu;Mohammadhossein Bateni;Sanjiv Kumar;
机构: Google;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44399
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11284
英摘: Bipartite ranking is a fundamental supervised learning problem, with the goal of learning a ranking over instances with maximal Area Under the ROC Curve (AUC) against a single binary target label. However, one may often observe multiple binary target labels, e.g., from distinct human annotators. How can one synthesize such labels into a single coherent ranking? In this work, we formally analyze two approaches to this problem -- loss aggregation and label aggregation -- by characterizing their Bayes-optimal solutions. We show that while both approaches can yield Pareto-optimal solutions, loss aggregation can exhibit label dictatorship: one can inadvertently (and undesirably) favor one label over others. This suggests that label aggregation can be preferable to loss aggregation, which we empirically verify.
中摘: 二部排名是一个基本的监督学习问题，目标是学习一种实例排序，使其在单一二元目标标签下的受试者操作特征曲线（ROC曲线）下的面积（AUC）最大化。然而，人们往往会观察到多个二元目标标签，例如来自不同的人类标注者。如何将这些标签综合为一个连贯的排序呢？在本文中，我们通过刻画它们的贝叶斯最优解，正式分析了这个问题的两种方法——损失聚合和标签聚合。我们展示了虽然这两种方法都可以产生帕累托最优解，但损失聚合可能会表现出标签独裁的现象：可能会无意地（且不希望地）偏好某个标签而忽视其他标签。这表明标签聚合可能比损失聚合更可取，并且我们在实验中验证了这一点。

作者: Zhengming Chen;Yewei Xia;Feng Xie;Jie Qiao;Zhifeng Hao;Ruichu Cai;Kun Zhang;

[No.71] IntLoRA: Integral Low-rank Adaptation of Quantized Diffusion Models
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: IntLoRA: 整体低秩量化扩散模型适应
类别: applications->computer vision
作者: Hang Guo;Yawei Li;Tao Dai;Shu-Tao Xia;Luca Benini;
机构: Tsinghua University;ETH Zurich;Shenzhen University;University of Bologna;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45657
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21759
英摘: Fine-tuning pre-trained diffusion models under limited budgets has gained great success. In particular, the recent advances that directly fine-tune the quantized weights using Low-rank Adaptation (LoRA) further reduces training costs. Despite these progress, we point out that existing adaptation recipes are not inference-efficient. Specifically, additional post-training quantization (PTQ) on tuned weights is needed during deployment, which results in noticeable performance drop when the bit-width is low. Based on this observation, we introduce IntLoRA, which adapts quantized diffusion models with integer-type low-rank parameters, to include inference efficiency during tuning. Specifically, IntLoRA enables pre-trained weights to remain quantized during training, facilitating fine-tuning on consumer-level GPUs. During inference, IntLoRA weights can be seamlessly merged into pre-trained weights to directly obtain quantized downstream weights without PTQ. Extensive experiments show our IntLoRA achieves significant speedup on both training and inference without losing performance.
中摘: 在有限预算下微调预训练扩散模型取得了巨大成功。特别是最近直接使用低秩适应（LoRA）对量化权重进行微调的进步，进一步降低了训练成本。尽管如此，我们指出现有的适应方法在推理效率方面并不理想。具体来说，在部署时需要额外进行后训练量化（PTQ），这在位宽较低时会导致明显的性能下降。基于这一观察，我们引入了IntLoRA，这是一种在微调过程中使用整数类型低秩参数来适应量化扩散模型的方法，旨在提高推理效率。具体而言，IntLoRA 允许预训练权重在训练过程中保持量化状态，从而便于在消费者级别的 GPU 上进行微调。在推理阶段，IntLoRA 权重可以无缝地与预训练权重合并，直接获得量化后的下游权重，无需进行 PTQ。广泛的实验表明，我们的 IntLoRA 在不牺牲性能的情况下，实现了训练和推理过程中的显著加速。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.72] From Low Rank Gradient Subspace Stabilization to Low-Rank Weights: Observations, Theories, and Applications
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 从低秩梯度子空间稳定化到低秩权重：观察、理论与应用
类别: deep learning->foundation models
作者: AJAY KUMAR JAISWAL;Yifan Wang;Lu Yin;Shiwei Liu;Runjin Chen;Jiawei Zhao;Ananth Grama;Yuandong Tian;Zhangyang Wang;
机构: Apple;University of Texas at Austin;Purdue University;University of Surrey;University of Oxford;;Meta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44501
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11239
英摘: Large Language Models' (LLMs) weight matrices can often be expressed in low-rank form with potential to relax memory and compute resource requirements. Unlike prior efforts that focus on developing novel matrix decompositions, in this work we study the non-uniform low-rank properties of weight matrices in LLMs through the lens of stabilizing gradient subspace. First, we provide a theoretical framework to understand the stabilization of gradient subspaces through Hessian analysis. Second, we empirically establish an important relationship between gradient dynamics and low-rank expressiveness of weight matrices. Our findings reveal that different LLM components exhibit varying levels of converged low-rank structures, necessitating variable rank reduction across them to minimize drop in performance due to compression. Drawing on this result, we present Weight Low-Rank Projection(WeLore) that unifies weight compression and memory-efficient fine-tuning into one, in a data-agnostic and one-shot manner. When used as a compression technique, WeLore categorizes weight matrices into Low-rank Components (LRCs) and Non-Low-rank Components (N-LRCs) and suitably encodes them for minimum performance loss. Our gradient dynamics perspective illustrates that LRCs tend to have better fine-tuning capabilities and their standalone fine-tuning can closely mimic and sometimes outperform the training loss trajectory and performance of full fine-tuning with notable memory and compute footprint reduction. Codes are available at https://github.com/VITA-Group/WeLore.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）的权重矩阵往往可以用低秩形式表示，并且有可能缓解内存和计算资源的需求。与以往主要集中在开发新的矩阵分解方法的努力不同，本研究通过梯度子空间稳定性的视角探讨了LLMs权重矩阵的非均匀低秩特性。首先，我们提供了一个理论框架来理解通过Hessian分析梯度子空间稳定性的机制。其次，我们实证地建立了梯度动态与权重矩阵低秩表达能力之间的关键关系。我们的发现揭示了不同的LLM组件表现出不同程度的收敛低秩结构，因此需要在它们之间进行可变的秩减少以最小化压缩导致的性能下降。基于这一结果，我们提出了Weight Low-Rank Projection（WeLore），这是一种统一权重压缩和高效内存微调的方法，且具有数据无关性和单次操作的特点。作为压缩技术时，WeLore将权重矩阵分为低秩组件（LRCs）和非低秩组件（N-LRCs），并适当地编码它们以最小化性能损失。从梯度动态的角度来看，LRCs通常具有更好的微调能力，它们独立的微调可以接近甚至超越完整微调的训练损失轨迹和性能，同时显著减少内存和计算需求。相关代码可在https://github.com/VITA-Group/WeLore获取。

[No.73] Pivoting Factorization: A Compact Meta Low-Rank Representation of Sparsity for Efficient Inference in Large Language Models
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 旋转因子分解：一种高效的大型语言模型推理中的紧凑元低秩稀疏表示
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Jialin Zhao;Yingtao Zhang;Carlo Vittorio Cannistraci;
机构: Tsinghua University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46433
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.19090
英摘: The rapid growth of Large Language Models has driven demand for effective model compression techniques to reduce memory and computation costs. Low-rank pruning has gained attention for its GPU compatibility across all densities. However, low-rank pruning struggles to match the performance of semi-structured pruning, often doubling perplexity at similar densities. In this paper, we propose Pivoting Factorization (PIFA), a novel lossless meta low-rank representation that unsupervisedly learns a compact form of any low-rank representation, effectively eliminating redundant information. PIFA identifies pivot rows (linearly independent rows) and expresses non-pivot rows as linear combinations, achieving 24.2% additional memory savings and 24.6% faster inference over low-rank layers at rank = 50% of dimension. To mitigate the performance degradation caused by low-rank pruning, we introduce a novel, retraining-free reconstruction method that minimizes error accumulation (M). MPIFA, combining M and PIFA into an end-to-end framework, significantly outperforms existing low-rank pruning methods, and achieves performance comparable to semi-structured pruning, while surpassing it in GPU efficiency and compatibility. Our code is available at https://github.com/biomedical-cybernetics/pivoting-factorization.
中摘: 大型语言模型的迅速增长推动了有效模型压缩技术的需求，以减少内存和计算成本。低秩剪枝因其在所有密度下的GPU兼容性而受到关注。然而，低秩剪枝往往难以与半结构化剪枝的性能相匹配，在相似的密度下，其困惑度常常翻倍。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的无损元低秩表示方法——Pivoting Factorization（PIFA），它能够在无需监督的情况下学习任何低秩表示的紧凑形式，有效地消除冗余信息。PIFA识别出枢行（线性独立的行）并用线性组合来表达非枢行，从而在维度为50%时实现了额外24.2%的内存节省和24.6%更快的推理速度。为了减轻低秩剪枝引起的性能下降，我们引入了一种全新的、无需重新训练的重建方法（M），该方法能够最小化误差累积。将M和PIFA结合进一个端到端框架中的MPIFA显著优于现有的低秩剪枝方法，并在GPU效率和兼容性方面超越了半结构化剪枝，同时达到了与半结构化剪枝相当的性能。我们的代码可在https://github.com/biomedical-cybernetics/pivoting-factorization获得。

[No.77] Rank-One Modified Value Iteration
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 一维修改的价值迭代
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Arman Sharifi Kolarijani;Tolga Ok;Peyman Mohajerin Esfahani;Mohamad Amin Sharifi Kolarijani;
机构: Delft University of Technology;University of Toronto;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46510
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01828
英摘: In this paper, we provide a novel algorithm for solving planning and learning problems of Markov decision processes. The proposed algorithm follows a policy iteration-type update by using a rank-one approximation of the transition probability matrix in the policy evaluation step. This rank-one approximation is closely related to the stationary distribution of the corresponding transition probability matrix, which is approximated using the power method. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of the proposed algorithm to optimal (action-)value function with the same rate and computational complexity as the value iteration algorithm in the planning problem and as the Q-learning algorithm in the learning problem. Through our extensive numerical simulations, however, we show that the proposed algorithm consistently outperforms first-order algorithms and their accelerated versions for both planning and learning problems.
中摘: 在本文中，我们提供了一种新的算法，用于解决马尔可夫决策过程的规划和学习问题。所提出的算法在策略评估步骤中使用了转移概率矩阵的秩一近似，并遵循策略迭代类型更新。这种秩一近似与相应转移概率矩阵的平稳分布密切相关，而平稳分布则是通过幂方法进行近似计算的。我们为所提出的算法提供了理论上的收敛保证，在规划问题中与值迭代算法具有相同的收敛率和计算复杂度，在学习问题中则与Q学习算法具有相同的收敛率和计算复杂度。然而，通过我们广泛的数值模拟表明，所提出的算法在规划和学习问题中始终优于一阶算法及其加速版本。

[No.79] Does Low Rank Adaptation Lead to Lower Robustness against Training-Time Attacks?
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩适应会导致对抗训练时间攻击的鲁棒性降低吗？
类别: social aspects->security
作者: Zi Liang;Haibo Hu;Qingqing Ye;Yaxin Xiao;RongHua Li;
机构: Hong Kong Polytechnic University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44219
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12871
英摘: Low rank adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a prominent technique for fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) thanks to its superb efficiency gains over previous methods. While extensive studies have examined the performance and structural properties of LoRA, its behavior upon training-time attacks remain underexplored, posing significant security risks. In this paper, we theoretically investigate the security implications of LoRA's low-rank structure during fine-tuning, in the context of its robustness against data poisoning and backdoor attacks. We propose an analytical framework that models LoRA's training dynamics, employs the neural tangent kernel to simplify the analysis of the training process, and applies information theory to establish connections between LoRA's low rank structure and its vulnerability against training-time attacks. Our analysis indicates that LoRA exhibits better robustness to backdoor attacks than full fine-tuning, while becomes more vulnerable to untargeted data poisoning due to its over-simplified information geometry. Extensive experimental evaluations have corroborated our theoretical findings.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）已成为一种突出的技术，用于微调大型语言模型（LLMs），这得益于它在效率方面相较于之前的方法取得了显著提升。尽管有大量的研究已经探讨了LoRA的性能和结构特性，但在训练时间攻击下的行为仍然鲜有研究，从而带来了重大的安全风险。在本文中，我们理论性地研究了LoRA在微调过程中低秩结构的安全影响，特别是在其对数据投毒和后门攻击的鲁棒性方面的表现。我们提出了一种分析框架，该框架模拟了LoRA的训练动态，利用神经 tangent 核简化了训练过程的分析，并应用信息论建立了LoRA低秩结构与其在训练时间攻击下脆弱性的联系。我们的分析表明，LoRA在抵抗后门攻击方面表现出更好的鲁棒性，但因其过于简化的信息几何结构，更容易受到无目标数据投毒的攻击。广泛的实验评估证实了我们的理论发现。

[No.80] LoRA Training Provably Converges to a Low-Rank Global Minimum Or It Fails Loudly (But it Probably Won't Fail)

作者: Junsu Kim;Jaeyeon Kim;Ernest K. Ryu;

[No.81] Ehrenfeucht-Haussler Rank and Chain of Thought
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 厄伦费尔特-豪斯勒秩和思维链
类别: theory->learning theory
作者: Pablo Barcelo;Alexander Kozachinskiy;Tomasz Steifer;
机构: Pontificia Universidad Católica;Pontificia Universidad Catolica de Chile;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46252
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.12997
英摘: The notion of \emph{rank} of a Boolean function has been a cornerstone in PAC learning theory, enabling quasipolynomial-time learning algorithms for polynomial-size decision trees. We present a novel characterization of rank, grounded in the well-known Transformer architecture. We show that the rank of a function $f$ corresponds to the minimum number of \emph{Chain of Thought} (CoT) steps required by a single-layer Transformer with hard attention to compute $f$. Based on this characterization we establish tight bounds on the number of CoT steps required for specific problems, showing that \(\ell\)-fold function composition necessitates exactly \(\ell\) CoT steps. Furthermore, we analyze the problem of identifying the position of the \(k\)-th occurrence of 1 in a Boolean sequence, proving that it requires \(k\) CoT steps. Finally, we introduce the notion of the multi-head rank that captures multi-head single-layer transformers, and perform the analysis of PAC-learnability of the classes of functions with bounded multi-head rank.
中摘: 布尔函数的秩概念在PAC学习理论中一直是一个基石，使得对于多项式大小的决策树能够实现准多项式时间的学习算法。我们提出了一种基于广为人知的Transformer架构的新颖秩刻画。我们证明了一个函数$f$的秩对应于单层Transformer（具有硬注意力机制）计算$f$所需的最小链式思维（Chain of Thought, CoT）步骤数。基于这种刻画，我们建立了特定问题所需CoT步骤数的紧界，表明$\ell$次函数复合恰好需要$\ell$个CoT步骤。此外，我们分析了识别布尔序列中第$k$个1的位置的问题，证明了它需要$k$个CoT步骤。最后，我们引入了多头秩的概念，以捕捉多头单层Transformer，并对具有有界多头秩的函数类的PAC可学习性进行了分析。

[No.82] Ranking with Multiple Oracles: From Weak to Strong Stochastic Transitivity
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 多 oracle 排序：从弱到强随机传递性

[No.83] Universal Biological Sequence Reranking for Improved De Novo Peptide Sequencing
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 通用生物序列重新排序以提高从头肽测序
类别: applications->everything else
作者: Zijie Qiu;Jiaqi Wei;Xiang Zhang;Sheng Xu;Kai Zou;Zhi Jin;ZhiQiang Gao;Nanqing Dong;Siqi Sun;
机构: Fudan University;Zhejiang University;;Shanghai AI Laboratory;Netmind;Protagolabs;Shanghai Innovation Institute;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45758
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17552
英摘: De novo peptide sequencing is a critical task in proteomics. However, the performance of current deep learning-based methods is limited by the inherent complexity of mass spectrometry data and the heterogeneous distribution of noise signals, leading to data-specific biases. We present RankNovo, the first deep reranking framework that enhances de novo peptide sequencing by leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple sequencing models. RankNovo employs a list-wise reranking approach, modeling candidate peptides as multiple sequence alignments and utilizing axial attention to extract informative features across candidates. Additionally, we introduce two new metrics, PMD (Peptide Mass Deviation) and RMD (residual Mass Deviation), which offer delicate supervision by quantifying mass differences between peptides at both the sequence and residue levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RankNovo not only surpasses its base models used to generate training candidates for reranking pre-training, but also sets a new state-of-the-art benchmark. Moreover, RankNovo exhibits strong zero-shot generalization to unseen models whose generations were not exposed during training, highlighting its robustness and potential as a universal reranking framework for peptide sequencing. Our work presents a novel reranking strategy that fundamentally challenges existing single-model paradigms and advances the frontier of accurate de novo sequencing. Our source code is provided on GitHub.
中摘: 从头肽测序是蛋白质组学中的关键任务。然而，当前基于深度学习的方法由于质谱数据固有的复杂性和噪声信号的异质分布而受到限制，导致数据特定的偏差。我们提出了RankNovo，这是首个通过利用多种测序模型互补优势来增强从头肽测序的深度重排框架。RankNovo采用列表级重排方法，将候选肽视为多个序列对齐，并利用轴向注意力提取跨候选者的特征信息。此外，我们引入了两个新的度量标准，PMD（肽质量偏差）和RMD（残差质量偏差），这些标准通过在序列和残基级别量化肽之间的质量差异提供了精细的监督。大量实验表明，RankNovo 不仅超越了用于生成重排预训练候选模型的基础模型，还设定了准确从头测序的新基准。此外，RankNovo 在未见过的生成模型上表现出强大的零样本泛化能力，这些模型在训练过程中并未暴露其生成物，这突显了其鲁棒性以及作为通用从头肽测序重排框架的潜力。我们的工作提出了一种新颖的重排策略，从根本上挑战现有的单模型范式，并推动了准确从头测序的前沿。我们的源代码已在GitHub上提供。

[No.84] Re-ranking Reasoning Context with Tree Search Makes Large Vision-Language Models Stronger
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于树搜索的重排序推理使大型视觉-语言模型更加强大
类别: deep learning->algorithms
作者: Qi Yang;Chenghao Zhang;Lubin Fan;Kun Ding;Jieping Ye;Shiming Xiang;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;Alibaba Group;Institute of Automation;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46017
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07785
英摘: Recent advancements in Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) have significantly improved performance in Visual Question Answering (VQA) tasks through multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). However, existing methods still face challenges, such as the scarcity of knowledge with reasoning examples and erratic responses from retrieved knowledge. To address these issues, in this study, we propose a multimodal RAG framework, termed RCTS, which enhances LVLMs by constructing a Reasoning Context-enriched knowledge base and a Tree Search re-ranking method. Specifically, we introduce a self-consistent evaluation mechanism to enrich the knowledge base with intrinsic reasoning patterns. We further propose a Monte Carlo Tree Search with Heuristic Rewards (MCTS-HR) to prioritize the most relevant examples. This ensures that LVLMs can leverage high-quality contextual reasoning for better and more consistent responses. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple VQA datasets, significantly outperforming In-Context Learning (ICL) and Vanilla-RAG methods. It highlights the effectiveness of our knowledge base and re-ranking method in improving LVLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/yannqi/RCTS-RAG.
中摘: 最近在大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）方面的进展通过多模态检索增强生成（RAG）显著提升了视觉问答（VQA）任务的表现。然而，现有方法仍然面临一些挑战，如缺乏包含推理示例的知识以及检索知识时出现的不一致响应。为了解决这些问题，在这项研究中，我们提出了一种名为RCTS的多模态RAG框架，该框架通过构建一个富含推理背景的知识库和一种树搜索重排序方法来增强LVLMs。具体而言，我们引入了一种自一致性评估机制，以内在的推理模式丰富知识库。此外，我们提出了带有启发式奖励的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS-HR），以优先选择最相关的示例。这确保了LVLMs能够利用高质量的上下文推理，从而提供更好且更一致的回答。广泛的实验表明，我们的框架在多个VQA数据集上达到了最先进的性能，显著优于上下文学习（ICL）和纯RAG方法。这突显了我们知识库和重排序方法对提升LVLMs的有效性。我们的代码可在https://github.com/yannqi/RCTS-RAG获取。

[No.85] Come Together, But Not Right Now: A Progressive Strategy to Boost Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 一起来，但不是现在：一种渐进策略以提升低等级适应能力
类别: deep learning->everything else
作者: Zhan Zhuang;Xiequn Wang;Wei Li;Yulong Zhang;Qiushi Huang;Shuhao Chen;Xuehao Wang;Yanbin Wei;Yuhe Nie;Kede Ma;Yu Zhang;Ying Wei;
机构: City University of Hong Kong;South University of Science and Technology of China;;Zhejiang University;University of Surrey;Southern University of Science and Technology;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;New York University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44836
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05713
英摘: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a leading parameter-efficient fine-tuning technique for adapting large foundation models, yet it often locks adapters into suboptimal minima near their initialization. This hampers model generalization and limits downstream operators such as adapter merging and pruning. Here, we propose CoTo, a progressive training strategy that gradually increases adapters' activation probability over the course of fine-tuning. By stochastically deactivating adapters, CoTo encourages more balanced optimization and broader exploration of the loss landscape. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that CoTo promotes layer-wise dropout stability and linear mode connectivity, and we adopt a cooperative-game approach to quantify each adapter's marginal contribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoTo consistently boosts single-task performance, enhances multi-task merging accuracy, improves pruning robustness, and reduces training overhead, all while remaining compatible with diverse LoRA variants. Code is available at https://github.com/zwebzone/coto.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）已成为一种参数高效微调技术，用于适应大型基础模型，但它往往会将适配器锁定在初始化附近的次优极小值。这阻碍了模型泛化，并限制了下游操作，如适配器合并和剪枝。在此，我们提出了一种渐进式训练策略CoTo，在微调过程中逐渐增加适配器的激活概率。通过随机禁用适配器，CoTo鼓励更平衡的优化和更广泛的损失景观探索。我们提供了一个理论分析，表明CoTo促进了逐层的dropout稳定性以及线性模式连通性，并采用合作博弈方法量化每个适配器的边际贡献。大量实验表明，CoTo一致地提升了单任务性能、增强了多任务合并准确性、提高了剪枝鲁棒性，并减少了训练开销，同时仍与各种LoRA变体兼容。代码可在https://github.com/zwebzone/coto获取。

[No.86] ResQ: Mixed-Precision Quantization of Large Language Models with Low-Rank Residuals
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: ResQ：具有低秩残差的大型语言模型混合精度量化
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Utkarsh Saxena;Sayeh Sharify;Kaushik Roy;Xin Wang;
机构: Purdue University;d-Matrix;d-Matrix Corp.;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46466
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14363
英摘: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of large language models (LLMs) holds the promise in reducing the prohibitive computational cost at inference time. Quantization of all weight, activation and key-value (KV) cache tensors to 4-bit without significantly degrading generalizability is challenging, due to the high quantization error caused by extreme outliers in activations. To tackle this problem, we propose ResQ, a PTQ method that pushes further the state-of-the-art. By means of principal component analysis (PCA), it identifies a low-rank subspace (in practice 1/8 of the hidden dimension) in which activation variances are highest, and keep the coefficients within this subspace in high precision, e.g. 8-bit, while quantizing the rest to 4-bit. Within each subspace, invariant random rotation is applied to further suppress outliers. We show that this is a provably optimal mixed precision quantization scheme that minimizes error. With the Llama and Qwen2.5 families of models, we demonstrate that ResQ outperforms recent uniform and mixed precision PTQ methods on a variety of benchmarks, achieving up to 33\% lower perplexity on Wikitext than the next best method SpinQuant, and upto 3\times speedup over 16-bit baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/utkarsh-dmx/project-resq.
中摘: 大语言模型（LLMs）在训练后的量化（PTQ）方面有望减少推理时的高昂计算成本。将所有权重、激活和关键值（KV）缓存张量量化为4位而不显著降低泛化能力极具挑战性，这是因为激活中的极端异常值导致了高量化误差。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了ResQ，这是一种进一步推动当前最佳水平的PTQ方法。通过主成分分析（PCA），它识别出一个低秩子空间（实际上占隐藏维度的1/8），在这个子空间中激活的方差最高，并保持该子空间内的系数以高精度表示，例如8位，而将其余部分量化为4位。在每个子空间内应用不变随机旋转，以进一步抑制异常值。我们证明了这是理论上最优的混合精度量化方案，能够最小化误差。在Llama和Qwen2.5系列模型上，我们展示了ResQ在多种基准测试中优于最近的均匀和混合精度PTQ方法，与SpinQuant相比，在Wikitext数据集上的困惑度降低了高达33%，并且相对于16位基线实现了高达3倍的速度提升。代码可在https://github.com/utkarsh-dmx/project-resq获取。

[No.88] Ranked Entropy Minimization for Continual Test-Time Adaptation
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 持续测试时自适应中的排序熵最小化
类别: deep learning->robustness
作者: Jisu Han;Jaemin Na;Wonjun Hwang;
机构: Ajou University;Tech. Innovation Group, Korea Telecom;Korea University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44208
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16441
英摘: Test-time adaptation aims to adapt to realistic environments in an online manner by learning during test time. Entropy minimization has emerged as a principal strategy for test-time adaptation due to its efficiency and adaptability. Nevertheless, it remains underexplored in continual test-time adaptation, where stability is more important. We observe that the entropy minimization method often suffers from model collapse, where the model converges to predicting a single class for all images due to a trivial solution. We propose ranked entropy minimization to mitigate the stability problem of the entropy minimization method and extend its applicability to continuous scenarios. Our approach explicitly structures the prediction difficulty through a progressive masking strategy. Specifically, it gradually aligns the model's probability distributions across different levels of prediction difficulty while preserving the rank order of entropy. The proposed method is extensively evaluated across various benchmarks, demonstrating its effectiveness through empirical results. Our code is available at https://github.com/pilsHan/rem
中摘: 测试时适应旨在通过在测试过程中学习来在线适应现实环境。由于其高效性和适应性，熵最小化已成为测试时适应的主要策略。然而，在持续测试时适应场景中，稳定性更为重要，这一领域仍被广泛探索。我们发现，熵最小化方法常常会遭受模型崩溃的问题，即模型由于一个简单的解决方案而收敛于对所有图像预测单一类别。为此，我们提出了一种排序熵最小化方法，以缓解熵最小化方法的稳定性问题，并将其适用范围扩展到连续场景中。我们的方法通过渐进式遮罩策略明确地结构化了预测难度。具体来说，它逐步调整模型在不同预测难度级别上的概率分布，同时保持熵的排名顺序。我们提出的这种方法在各种基准上进行了广泛评估，并通过实验证明了其有效性。我们的代码可在https://github.com/pilsHan/rem 获取。

[No.91] The Panaceas for Improving Low-Rank Decomposition in Communication-Efficient Federated Learning
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 提高通信效率联邦学习中低秩分解性能的方法
类别: optimization->large scale parallel and distributed
作者: Shiwei Li;Xiandi Luo;Haozhao Wang;Xing Tang;Shijie Xu;weihongluo;Yuhua Li;xiuqiang He;Ruixuan Li;

英摘: To improve the training efficiency of federated learning (FL), previous research has employed low-rank decomposition techniques to reduce communication overhead. In this paper, we seek to enhance the performance of these low-rank decomposition methods. Specifically, we focus on three key issues related to decomposition in FL: what to decompose, how to decompose, and how to aggregate. Subsequently, we introduce three novel techniques: Model Update Decomposition (MUD), Block-wise Kronecker Decomposition (BKD), and Aggregation-Aware Decomposition (AAD), each targeting a specific issue. [No.92] Guarantees of a Preconditioned Subgradient Algorithm for Overparameterized Asymmetric Low-rank Matrix Recovery
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 预条件子梯度算法在过度参数化非对称低秩矩阵恢复中的保证
类别: optimization->nonconvex
作者: Paris Giampouras;HanQin Cai;Rene Vidal;
机构: University of Warwick;University of Central Florida;University of Pennsylvania;Amazon;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45824
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16826
英摘: In this paper, we focus on a matrix factorization-based approach to recover low-rank {\it asymmetric} matrices from corrupted measurements. We propose an {\it Overparameterized Preconditioned Subgradient Algorithm (OPSA)} and provide, for the first time in the literature, linear convergence rates independent of the rank of the sought asymmetric matrix in the presence of gross corruptions. Our work goes beyond existing results in preconditioned-type approaches addressing their current limitation, i.e., the lack of convergence guarantees in the case of {\it asymmetric matrices of unknown rank}. By applying our approach to (robust) matrix sensing, we highlight its merits when the measurement operator satisfies a mixed-norm restricted isometry property. Lastly, we present extensive numerical experiments that validate our theoretical results and demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach for different levels of overparameterization and outlier corruptions.
中摘: 在本文中，我们关注一种基于矩阵分解的方法，用于从受污染的测量值中恢复低秩的非对称矩阵。我们提出了一种“过参数化预条件子次梯度算法（OPSA）”，并在文献中首次提供了独立于所求非对称矩阵秩的线性收敛速率，特别是在存在严重污染的情况下。我们的工作超越了现有预条件子类型方法的限制，解决了它们目前存在的问题，即在处理未知秩的非对称矩阵时缺乏收敛保证。通过将我们的方法应用于稳健矩阵感知，我们强调了当测量算子满足混合范数受限等距性质时，该方法的优点。最后，我们展示了广泛的数值实验，这些实验验证了我们的理论结果，并证明了我们的方法在不同程度的过参数化和离群值污染情况下的有效性。

[No.94] OrthoRank: Token Selection via Sink Token Orthogonality for Efficient LLM inference
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 正交排名：基于Sink token正交性的高效LLM推理中的词元选择
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Seungjun Shin;Jaehoon Oh;Dokwan Oh;
机构: Samsung;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46443
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03865
英摘: Attention mechanisms are central to the success of large language models (LLMs), enabling them to capture intricate token dependencies and implicitly assign importance to each token. Recent studies have revealed the sink token, which receives disproportionately high attention despite their limited semantic role. In this paper, we first expand the relationship between the sink token and other tokens, moving beyond attention to explore their similarity in hidden states, considering the layer depth. We observe that as the layers get deeper, the cosine similarity between the normalized hidden states of the sink token and those of other tokens increases, and that the normalized hidden states of the sink token exhibit negligible changes. These imply that other tokens consistently are directed toward the sink token throughout the layers. Next, we propose a dynamic token selection method, called OrthoRank, using these findings to select important tokens. Specifically, in a certain layer, we define token importance by the speed at which the token moves toward the sink token. This is converted into orthogonality with the sink token, meaning that tokens that are more orthogonal to the sink token are assigned greater importance. Finally, through extensive experiments, we demonstrated that our method results in lower perplexity and higher zero-shot accuracy compared to layer pruning methods at the same sparsity ratio with comparable throughput, while also achieving superior performance on LongBench.
中摘: 注意力机制对于大型语言模型（LLMs）的成功至关重要，能够捕捉复杂的标记依赖关系并隐式地赋予每个标记重要性。最近的研究揭示了“汇合标记”这一概念，尽管其在语义角色上作用有限，却收到了不成比例的高关注。在这篇论文中，我们首先扩展了汇合标记与其他标记之间的关系，超越单纯的注意力机制，探索它们在隐藏状态中的相似性，并考虑了层数深度。我们观察到，随着层数加深，汇合标记和其它标记的归一化隐藏状态之间的余弦相似度增加，而汇合标记的归一化隐藏状态几乎没有变化。这表明在整个层中，其它标记始终被导向汇合标记。接下来，我们提出了一种动态标记选择方法，称为OrthoRank，利用这些发现来选择重要的标记。具体而言，在某一特定层中，我们定义标记的重要性为该标记向汇合标记移动的速度。我们将这种速度转化为与汇合标记的正交性，意味着与汇合标记更正交的标记会被赋予更高的重要性。最后，通过大量的实验，我们证明了我们的方法在相同的稀疏比下比层剪枝方法具有更低的困惑度和更高的零样本准确率，同时在LongBench上的性能也更为优越。

[No.96] SLiM: One-shot Quantization and Sparsity with Low-rank Approximation for LLM Weight Compression
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: SLiM：基于低秩逼近的一次性量化和稀疏化以压缩LLM权重
类别: deep learning
作者: Mohammad Mozaffari;Amir Yazdanbakhsh;Maryam Mehri Dehnavi;
机构: University of Toronto;Google;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46479
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09615
英摘: Conventional model compression techniques for LLMs address high memory consumption and slow inference challenges but typically require computationally expensive retraining to preserve accuracy. In contrast, one-shot compression methods eliminate retraining cost, but struggle to achieve accuracy comparable to dense models. This paper presents SLIM, a new one-shot compression framework that holistically integrates hardware-friendly quantization, sparsity, and low-rank approximation into a unified process. First, we formulate the quantization process using a probabilistic approach (SLIM-Quant) that enables us to apply uniform quantization. Then, we use an existing one-shot pruning method to apply semi-structured sparsity on top of the quantized weights. Finally, to compensate for the introduced aggregated quantization and sparsity error, we use a novel saliency function with unique invertible and additive features that enables us to mathematically compute the value of low-rank adapters. SLIM improves model accuracy by up to 5.66% (LLaMA-2-7B) for 2:4 sparsity with 4-bit weight quantization, outperforming prior methods. Models compressed with SLIM achieve up to 4.3x and 3.8x on Nvidia RTX3060 and A100 GPUs, respectively. Additionally, they achieve up to 0.23x end-to-end memory reduction in comparison to their dense counterparts. We also propose an optional PEFT recipe that further improves accuracy by up to 1.66% (LLaMA-2-13B) compared to SLIM without fine-tuning.
中摘: 常规的大语言模型（LLM）压缩技术可以解决高内存消耗和推理速度慢的问题，但通常需要昂贵的重新训练来保持准确性。相比之下，一次性压缩方法可以消除重新训练的成本，但在实现与密集模型相当的准确性方面存在困难。本文介绍了一种名为SLIM的新的一次性压缩框架，该框架将硬件友好的量化、稀疏性和低秩近似综合集成到一个统一的过程中。首先，我们使用概率方法（SLIM-Quant）来制定量化过程，这使我们能够应用均匀量化。然后，我们利用现有的一次性剪枝方法，在量化权重的基础上应用半结构化稀疏性。最后，为了补偿引入的聚合量化和稀疏性误差，我们使用了一个具有独特可逆性和加性特征的新型显著性函数，这使我们能够数学地计算低秩适配器的价值。SLIM在2:4稀疏性情况下，通过4位权重量化提高了模型准确性最多5.66%（LLaMA-2-7B），超过了先前的方法。使用SLIM压缩的模型在Nvidia RTX3060和A100 GPU上分别实现了最多4.3倍和3.8倍的内存减少。此外，它们相对于其密集版本实现了最高0.23倍的端到端内存减少。我们还提出了一种可选的PEFT配方，进一步提高了模型准确性，与不进行微调的SLIM相比，在LLaMA-2-13B模型上最多提高了1.66%。

[No.98] Ranked from Within: Ranking Large Multimodal Models Without Labels
来源: ICML 2025
命中: rank
标题: 来自内部排名：无需标签排名大型多模态模型
类别: social aspects->robustness
作者: Weijie Tu;Weijian Deng;Dylan Campbell;Yu Yao;Jiyang Zheng;Tom Gedeon;Tongliang Liu;
机构: Australian National University;University of Sydney;Curtin University;RIKEN;Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45911
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06461
英摘: Can the relative performance of a pre-trained large multimodal model (LMM) be predicted without access to labels? As LMMs proliferate, it becomes increasingly important to develop efficient ways to choose between them when faced with new data or tasks. The usual approach does the equivalent of giving the models an exam and marking them. We opt to avoid marking and the associated labor of determining the ground-truth answers. Instead, we explore other signals elicited and ascertain how well the models know their own limits, evaluating the effectiveness of these signals at unsupervised model ranking. We evaluate $47$ state-of-the-art LMMs (\eg, LLaVA) across $9$ visual question answering benchmarks, analyzing how well uncertainty-based metrics can predict relative model performance. Our findings show that uncertainty scores derived from softmax distributions provide a robust and consistent basis for ranking models across various tasks. This facilitates the ranking of LMMs on unlabeled data, providing a practical approach for selecting models for diverse target domains without requiring manual annotation.
中摘: 预训练的大规模多模态模型（LMM）的相对性能是否可以在没有标签的情况下进行预测？随着LMMs的应用越来越广泛，当面对新数据或新任务时，开发高效的方法来选择合适的模型变得尤为重要。通常的做法相当于给模型进行考试并打分。我们选择避免评分以及确定正确答案所伴随的劳动。相反，我们探索其他信号，并确定这些信号能否反映出模型对自己的限制有多了解，评估这些信号在无监督模型排名中的有效性。我们在9个视觉问答基准测试中评估了47个最先进的LMMs（例如，LLaVA），分析基于不确定性度量的相对模型性能预测能力。我们的研究发现，从softmax分布中派生的不确定性分数为各种任务下的模型排名提供了稳健且一致的基础。这使得在未标记数据上对LMMs进行排名成为可能，提供了一种无需人工标注即可为不同目标领域选择模型的实际方法。

[No.100] Secant Line Search for Frank-Wolfe Algorithms
来源: ICML 2025

类别: optimization->convex

[No.101] Spectral Perturbation Bounds for Low-Rank Approximation with Applications to Privacy
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩逼近的谱扰动界及其在隐私保护中的应用
类别: theory
作者: Phuc Tran;Van Vu;Nisheeth K. Vishnoi;
机构: Yale University;;
类型: Oral
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=F0JzotXYgC
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25670
英摘: A central challenge in machine learning is to understand how noise or measurement errors affect low-rank approximations, particularly in the spectral norm. This question is especially important in differentially private low-rank approximation, where one aims to preserve the top-$p$ structure of a data-derived matrix while ensuring privacy. Prior work often analyzes Frobenius norm error or changes in reconstruction quality, but these metrics can over- or under-estimate true subspace distortion. The spectral norm, by contrast, captures worst-case directional error and provides the strongest utility guarantees. We establish new high-probability spectral-norm perturbation bounds for symmetric matrices that refine the classical Eckart--Young--Mirsky theorem and explicitly capture interactions between a matrix $A \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times n}$ and an arbitrary symmetric perturbation $E$. Under mild eigengap and norm conditions, our bounds yield sharp estimates for $\|(A + E)_p - A_p\|$, where $A_p$ is the best rank-$p$ approximation of $A$, with improvements of up to a factor of $\sqrt{n}$. As an application, we derive improved utility guarantees for differentially private PCA, resolving an open problem in the literature. Our analysis relies on a novel contour bootstrapping method from complex analysis and extends it to a broad class of spectral functionals, including polynomials and matrix exponentials. Empirical results on real-world datasets confirm that our bounds closely track the actual spectral error under diverse perturbation regimes.
中摘: 机器学习中的一个核心挑战是如何理解噪声或测量误差对低秩近似的影响，特别是在谱范数方面。这个问题在差分隐私的低秩近似中尤为重要，其中的目标是在保护数据矩阵的前 $p$ 个结构的同时保持其隐私性。以往的研究通常分析弗罗贝尼乌斯范数误差或重构质量的变化，但这些度量可能会过高或过低估计真实的子空间扭曲。相比之下，谱范数捕捉最坏情况的方向误差，并提供最强的实用性保证。我们建立了新的高概率谱范数扰动界，改进了经典的厄尔夫-扬-米尔斯基定理，并明确地捕捉了矩阵 $A \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times n}$ 和任意对称扰动 $E$ 之间的相互作用。在轻微的特征间隙和范数条件下，我们的界提供了对 $\|(A + E)_p - A_p\|$ 的精确估计，其中 $A_p$ 是 $A$ 的最佳秩-$p$ 近似，误差改善最多可达 $\sqrt{n}$ 倍。作为应用，我们推导出了改进的差分隐私主成分分析（PCA）的实用性保证，解决了文献中的一个开放问题。我们的分析依赖于复分析中的一个新颖的轮廓自助方法，并将其扩展到广泛的谱函数类，包括多项式和矩阵指数函数。实世界数据集上的实验结果证实，在各种扰动条件下，我们的界紧密跟踪实际的谱误差。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.102] FlyLoRA: Boosting Task Decoupling and Parameter Efficiency via Implicit Rank-Wise Mixture-of-Experts
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: FlyLoRA：通过隐式秩别混合专家提升任务解耦和参数效率
类别: deep learning
作者: Heming Zou;Yunliang Zang;Wutong Xu;Yao Zhu;Xiangyang Ji;
机构: Tsinghua University;Tianjin University;Zhejiang University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=nGQLYn13Xf
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08396
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method for foundation models, but it suffers from parameter interference, resulting in suboptimal performance. Although Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-based LoRA variants show promise in mitigating intra-task correlations in single-task instruction tuning, they introduce additional router parameters and remain ineffective in multi-task model merging where inter-task interference arises. Inspired by the fly olfactory circuit, we propose FlyLoRA, an implicit MoE-based LoRA variant that introduces: (1) rank-wise expert activation in the up-projection matrix, and (2) an implicit router that unifies expert routing and down-projection, where a frozen sparse random projection matrix replaces the traditional dense trainable version. This design resolves the trade-off between intra-task decorrelation and computational efficiency by eliminating the need for an explicit router, while inherently mitigating inter-task interference due to the orthogonality property of random matrices. Extensive experiments across four domains -- general knowledge understanding, scientific question answering, mathematical reasoning, and code generation -- demonstrate consistent performance improvements over existing methods. Beyond empirical gains, FlyLoRA highlights how biological structures can inspire innovations in AI technologies. Code is available at https://github.com/gfyddha/FlyLoRA.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）是一种广泛使用的参数高效微调方法，适用于基础模型，但会遭受参数干扰的问题，导致性能不佳。尽管基于Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）的LoRA变体在单任务指令微调中显示出减轻任务内相关性的潜力，但在多任务模型合并中，由于任务间干扰的出现，它们仍然无效。受苍蝇嗅觉电路的启发，我们提出了一种隐式的MoE基LoRA变体FlyLoRA，引入了以下两点：(1) 在上投影矩阵中的秩别化专家激活，以及(2) 一个隐式路由器，将专家路由和下投影统一起来，其中冻结的稀疏随机投影矩阵替代了传统的密集可训练版本。这种设计通过消除显式路由器的需求解决了任务内去相关性和计算效率之间的权衡问题，并且由于随机矩阵的正交性特性，内在地缓解了任务间的干扰。在四个领域——通用知识理解、科学问答、数学推理和代码生成——进行的大量实验表明，FlyLoRA在现有方法上一致地实现了性能改进。除了实证收益外，FlyLoRA 还展示了生物结构如何启发人工智能技术的创新。代码可在 https://github.com/gfyddha/FlyLoRA 获取。

[No.103] COALA: Numerically Stable and Efficient Framework for Context-Aware Low-Rank Approximation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: COALA：一种数值稳定且高效的上下文感知低秩逼近框架

英摘: Recent studies suggest that context-aware low-rank approximation is a useful tool for compression and fine-tuning of modern large-scale neural networks. In this type of approximation, a norm is weighted by a matrix of input activations, significantly improving metrics over the unweighted case. Nevertheless, existing methods for neural networks suffer from numerical instabilities due to their reliance on classical formulas involving explicit Gram matrix computation and their subsequent inversion. We demonstrate that this can degrade the approximation quality or cause numerically singular matrices.
  To address these limitations, we propose a novel inversion-free regularized framework that is based entirely on stable decompositions and overcomes the numerical pitfalls of prior art. Our method can handle possible challenging scenarios: (1) when calibration matrices exceed GPU memory capacity, (2) when input activation matrices are nearly singular, and even (3) when insufficient data prevents unique approximation. For the latter, we prove that our solution converges to a desired approximation and derive explicit error bounds.
中摘: 最近的研究表明，上下文感知低秩近似是一种有用的工具，可以用于现代大规模神经网络的压缩和微调。在这种近似方法中，范数通过输入激活矩阵加权，显著提高了与无加权情况相比的指标。然而，现有的神经网络方法由于依赖于涉及显式Gram矩阵计算及其后续求逆的经典公式，而遭受数值不稳定性的困扰。

为了解决这些限制，我们提出了一种全新的无需求逆的正则化框架，该框架完全基于稳定分解，并克服了以往方法中的数值难题。我们的方法能够处理可能出现的各种挑战场景：（1）当校准矩阵超出GPU内存容量时，（2）当输入激活矩阵接近奇异时，甚至（3）当数据不足导致无法获得唯一的近似时。对于第三种情况，我们证明了我们的解决方案会收敛到期望的近似，并推导出了明确的误差界。

To address this, extensions such as the top-k Mallows model have been proposed, aligning better with practical applications. In this paper, we address several challenges related to the generalized top-k Mallows model, with a focus on analyzing buyer choices. [No.106] GaRA-SAM: Robustifying Segment Anything Model with Gated-Rank Adaptation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: GaRA-SAM: 基于门控秩适应性增强的Segment Anything模型
类别: applications
作者: Sohyun Lee;Yeho Gwon;Lukas Hoyer;Suha Kwak;
机构: Pohang University of Science and Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=VXygIRRHxz
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02882
英摘: Improving robustness of the Segment Anything Model (SAM) to input degradations is critical for its deployment in high-stakes applications such as autonomous driving and robotics. Our approach to this challenge prioritizes three key aspects: first, parameter efficiency to maintain the inherent generalization capability of SAM; second, fine-grained and input-aware robustification to precisely address the input corruption; and third, adherence to standard training protocols for ease of training. To this end, we propose gated-rank adaptation (GaRA). GaRA introduces lightweight adapters into intermediate layers of the frozen SAM, where each adapter dynamically adjusts the effective rank of its weight matrix based on the input by selectively activating (rank-1) components of the matrix using a learned gating module. This adjustment enables fine-grained and input-aware robustification without compromising the generalization capability of SAM. Our model, GaRA-SAM, significantly outperforms prior work on all robust segmentation benchmarks. In particular, it surpasses the previous best IoU score by up to 21.3\%p on ACDC, a challenging real corrupted image dataset.
中摘: 提高Segment Anything模型（SAM）对输入降级的鲁棒性对于其在自动驾驶和机器人等高风险应用中的部署至关重要。我们针对这一挑战采取的方法优先考虑三个关键方面：首先，参数效率以保持SAM固有的泛化能力；其次，细粒度和输入感知的鲁棒性增强，以精确解决输入污染问题；最后，遵循标准训练协议以便于训练。为此，我们提出了一种门控秩适应（GaRA）。GaRA在冻结的SAM中间层中引入了轻量级适配器，每个适配器根据输入动态调整其权重矩阵的有效秩，通过学习门控模块选择性地激活（秩1）矩阵的组件来进行这一调整。这种调整能够实现细粒度和输入感知的鲁棒性增强，同时不牺牲SAM的泛化能力。我们的模型GaRA-SAM在所有鲁棒分割基准测试中显著优于先前的工作，在ACDC这一具有挑战性的实际污染图像数据集上，尤其在IoU得分方面超越了之前的最佳成绩，提高了高达21.3%。

[No.108] Fira: Can We Achieve Full-rank Training of LLMs Under Low-rank Constraint?
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: Fira: 在低秩约束下，我们能否实现LLMs的满秩训练？
类别: deep learning
作者: Xi Chen;Kaituo Feng;Changsheng Li;Xunhao Lai;Xiangyu Yue;Ye Yuan;Guoren Wang;
机构: Beijing Institute of Technology;Chinese University of Hong Kong;Peking University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=7aSBAw7tJf
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01623
英摘: Low-rank training has emerged as a promising approach for reducing memory usage in training Large Language Models (LLMs). Previous methods either rely on decomposing weight matrices (e.g., LoRA), or seek to decompose gradient matrices (e.g., GaLore) to ensure reduced memory consumption. However, both of them constrain the training in a low-rank subspace, thus inevitably leading to sub-optimal performance. This raises a question: whether it is possible to consistently preserve the low-rank constraint for memory efficiency, while achieving full-rank training (i.e., training with full-rank gradients of full-rank weights) to avoid inferior outcomes? In this paper, we propose a new plug-and-play training framework for LLMs called Fira, as the first attempt to achieve this goal. First, we observe an interesting phenomenon during LLM training: the scaling impact of adaptive optimizers (e.g., Adam) on the gradient norm remains similar from low-rank to full-rank training. Based on this observation, we propose a norm-based scaling method, which utilizes the scaling impact of low-rank optimizers as substitutes for that of original full-rank optimizers to enable full-rank training. In this way, we can preserve the low-rank constraint in the optimizer while achieving full-rank training for better performance. Moreover, we find that there are sudden gradient rises during the optimization process, potentially causing loss spikes. To address this, we further put forward a norm-growth limiter to smooth the gradient via regulating the relative increase of gradient norms. Extensive experiments on the pre-training and fine-tuning of LLMs show that Fira outperforms both LoRA and GaLore, achieving performance that is comparable to or even better than full-rank training.
中摘: 低秩训练已成为减少大型语言模型（LLMs）训练时内存使用的一种有前途的方法。以往的方法要么依赖于分解权重矩阵（例如LoRA），要么寻求分解梯度矩阵（例如GaLore），以确保降低内存消耗。然而，这两种方法都会将训练限制在低秩子空间内，从而不可避免地导致性能不佳。这引发了一个问题：是否有可能在保持低秩约束以提高内存效率的同时，实现全秩训练（即使用全秩权重的全秩梯度进行训练），以避免性能不佳的结果？在本文中，我们提出了一种新的插件式训练框架Fira，这是首次尝试实现这一目标。首先，我们在LLM训练过程中观察到一个有趣的现象：自适应优化器（如Adam）对梯度范数的影响从低秩训练到全秩训练保持相似。基于这一观察，我们提出了一种基于范数的缩放方法，该方法利用低秩优化器的缩放影响来替代原始全秩优化器的缩放影响，从而实现全秩训练。这样一来，我们可以在优化器中保留低秩约束，同时通过实现更好的训练效果来进行全秩训练。此外，我们发现优化过程中会出现梯度突然上升的情况，可能会导致损失峰值。为了解决这个问题，我们进一步提出了梯度增长限制器，通过调节梯度范数的相对增加来平滑梯度。在LLM预训练和微调的广泛实验中，Fira的表现优于LoRA和GaLore，其性能与甚至优于全秩训练。
