[No.110] MokA: Multimodal Low-Rank Adaptation for MLLMs
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: MokA: 多模态低秩适应性技术用于元大型语言模型
类别: deep learning
作者: Yake Wei;Yu Miao;Dongzhan Zhou;Di Hu;
机构: Renmin University of China;Beijing University of Posts and Telecommunications;Shanghai AI Laboratory;
类型: Oral
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=oJ84bedrtM
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05191
英摘: In this paper, we reveal that most current efficient multimodal fine-tuning methods are hindered by a key limitation: they are directly borrowed from LLMs, often neglecting the intrinsic differences of multimodal scenarios and even affecting the full utilization of all modalities. Inspired by our empirical observation, we argue that unimodal adaptation and cross-modal adaptation are two essential parts for the effective fine-tuning of MLLMs. From this perspective, we propose Multimodal low-rank Adaptation (MokA), a multimodal-aware efficient fine-tuning strategy that takes multimodal characteristics into consideration. It compresses unimodal information by modality-specific parameters while explicitly enhancing cross-modal interaction, ensuring both unimodal and cross-modal adaptation. Extensive experiments cover three representative multimodal scenarios (audio-visual-text, visual-text, and speech-text), and multiple LLM backbones (LLaMA2/3, Qwen2, Qwen2.5-VL, etc). Consistent improvements indicate the efficacy and versatility of the proposed method. Ablation studies and efficiency evaluation are also conducted to fully asses our method. Overall, we think MokA provides a more targeted solution for efficient adaptation of MLLMs, paving the way for further exploration. The project page is at https://gewu-lab.github.io/MokA.
中摘: 在本文中，我们揭示了当前大多数高效的多模态微调方法受到一个关键限制的影响：这些方法直接借鉴自大规模语言模型（LLMs），往往忽视了多模态场景固有的差异，甚至影响了所有模态的充分利用。受我们的实证观察启发，我们认为单模态适应和跨模态适应是MLLMs有效微调的两个必不可少的部分。从这个角度来看，我们提出了Multimodal低秩适应（MokA）策略，这是一种多模态感知的高效微调策略，考虑了多模态的特点。它通过特定于模态的参数压缩单模态信息，同时明确增强跨模态交互，确保了单模态和跨模态的适应性。广泛实验覆盖了三种代表性多模态场景（音频-视觉-文本、视觉-文本和语音-文本），以及多种LLM基础模型（如LLaMA2/3、Qwen2、Qwen2.5-VL等）。一致的改进表明所提出方法的有效性和灵活性。我们也进行了消融研究和效率评估，以全面评估我们的方法。总体而言，我们认为MokA为MLLMs的高效适应提供了更具针对性的解决方案，为进一步探索铺平了道路。项目页面位于https://gewu-lab.github.io/MokA。

[No.111] On the rankability of visual embeddings
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 视觉嵌入的可排序性
类别: deep learning
作者: Ankit Sonthalia;Arnas Uselis;Seong Joon Oh;
机构: ;Parameter Lab;Eberhard Karls University of Tübingen;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=sPbsnUCyNt
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03683
英摘: We study whether visual embedding models capture continuous, ordinal attributes along linear directions, which we term _rank axes_. We define a model as _rankable_ for an attribute if projecting embeddings onto such an axis preserves the attribute's order. Across 7 popular encoders and 9 datasets with attributes like age, crowd count, head pose, aesthetics, and recency, we find that many embeddings are inherently rankable. Surprisingly, a small number of samples, or even just two extreme examples, often suffice to recover meaningful rank axes, without full-scale supervision. These findings open up new use cases for image ranking in vector databases and motivate further study into the structure and learning of rankable embeddings. Our code is available at https://github.com/aktsonthalia/rankable-vision-embeddings.
中摘: 我们研究视觉嵌入模型是否沿线性方向捕捉连续的、序数的属性，我们将这些线性方向称为“秩轴”。如果将嵌入投影到这样的轴上能够保持属性的顺序，我们就称一个模型对该属性是可排序的。在7个流行的编码器和9个包含年龄、人群数量、头部姿态、美观度和时效性等属性的数据集中，我们发现许多嵌入本质上是可排序的。令人惊讶的是，往往只需要少量样本，甚至仅仅两个极端例子就能恢复有意义的秩轴，而无需进行全面监督。这些发现为矢量数据库中的图像排名开辟了新的应用场景，并激发了对可排序嵌入结构和学习的进一步研究。我们的代码可在https://github.com/aktsonthalia/rankable-vision-embeddings获取。

[No.112] PoLAR: Polar-Decomposed Low-Rank Adapter Representation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: PoLAR：极分解低秩适配器表示
类别: deep learning
作者: Kai Lion;Liang Zhang;Bingcong Li;Niao He;
机构: ETH Zurich;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=jDxFD45kkc
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03133
英摘: We show that low-rank adaptation of large-scale models suffers from a low stable rank that is well below the linear algebraic rank of the subspace, degrading fine-tuning performance. To mitigate the underutilization of the allocated subspace, we propose PoLAR, a parameterization inspired by the polar decomposition that factorizes the low-rank update into two direction matrices constrained to Stiefel manifolds and an unconstrained scale matrix. Our theory shows that PoLAR yields an exponentially faster convergence rate on a canonical low-rank adaptation problem. Pairing the parameterization with Riemannian optimization leads to consistent gains on three different benchmarks testing general language understanding, commonsense reasoning, and mathematical problem solving with base model sizes ranging from 350M to 27B.
中摘: 我们证明了低秩适应的大规模模型存在一个远低于子空间线性代数秩的低稳定秩，这会降低微调性能。为了缓解分配子空间的利用率不足问题，我们提出了一种名为PoLAR的参数化方法，该方法受到极分解的启发，将低秩更新分解为两个受Stiefel流形约束的方向矩阵和一个无约束的比例矩阵。我们的理论表明，PoLAR在经典的低秩适应问题上具有指数级更快的收敛速度。将该参数化方法与黎曼优化相结合，在三个不同基准测试中分别针对通用语言理解、常识推理和数学问题解决，从350M到27B不等的基础模型大小，均能实现一致性的性能提升。

英摘: We propose a novel approach to learn relational policies for classical planning based on learning to rank actions. [No.117] QSVD: Efficient Low-rank Approximation for Unified Query-Key-Value Weight Compression in Low-Precision Vision-Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: QSVD：统一查询-键-值权重压缩的高效低秩近似在低精度视觉语言模型中

英摘: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are integral to tasks such as image captioning and visual question answering, but their high computational cost, driven by large memory footprints and processing time, limits their scalability and real-time applicability. In this work, we propose leveraging Singular-Value Decomposition (SVD) over the joint query (Q), key (K), and value (V) weight matrices to reduce KV cache size and computational overhead. Our method outperforms previous approaches that rely solely on quantization or SVD by achieving more than $10\%$ accuracy improvement while consuming less hardware cost, making it better for real-time deployment on resource-constrained devices. [No.119] Ravan: Multi-Head Low-Rank Adaptation for Federated Fine-Tuning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 拉万：多头低秩适应性 federated 微调
类别: deep learning
作者: Arian Raje;Baris Askin;Divyansh Jhunjhunwala;Gauri Joshi;
机构: Carnegie Mellon University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=gyn4n8oC9B
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05568
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) have not yet effectively leveraged the vast amounts of edge-device data, and federated learning (FL) offers a promising paradigm to collaboratively fine-tune LLMs without transferring private edge data to the cloud. To operate within the computation and communication constraints of edge devices, recent literature on federated fine-tuning of LLMs proposes the use of low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and similar parameter-efficient methods. However, LoRA-based methods suffer from accuracy degradation in FL settings, primarily because of data and computational heterogeneity across clients. We propose Ravan, an adaptive multi-head LoRA method that balances parameter efficiency and model expressivity by reparameterizing the weight updates as the sum of multiple LoRA heads $s_i\textbf{B}_i\textbf{H}_i\textbf{A}_i$ in which only the core matrices $\textbf{H}_i$ and their lightweight scaling factors $s_i$ are trained. These trainable scaling factors let the optimization focus on the most useful heads, recovering a higher-rank approximation of the full update without increasing the number of communicated parameters since clients upload $s_i\textbf{H}_i$ directly. Experiments on vision and language benchmarks show that Ravan improves test accuracy by $2-8\%$ over prior parameter-efficient baselines, making it a robust and scalable solution for federated fine-tuning of LLMs.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）尚未有效地利用边缘设备的数据，而联邦学习（FL）提供了一种在不将私人边缘数据传输到云端的情况下协同微调LLMs的有前景的方法。为了在边缘设备的计算和通信限制内运行，最近关于联邦微调LLMs的研究提出使用低秩适应（LoRA）和类似的方法来提高参数效率。然而，在FL设置中基于LoRA的方法会遭受准确度下降的问题，主要是由于客户端之间数据和计算的异构性。我们提出了一种名为Ravan的自适应多头LoRA方法，通过将权重更新重新参数化为多个LoRA头的和$s_i\textbf{B}_i\textbf{H}_i\textbf{A}_i$，其中只有核心矩阵$\textbf{H}_i$及其轻量级缩放因子$s_i$被训练。这些可训练的缩放因子使得优化能够专注于最有用的头，从而恢复了完整更新的更高秩近似，而无需增加通信参数的数量，因为客户端直接上传的是$s_i\textbf{H}_i$。在视觉和语言基准测试上的实验表明，Ravan在前参数高效基线的基础上提高了2-8%的测试准确率，使其成为联邦微调LLMs的稳健且可扩展的解决方案。

[No.120] Revisiting Frank-Wolfe for Structured Nonconvex Optimization
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 重新审视弗兰克-沃尔夫算法在结构化非凸优化中的应用
类别: optimization
作者: Hoomaan Maskan;Yikun Hou;Suvrit Sra;Alp Yurtsever;
机构: Umeå University;;Technische Universität München;Massachusetts Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=hJRklWq4Ah
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08921
英摘: We introduce a new projection-free (Frank-Wolfe) method for optimizing structured nonconvex functions that are expressed as a difference of two convex functions. This problem class subsumes smooth nonconvex minimization, positioning our method as a promising alternative to the classical Frank-Wolfe algorithm. DC decompositions are not unique; by carefully selecting a decomposition, we can better exploit the problem structure, improve computational efficiency, and adapt to the underlying problem geometry to find better local solutions. We prove that the proposed method achieves a first-order stationary point in $O(1/ε^2)$ iterations, matching the complexity of the standard Frank-Wolfe algorithm for smooth nonconvex minimization in general. Specific decompositions can, for instance, yield a gradient-efficient variant that requires only $O(1/ε)$ calls to the gradient oracle. Finally, we present numerical experiments demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed method compared to other projection-free algorithms.
中摘: 我们提出了一种新的无投影（弗兰克-沃尔夫）方法，用于优化可表示为两个凸函数之差的结构化非凸函数。这类问题包括平滑非凸最小化，因此将我们的方法定位为经典弗兰克-沃尔夫算法的一种有前途的替代方案。DC分解不是唯一的；通过仔细选择分解方式，我们可以更好地利用问题结构，提高计算效率，并适应底层问题几何形状以找到更好的局部解。我们证明了所提出的方法在$O(1/ε^2)$次迭代中可以达到一阶稳定点，在一般情况下与标准弗兰克-沃尔夫算法用于平滑非凸最小化的复杂度相同。特定的分解方式可以例如提供一种梯度高效的变体，只需要$O(1/ε)$次梯度查询即可。最后，我们展示了数值实验，这些实验表明所提出的方法在与其他无投影算法的比较中更有效。

[No.121] RankSEG-RMA: An Efficient Segmentation Algorithm via Reciprocal Moment Approximation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: RankSEG-RMA：一种通过互反矩近似实现的高效分割算法
类别: general machine learning
作者: Zixun Wang;Ben Dai;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=4tRMm1JJhw
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15362
英摘: Semantic segmentation labels each pixel in an image with its corresponding class, and is typically evaluated using the Intersection over Union (IoU) and Dice metrics to quantify the overlap between predicted and ground-truth segmentation masks. In the literature, most existing methods estimate pixel-wise class probabilities, then apply argmax or thresholding to obtain the final prediction. These methods have been shown to generally lead to inconsistent or suboptimal results, as they do not directly maximize segmentation metrics. To address this issue, a novel consistent segmentation framework, RankSEG, has been proposed, which includes RankDice and RankIoU specifically designed to optimize the Dice and IoU metrics, respectively. Although RankSEG almost guarantees improved performance, it suffers from two major drawbacks. First, it is its computational expense-RankDice has a complexity of O(d log d) with a substantial constant factor (where d represents the number of pixels), while RankIoU exhibits even higher complexity O(d^2), thus limiting its practical application. For instance, in LiTS, prediction with RankSEG takes 16.33 seconds compared to just 0.01 seconds with the argmax rule. Second, RankSEG is only applicable to overlapping segmentation settings, where multiple classes can occupy the same pixel, which contrasts with standard benchmarks that typically assume non-overlapping segmentation. In this paper, we overcome these two drawbacks via a reciprocal moment approximation (RMA) of RankSEG with the following contributions: (i) we improve RankSEG using RMA, namely RankSEG-RMA, reduces the complexity of both algorithms to O(d) while maintaining comparable performance; (ii) inspired by RMA, we develop a pixel-wise score function that allows efficient implementation for non-overlapping segmentation settings.
中摘: 语义分割为图像中的每个像素分配其对应的类别标签，并通常使用交并比（IoU）和Dice指标来量化预测分割掩码与ground-truth分割掩码之间的重叠程度。在文献中，大多数现有方法估计像素级类别概率，然后应用argmax或阈值处理以获得最终预测。这些方法已被证明通常会导致不一致或次优的结果，因为它们并没有直接最大化分割指标。为解决这一问题，提出了一种新颖的一致分割框架RankSEG，该框架包括专为优化Dice和IoU指标设计的RankDice和RankIoU。尽管RankSEG几乎可以保证性能提升，但它存在两个主要缺点。首先，它的计算成本较高——RankDice具有O(d log d)的复杂性，其中包含一个显著的常数因子（d表示像素数量），而RankIoU甚至表现出更高的复杂性O(d^2)，这限制了其实用性。例如，在LiTS数据集中，使用RankSEG进行预测需要16.33秒，而使用argmax规则只需0.01秒。其次，RankSEG仅适用于重叠分割设置，即多个类别可以占据同一个像素，这与标准基准假设的非重叠分割形成了对比。在本文中，我们通过引入RankSEG的互逆矩近似（RMA）克服了这两个缺点，并做出了以下贡献：(i) 我们改进了RankSEG，使用RMA后的RankSEG-RMA将两种算法的复杂度降低至O(d)同时保持相当的性能；(ii) 受RMA启发，我们开发了一个像素级评分函数，以便于在非重叠分割设置下的高效实现。

[No.123] Beyond Higher Rank: Token-wise Input-Output Projections for Efficient Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 超越高秩：基于令牌的输入输出投影以实现高效的低秩适应
类别: deep learning
作者: Shiwei Li;Xiandi Luo;Haozhao Wang;Xing Tang;Ziqiang Cui;Dugang Liu;Yuhua Li;xiuqiang He;Ruixuan Li;
机构: Huazhong University of Science and Technology;Beijing University of Posts and Telecommunications;Shenzhen Technology University;Tencent;;Shenzhen University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=w2xl15ZbD3
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23123
英摘: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is a parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) method widely used in large language models (LLMs). LoRA essentially describes the projection of an input space into a low-dimensional output space, with the dimensionality determined by the LoRA rank. In standard LoRA, all input tokens share the same weights and undergo an identical input-output projection. This limits LoRA's ability to capture token-specific information due to the inherent semantic differences among tokens. To address this limitation, we propose Token-wise Projected Low-Rank Adaptation (TopLoRA), which dynamically adjusts LoRA weights according to the input token, thereby learning token-wise input-output projections in an end-to-end manner. Formally, the weights of TopLoRA can be expressed as $BΣ_X A$, where $A$ and $B$ are low-rank matrices (as in standard LoRA), and $Σ_X$ is a diagonal matrix generated from each input token $X$. Notably, TopLoRA does not increase the rank of LoRA weights but achieves more granular adaptation by learning token-wise LoRA weights (i.e., token-wise input-output projections). Extensive experiments across multiple models and datasets demonstrate that TopLoRA consistently outperforms LoRA and its variants. The code is available at https://github.com/Leopold1423/toplora-neurips25.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）是一种广泛应用于大型语言模型（LLMs）的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法。LoRA本质上描述了将输入空间投影到由LoRA秩决定的低维输出空间。在标准LoRA中，所有输入词元共享相同的权重，并经历相同的输入-输出投影。这限制了LoRA捕捉词元特定信息的能力，因为词元之间存在固有的语义差异。为了解决这一限制，我们提出了一种词元级动态投影低秩适应（TopLoRA）方法，该方法根据输入词元动态调整LoRA权重，从而以端到端的方式学习词元级的输入-输出投影。具体而言，TopLoRA的权重可以表示为$BΣ_X A$，其中$A$和$B$是低秩矩阵（与标准LoRA相同），而$Σ_X$是从每个输入词元$X$生成的对角矩阵。值得注意的是，TopLoRA并没有增加LoRA权重的秩，而是通过学习词元级的LoRA权重（即词元级的输入-输出投影）来实现更精细的适应。在多个模型和数据集上的大量实验表明，TopLoRA始终优于LoRA及其变体。代码可在https://github.com/Leopold1423/toplora-neurips25获取。

[No.124] Demystifying Language Model Forgetting with Low-rank Example Associations
来源: NeurIPS 2025

类别: deep learning

英摘: Large language models (LLMs) suffer from forgetting of upstream knowledge when fine-tuned. Despite efforts on mitigating forgetting, few have investigated how forgotten upstream examples are dependent on newly learned tasks. Insights on such dependencies enable efficient and targeted mitigation of forgetting. In this paper, we empirically analyze forgetting that occurs in $N$ upstream examples of language modeling or instruction-tuning after fine-tuning LLMs on one of $M$ new tasks, visualized in $M\times N$ matrices. We show that the matrices are often well-approximated with low-rank matrices, indicating the dominance of simple associations between the learned tasks and forgotten upstream examples. Leveraging the analysis, we predict forgetting of upstream examples when fine-tuning LLMs on unseen tasks with matrix completion over the empirical associations. This enables fast identification of most forgotten examples without expensive inference on the entire upstream data. Despite simplicity, the approach outperforms prior approaches that learn semantic relationships of learned tasks and upstream examples with LMs. We demonstrate the practical utility of our analysis by showing statistically significantly reduced forgetting as we upweight predicted examples for replay during fine-tuning. Code, data, and statistics collected: https://github.com/AuCson/low-rank-forgetting
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在微调过程中会遗忘上游知识。尽管已经努力减轻遗忘现象，但很少有人研究遗忘的上游示例与新学习任务之间的依赖关系。对这种依赖关系的理解能够实现高效且针对性的遗忘缓解。在本文中，我们通过分析在对LLMs进行其中一种新任务微调后，语言建模或指令调整中发生的$N$个上游示例的遗忘情况，并将其可视化为$M\times N$矩阵来实证研究这一问题。我们发现这些矩阵通常可以用低秩矩阵很好地近似，这表明学习任务和遗忘的上游示例之间存在简单的关联。利用这一分析，我们可以通过矩阵完成的方法，在实际关联上预测在对未见过的任务进行LLMs微调时遗忘的上游示例，从而快速识别大部分遗忘的示例，而无需对整个上游数据进行昂贵的推理。尽管该方法简单，但它在减轻遗忘方面优于先前通过语言模型学习已学习任务和上游示例的语义关系的方法。我们通过实验证明了该分析的实际用途：通过增加预测遗忘示例的权重来进行回放，显著减少了遗忘现象。相关代码、数据和统计信息可在https://github.com/AuCson/low-rank-forgetting获取。

[No.127] AuroRA: Breaking Low-Rank Bottleneck of LoRA with Nonlinear Mapping
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: AuroRA：打破LoRA低秩瓶颈的非线性映射

作者: Haonan Dong;Wenhao Zhu;Guojie Song;Liang Wang;

英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a widely adopted parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) method validated across NLP and CV domains. However, LoRA faces an inherent low-rank bottleneck: narrowing its performance gap with full finetuning requires increasing the rank of its parameter matrix, resulting in significant parameter overhead. Recent linear LoRA variants have attempted to enhance expressiveness by introducing additional linear mappings; however, their composition remains inherently linear and fails to fundamentally improve LoRA's representational capacity. To address this limitation, we propose AuroRA, which incorporates an Adaptive Nonlinear Layer (ANL) between two linear projectors to capture fixed and learnable nonlinearities. This combination forms an MLP-like structure with a compressed rank, enabling flexible and precise approximation of diverse target functions while theoretically guaranteeing lower approximation errors and bounded gradients. Extensive experiments on 22 datasets and 6 pretrained models demonstrate that AuroRA: (I) not only matches or surpasses full fine-tuning performance with only 6.18% ~ 25% of LoRA's parameters but also (II) outperforms competitive PEFT methods by up to 10.88% in both NLP and CV tasks, and (III) exhibits robust performance across various rank configurations.

[No.128] Efficient Low Rank Attention for Long-Context Inference in Large Language Models

类别: deep learning
作者: Li Tenghui;Guoxu Zhou;Xuyang ZHAO;Yuning Qiu;Qibin Zhao;
机构: RIKEN;Guangdong University of Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Mc0eJHZhW5
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23649
英摘: As the length of input text increases, the key-value (KV) cache in LLMs imposes prohibitive GPU memory costs and limits long-context inference on resource constrained devices. Existing approaches, such as KV quantization and pruning, reduce memory usage but suffer from numerical precision loss or suboptimal retention of key-value pairs. In this work, Low Rank Query and Key attention (LRQK) is introduced, a two-stage framework that jointly decomposes full-precision query and key matrices into compact rank-\(r\) factors during the prefill stage, and then employs these low-dimensional projections to compute proxy attention scores in \(\mathcal{O}(lr)\) time at each decode step. By selecting only the top-\(k\) tokens and a small fixed set of recent tokens, LRQK employs a mixed GPU-CPU cache with a hit-and-miss mechanism where only missing full-precision KV pairs are transferred, thereby preserving exact attention outputs while reducing CPU-GPU data movement. Extensive experiments on the RULER and LongBench benchmarks with LLaMA-3-8B and Qwen2.5-7B demonstrate that LRQK matches or surpasses leading sparse-attention methods in long context settings, while delivering significant memory savings with minimal accuracy loss. Our code is available at https://github.com/tenghuilee/LRQK.
中摘: 随着输入文本长度的增加，LLM中的键值（KV）缓存会带来高昂的GPU内存成本，并限制在资源受限设备上进行长时间上下文推理。现有的方法，如KV量化和剪枝，可以减少内存使用，但会遭受数值精度损失或关键值对保留不足的问题。在这项工作中，我们引入了低秩查询和键注意机制（LRQK），这是一种两阶段框架，在预填充阶段将全精度的查询和键矩阵联合分解为紧凑的秩-\(r\) 因子，然后在每个解码步骤中使用这些低维度投影来计算代理注意分数，计算复杂度为\(\mathcal{O}(lr)\)。通过仅选择前-\(k\) 个令牌和一小部分最近的令牌，LRQK 使用混合GPU-CPU缓存，并采用命中和未命中的机制，仅转移缺失的全精度KV对，从而保持精确的注意输出同时减少CPU-GPU数据移动。在RULER和LongBench基准测试中使用LLaMA-3-8B和Qwen2.5-7B进行的广泛实验表明，LRQK 在长时间上下文设置中能够匹配或超越领先的稀疏注意机制，同时提供显著的内存节省并保持最小的准确率损失。我们的代码可在https://github.com/tenghuilee/LRQK获取。

[No.129] GoRA: Gradient-driven Adaptive Low Rank Adaptation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: GoRA：梯度驱动自适应低秩适应
类别: deep learning
作者: haonan he;Peng Ye;Yuchen Ren;yuan yuan;LuyangZhou;ShucunJu;lei chen;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;Chinese University of Hong Kong;;Chinese Academy of Sciences;Anhui Disaster Warning & Agrometeorological Information Center;Anhui Disaster Warning &Agrometeorological Information Center;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=d1dL1ymD6N
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12171
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a crucial method for efficiently fine-tuning large language models (LLMs), with its effectiveness influenced by two key factors: rank selection and weight initialization. While numerous LoRA variants have been proposed to improve performance by addressing one of these aspects, they often compromise usability or computational efficiency. In this paper, we analyze and identify the core limitations of existing approaches and propose a novel framework--GoRA (Gradient-driven Adaptive Low Rank Adaptation)--that simultaneously adapts both the rank and initialization strategy within a unified framework. GoRA leverages gradient information during training to dynamically assign optimal ranks and initialize low-rank adapter weights in an adaptive manner. To our knowledge, GoRA is the first method that not only addresses the limitations of prior approaches--which often focus on either rank selection or initialization in isolation--but also unifies both aspects within a single framework, enabling more effective and efficient adaptation. Extensive experiments across various architectures and modalities show that GoRA consistently outperforms existing LoRA-based methods while preserving the efficiency of vanilla LoRA. For example, when fine-tuning Llama3.1-8B-Base for mathematical reasoning, GoRA achieves a 5.13-point improvement over standard LoRA and even outperforms full fine-tuning by 2.05 points under high-rank settings. Code is available at: https://github.com/hhnqqq/MyTransformers.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）是一种高效微调大型语言模型（LLM）的关键方法，其效果受两个关键因素的影响：秩选择和权重初始化。尽管提出了许多LoRA变体来通过解决其中一个方面的问题来提高性能，但它们往往在便利性和计算效率之间做出妥协。在这篇论文中，我们分析并识别了现有方法的核心局限性，并提出了一种新的框架——GoRA（梯度驱动的自适应低秩适应），该框架在同一框架内同时适应秩和初始化策略。GoRA 在训练过程中利用梯度信息动态分配最优秩，并以自适应方式初始化低秩适配器权重。据我们所知，GoRA 是首个不仅解决了先前方法在秩选择或初始化方面的单一问题，还统一了这两个方面的单一框架的方法，从而实现更有效的且更高效的适应。在各种架构和模态的广泛实验中，GoRA 一致地优于现有的LoRA基线方法，同时保持了LoRA的基本效率。例如，在对Llama3.1-8B-Base进行数学推理的微调时，GoRA 在标准LoRA上的表现提高了5.13个百分点，并且在高秩设置下甚至比完全微调高出2.05个百分点。代码可在以下地址获取：https://github.com/hhnqqq/MyTransformers。

[No.130] The Persistence of Neural Collapse Despite Low-Rank Bias
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 尽管存在低秩偏差，神经网络塌缩的持久性
类别: theory
作者: Connall Garrod;Jonathan P. Keating;
机构: University of Oxford;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=AumtzgHji1
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23169
英摘: Neural collapse (NC) and its multi-layer variant, deep neural collapse (DNC), describe a structured geometry that occurs in the features and weights of trained deep networks. Recent theoretical work by Sukenik et al. using a deep unconstrained feature model (UFM) suggests that DNC is suboptimal under mean squared error (MSE) loss. They heuristically argue that this is due to low-rank bias induced by L2 regularization. In this work, we extend this result to deep UFMs trained with cross-entropy loss, showing that high-rank structures, including DNC, are not generally optimal. We characterize the associated low-rank bias, proving a fixed bound on the number of non-negligible singular values at global minima as network depth increases. We further analyze the loss surface, demonstrating that DNC is more prevalent in the landscape than other critical configurations, which we argue explains its frequent empirical appearance. Our results are validated through experiments in deep UFMs and deep neural networks.
中摘: 神经崩溃（NC）及其多层变体深度神经崩溃（DNC）描述了在训练深层网络的特征和权重中出现的一种结构化几何形态。Sukenik等人近期使用深层无约束特征模型（UFM）的理论工作表明，在均方误差（MSE）损失下，DNC是次优的。他们推测这可能是由于L2正则化引起的低秩偏差所致。在本文中，我们将这一结果扩展到使用交叉熵损失训练的深层UFM，证明高秩结构，包括DNC，并非一般情况下最优的。我们表征了相关低秩偏差，证明随着网络深度增加，在全局最小值处非可忽略奇异值的数量存在固定的上限。此外，我们进一步分析了损失表面，展示了在景观中DNC比其他关键配置更为普遍，我们认为这是其在实验中频繁出现的原因。我们的结果通过在深层UFM和深层神经网络中的实验得到了验证。

[No.131] Accurate and Efficient Low-Rank Model Merging in Core Space
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 核心空间中的准确高效低秩模型融合
类别: deep learning
作者: Aniello Panariello;Daniel Marczak;Simone Magistri;Angelo Porrello;Bartłomiej Twardowski;Andrew D. Bagdanov;Simone Calderara;Joost van de Weijer;
机构: University of Modena and Reggio Emilia;Institute for Development, Economic Analysis, and Simulation (IDEAS);Warsaw University of Technology;University of Florence;IDEAS Research Institute;Universitat Autònoma de Barcelona;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=y1z7SAS8q8
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17786
英摘: In this paper, we address the challenges associated with merging low-rank adaptations of large neural networks. With the rise of parameter-efficient adaptation techniques, such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), model fine-tuning has become more accessible. While fine-tuning models with LoRA is highly efficient, existing merging methods often sacrifice this efficiency by merging fully-sized weight matrices. We propose the Core Space merging framework, which enables the merging of LoRA-adapted models within a common alignment basis, thereby preserving the efficiency of low-rank adaptation while substantially improving accuracy across tasks. We further provide a formal proof that projection into Core Space ensures no loss of information and provide a complexity analysis showing the efficiency gains. Extensive empirical results demonstrate that Core Space significantly improves existing merging techniques and achieves state-of-the-art results on both vision and language tasks while utilizing a fraction of the computational resources. Codebase is available at https://github.com/apanariello4/core-space-merging.
中摘: 在本文中，我们探讨了合并低秩适应的大神经网络时所面临的挑战。随着参数高效适应技术的发展，如低秩适应（LoRA），模型微调变得更加容易。尽管使用LoRA进行微调非常高效，但现有的合并方法往往通过合并完全大小的权重矩阵牺牲了这种效率。我们提出了核心空间合并框架，该框架能够在公共对齐基的基础上合并LoRA适应的模型，从而保持低秩适应的高效性，并显著提高各种任务的准确性。此外，我们还提供了一个正式证明，表明将模型投影到核心空间不会丢失任何信息，并提供了复杂性分析以展示效率提升。广泛的实证结果表明，核心空间显著改进了现有的合并技术，并且在利用极少的计算资源的情况下，在视觉和语言任务上达到了最先进的性能。代码库可在https://github.com/apanariello4/core-space-merging获取。

[No.132] From Condensation to Rank Collapse: A Two-Stage Analysis of Transformer Training Dynamics
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 从凝缩到秩崩溃：Transformer训练动力学的两阶段分析
类别: deep learning
作者: Zheng-An Chen;Tao Luo;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;;
类型: Oral
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=gm5mkiTGOy
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06954
英摘: Although transformer-based models have shown exceptional empirical performance, the fundamental principles governing their training dynamics are inadequately characterized beyond configuration-specific studies. Inspired by empirical evidence showing improved reasoning capabilities under small initialization scales in language models, we employ the gradient flow analytical framework established in [Zhou et al. NeurIPS 2022] to systematically investigate linearized Transformer training dynamics. Our theoretical analysis dissects the dynamics of attention modules into two distinct stages. In the first stage, asymmetric weight perturbations from random initialization sustain non-degenerate gradient dynamics in parameter matrices, facilitating systematic escape from small initialization regimes. Subsequently, these matrices undergo condensation, progressively aligning toward the target orientation. In the second stage, the previously static key-query matrices actively participate in training, driving the normalized matrices toward asymptotic rank collapse. This two-stage framework generalizes classical directional convergence results.
中摘: 尽管基于变换器的模型在实际应用中表现出色，但它们训练动力学的基本原理在特定配置研究之外并未得到充分描述。受语言模型在小初始化尺度下表现出增强的推理能力的实证证据启发，我们采用[周等人，NeurIPS 2022]中建立的梯度流分析框架，系统地探讨线性化变换器训练动力学。我们的理论分析将注意力模块的动力学分解为两个不同的阶段。在第一个阶段，随机初始化产生的非对称权重扰动维持了参数矩阵中的非退化梯度动态，从而有助于系统地从小初始化区域中逃逸。随后，这些矩阵经历了凝聚过程，逐步向目标方向对齐。在第二个阶段，先前静态的关键-查询矩阵积极参与训练，推动归一化矩阵趋向于渐近秩坍缩。这种两阶段框架扩展了经典的定向收敛结果。

[No.133] Gated Integration of Low-Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning of Large Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 面向持续学习的大语言模型的门控低秩适应集成
类别: deep learning
作者: Yan-Shuo Liang;Jiarui Chen;Wu-Jun Li;
机构: Nanjing University;Wuhan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=lVV7F0piDK
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15424
英摘: Continual learning (CL), which requires the model to learn multiple tasks sequentially, is crucial for large language models (LLMs). Recently, low-rank adaptation~(LoRA), one of the most representative parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, has gained increasing attention in CL of LLMs. However, most existing CL methods based on LoRA typically expand a new LoRA branch to learn each new task and force the new and old LoRA branches to influence old tasks equally, potentially leading to forgetting. In this work, we propose a new method, called gated integration of low-rank adaptation (GainLoRA), for CL of LLMs. GainLoRA expands a new LoRA branch for each new task and introduces gating modules to integrate the new and old LoRA branches. Furthermore, GainLoRA leverages the new gating module to minimize the influence from the new LoRA branch to old tasks, effectively mitigating forgetting and improving the model's overall performance. Experimental results on CL benchmarks demonstrate that GainLoRA outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods.
中摘: 持续学习（CL），要求模型依次学习多个任务，在大型语言模型（LLMs）中至关重要。最近，低秩适应（LoRA），作为最具有代表性的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法之一，逐渐引起了对LLM持续学习的关注。然而，大多数基于LoRA的现有CL方法通常为每个新任务扩展一个新的LoRA分支，并强制新的和旧的LoRA分支对旧任务产生相同的影响，这可能会导致模型遗忘。在本文中，我们提出了一种新的方法，称为低秩适应的门控集成（GainLoRA），用于LLM的持续学习。GainLoRA为每个新任务扩展一个新的LoRA分支，并引入门控模块来集成新旧LoRA分支。此外，GainLoRA利用新的门控模块最小化新LoRA分支对旧任务的影响，从而有效缓解遗忘并提高模型的整体性能。在持续学习基准上的实验结果表明，GainLoRA优于现有的最先进的方法。

[No.134] The Primacy of Magnitude in Low-Rank Adaptation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩适应中的幅度优先原则
类别: deep learning
作者: Zicheng Zhang;Haoran Li;Yifeng Zhang;Guoqiang Gong;Jiaxing Wang;pengzhang liu;Qixia Jiang;Junxing Hu;
机构: ;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;JD.com;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=s4LnWgjacg
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06558
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) offers a parameter-efficient paradigm for tuning large models. While recent spectral initialization methods improve convergence and performance over the naive "Noise & Zeros" scheme, their extra computational and storage overhead undermines efficiency. In this paper, we establish update magnitude as the fundamental driver of LoRA performance and propose LoRAM, a magnitude-driven "Basis & Basis" initialization scheme that matches spectral methods without their inefficiencies. Our key contributions are threefold: (i) Magnitude of weight updates determines convergence. We prove low-rank structures intrinsically bound update magnitudes, unifying hyperparameter tuning in learning rate, scaling factor, and initialization as mechanisms to optimize magnitude regulation. (ii) Spectral initialization succeeds via magnitude amplification. We demystify that the presumed knowledge-driven benefit of the spectral component essentially arises from the boost in the weight update magnitude. (iii) A novel and compact initialization strategy, LoRAM, scales deterministic orthogonal bases using pretrained weight magnitudes to simulate spectral gains. Extensive experiments show that LoRAM serves as a strong baseline, retaining the full efficiency of LoRA while matching or outperforming spectral initialization across benchmarks.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）提供了一种参数高效的大模型调优范式。尽管最近的谱初始化方法在收敛性和性能上优于简单的“噪声与零”方案，但它们额外的计算和存储开销削弱了效率。在本文中，我们确立了权重更新幅度作为LoRA性能的基本驱动因素，并提出了一种名为LoRAM的幅度驱动的“基向量与基向量”初始化方案，该方案在不牺牲效率的情况下匹配谱方法的效果。我们的主要贡献包括三个方面：(i) 权重更新的幅度决定了收敛性。我们证明低秩结构固有地限制了更新幅度，将学习率、缩放因子和初始化统一为优化幅度调节的机制。(ii) 谱初始化通过幅度放大取得成功。我们揭示了所谓的知识驱动的谱组件优势实际上主要是由于权重更新幅度的提升。(iii) 提出了一种新颖且紧凑的初始化策略LoRAM，利用预训练的权重幅度来确定论正交基向量，模拟谱方法的效果。广泛的实验表明，LoRAM作为一个强大的基线，保留了LoRA的全部效率，在基准测试中能够匹配或超越谱初始化的效果。

[No.135] Accelerated Evolving Set Processes for Local PageRank Computation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 加速演变集过程以进行局部PageRank计算
类别: general machine learning
作者: BinbinHuang;Luo Luo;Yanghua Xiao;Deqing Yang;Baojian Zhou;
机构: Fudan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=zDOo34mbpl
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08010
英摘: This work proposes a novel framework based on nested evolving set processes to accelerate Personalized PageRank (PPR) computation. At each stage of the process, we employ a localized inexact proximal point iteration to solve a simplified linear system. We show that the time complexity of such localized methods is upper bounded by $\min\{\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(R^2/ε^2), \tilde{\mathcal{O}}(m)\}$ to obtain an $ε$-approximation of the PPR vector, where $m$ denotes the number of edges in the graph and $R$ is a constant defined via nested evolving set processes. Furthermore, the algorithms induced by our framework require solving only $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrtα)$ such linear systems, where $α$ is the damping factor. When $1/ε^2\ll m$, this implies the existence of an algorithm that computes an $\ epsilon $-approximation of the PPR vector with an overall time complexity of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(R^2 / (\sqrtαε^2)\right)$, independent of the underlying graph size. Our result resolves an open conjecture from existing literature. Experimental results on real-world graphs validate the efficiency of our methods, demonstrating significant convergence in the early stages.
中摘: 这项工作提出了一种基于嵌套演化集过程的新框架，以加速个性化PageRank（PPR）计算。在过程的每一阶段，我们采用局部不精确的近似点迭代来求解简化线性系统。我们证明了此类局部方法的时间复杂度被上界限制为$\min\{\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(R^2/ε^2), \tilde{\mathcal{O}}(m)\}$，以获得PPR向量的$ε$-逼近，其中$m$表示图中的边数，$R$是通过嵌套演化集过程定义的常数。此外，由我们框架诱导出的算法只需解决$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{α})$个这样的线性系统，其中$α$是阻尼因子。当$1/ε^2 \ll m$时，这意味着存在一个算法，其整体时间复杂度为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(R^2 / (\sqrt{α}ε^2)\right)$，与底层图的大小无关。我们的结果解决了现有文献中的一个公开猜想。实验结果表明，我们的方法在实际应用中具有很高的效率，在早期阶段就能实现显著的收敛。

[No.136] A geometric framework for momentum-based optimizers for low-rank training
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于几何框架的动量优化器在低秩训练中的应用
类别: deep learning
作者: Steffen Schotthöfer;Timon Klein;Jonas Kusch;
机构: Oak Ridge National Laboratory;Otto von Guericke University;Norwegian University of Life Sciences;
类型: Poster

英摘: Low-rank pre-training and fine-tuning have recently emerged as promising techniques for reducing the computational and storage costs of large neural networks. Training low-rank parameterizations typically relies on conventional optimizers such as heavy ball momentum methods or Adam. In this work, we identify and analyze potential difficulties that these training methods encounter when used to train low-rank parameterizations of weights. In particular, we show that classical momentum methods can struggle to converge to a local optimum due to the geometry of the underlying optimization landscape. To address this, we introduce novel training strategies derived from dynamical low-rank approximation, which explicitly account for the underlying geometric structure. Our approach leverages and combines tools from dynamical low-rank approximation and momentum-based optimization to design optimizers that respect the intrinsic geometry of the parameter space. We validate our methods through numerical experiments, demonstrating faster convergence, and stronger validation metrics at given parameter budgets.
中摘: 低秩预训练和微调最近被证明是降低大型神经网络计算和存储成本的有效技术。训练低秩参数化通常依赖于传统的优化器，如重球动量方法或Adam。在本文中，我们识别并分析了这些训练方法在用于训练权重的低秩参数化时遇到的潜在困难。特别是，我们表明，经典的动量方法由于底层优化景观的几何结构而可能难以收敛到局部最优解。为解决这一问题，我们引入了一种新的训练策略，该策略源自动态低秩近似，并明确考虑了底层的几何结构。我们的方法利用并结合了动态低秩近似和基于动量的优化工具，设计出尊重参数空间内在几何结构的优化器。通过数值实验验证了我们的方法，结果显示在给定的参数预算下具有更快的收敛速度和更强的验证指标。

[No.138] Learning Counterfactual Outcomes Under Rank Preservation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 学习保持排名下的反事实结果
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Peng Wu;Haoxuan Li;Chunyuan Zheng;Yan Zeng;Jiawei Chen;Yang Liu;Ruocheng Guo;Kun Zhang;
机构: Beijing Technology and Business University;Peking University;;University of California, Santa Cruz;Intuit AI;ByteDance;Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=7bEPq5MAQi
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.06398
英摘: Counterfactual inference aims to estimate the counterfactual outcome at the individual level given knowledge of an observed treatment and the factual outcome, with broad applications in fields such as epidemiology, econometrics, and management science. Previous methods rely on a known structural causal model (SCM) or assume the homogeneity of the exogenous variable and strict monotonicity between the outcome and exogenous variable. In this paper, we propose a principled approach for identifying and estimating the counterfactual outcome. We first introduce a simple and intuitive rank preservation assumption to identify the counterfactual outcome without relying on a known structural causal model. Building on this, we propose a novel ideal loss for theoretically unbiased learning of the counterfactual outcome and further develop a kernel-based estimator for its empirical estimation. Our theoretical analysis shows that the rank preservation assumption is not stronger than the homogeneity and strict monotonicity assumptions, and shows that the proposed ideal loss is convex, and the proposed estimator is unbiased. Extensive semi-synthetic and real-world experiments are conducted to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.
中摘: 反事实推理旨在根据观察到的治疗和实际结果的知识，在个体层面估计反事实结果，其在流行病学、计量经济学和管理科学等领域有着广泛的应用。以往的方法依赖于已知的结构因果模型（SCM），或者假设外生变量的一致性和结果与外生变量之间的严格单调性。本文提出了一种原则性的方法来识别和估计反事实结果。我们首先引入了一个简单直观的秩保持假设，无需依赖已知的结构因果模型即可识别反事实结果。在此基础上，我们提出了一个新颖的理想损失函数，以实现理论上无偏的学习反事实结果，并进一步开发了一个核基估计器来进行其实证估计。我们的理论分析表明，秩保持假设并不比一致性假设和严格单调性假设更强，并且展示了所提出理想损失函数的凸性以及所提出的估计器的无偏性。通过广泛的半合成和真实世界实验，我们证明了所提出方法的有效性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.139] Preference-Based Dynamic Ranking Structure Recognition
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于偏好动态排名结构识别
类别: general machine learning
作者: Nan Lu;Jian Shi;Xinyu Tian;
机构: Harvard University;Chinese Academy of Sciences;University of Minnesota;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=w1yccSuVtg
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24493
英摘: Preference-based data often appear complex and noisy but may conceal underlying homogeneous structures. This paper introduces a novel framework of ranking structure recognition for preference-based data. We first develop an approach to identify dynamic ranking groups by incorporating temporal penalties into a spectral estimation for the celebrated Bradley-Terry model. To detect structural changes, we introduce an innovative objective function and present a practicable algorithm based on dynamic programming. Theoretically, we establish the consistency of ranking group recognition by exploiting properties of a random `design matrix' induced by a reversible Markov chain. We also tailor a group inverse technique to quantify the uncertainty in item ability estimates. Additionally, we prove the consistency of structure change recognition, ensuring the robustness of the proposed framework. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate the practical utility and interpretability of our approach.
中摘: 基于偏好的数据往往显得复杂且嘈杂，但可能隐藏着潜在的同质结构。本文介绍了一种新颖的偏好基数据排名结构识别框架。我们首先通过将时间惩罚项纳入经典的布雷德利-特里（Bradley-Terry）模型的谱估计方法中，来识别动态排名组。为了检测结构变化，我们引入了一个创新的目标函数，并基于动态规划提出了一种可行算法。理论上，我们利用可逆马尔可夫链诱导的随机“设计矩阵”的性质，建立了排名组识别的一致性。此外，我们还定制了一种群逆技术来量化项目能力估计的不确定性。另外，我们也证明了结构变化识别的一致性，确保了所提框架的稳健性。我们在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法具有实际应用价值和可解释性。

[No.140] GeoRanker: Distance-Aware Ranking for Worldwide Image Geolocalization
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: GeoRanker：全球图像地理定位的距离感知排名
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Pengyue Jia;Seongheon Park;Song Gao;Xiangyu Zhao;Sharon Li;
机构: City University of Hong Kong;University of Wisconsin-Madison;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Zjq1CkKDGt
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13731
英摘: Worldwide image geolocalization-the task of predicting GPS coordinates from images taken anywhere on Earth-poses a fundamental challenge due to the vast diversity in visual content across regions. While recent approaches adopt a two-stage pipeline of retrieving candidates and selecting the best match, they typically rely on simplistic similarity heuristics and point-wise supervision, failing to model spatial relationships among candidates. In this paper, we propose GeoRanker, a distance-aware ranking framework that leverages large vision-language models to jointly encode query-candidate interactions and predict geographic proximity. In addition, we introduce a multi-order distance loss that ranks both absolute and relative distances, enabling the model to reason over structured spatial relationships. To support this, we curate GeoRanking, the first dataset explicitly designed for geographic ranking tasks with multimodal candidate information. GeoRanker achieves state-of-the-art results on two well-established benchmarks (IM2GPS3K and YFCC4K), significantly outperforming current best methods.
中摘: 全球图像地理定位——预测来自地球任何地区的图像的GPS坐标的任务——由于地区间视觉内容的巨大多样性而构成了一个基本挑战。尽管最近的方法采用了一种两阶段管道，即首先检索候选对象，然后选择最佳匹配，但它们通常依赖于简单的相似性启发式方法和点监督，未能建模候选对象之间的空间关系。在本文中，我们提出了GeoRanker，这是一种距离感知的排名框架，利用大规模的视觉-语言模型联合编码查询-候选对象交互，并预测地理位置的接近度。此外，我们引入了一种多阶距离损失，能够对绝对距离和相对距离进行排序，从而使模型能够在结构化空间关系上进行推理。为此，我们构建了GeoRanking，这是首个明确为地理排名任务设计的多模态候选信息数据集。GeoRanker在两个广泛认可的基准测试（IM2GPS3K和YFCC4K）上取得了最先进的成果，显著优于当前的最佳方法。

[No.141] Post Hoc Regression Refinement via Pairwise Rankings
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 事后回归精炼通过成对排名
类别: general machine learning
作者: Kevin Tirta Wijaya;Michael Sun;Minghao Guo;Hans-peter Seidel;Wojciech Matusik;Vahid Babaei;
机构: ;Massachusetts Institute of Technology;Max-Planck-Gesellschaft zur Förderung der Wissenschaften e.V.;Max-Planck Institute;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=CmKar1zptJ
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.16495
英摘: Accurate prediction of continuous properties is essential to many scientific and engineering tasks. Although deep-learning regressors excel with abundant labels, their accuracy deteriorates in data-scarce regimes. We introduce RankRefine, a model-agnostic, plug-and-play post hoc method that refines regression with expert knowledge coming from pairwise rankings. Given a query item and a small reference set with known properties, RankRefine combines the base regressor's output with a rank-based estimate via inverse variance weighting, requiring no retraining. In molecular property prediction task, RankRefine achieves up to 10% relative reduction in mean absolute error using only 20 pairwise comparisons obtained through a general-purpose large language model (LLM) with no finetuning. As rankings provided by human experts or general-purpose LLMs are sufficient for improving regression across diverse domains, RankRefine offers practicality and broad applicability, especially in low-data settings.
中摘: 准确预测连续属性对于许多科学和工程任务至关重要。尽管深度学习回归模型在标签丰富的数据集上表现出色，但在数据稀缺的情况下其准确性会下降。我们引入了RankRefine，这是一种模型无关的、即插即用的后处理方法，通过利用成对排名中的专家知识来细化回归预测。给定一个查询项和一个包含已知属性的小参考集，RankRefine 将基础回归模型的输出与基于排名的估计值通过逆方差加权相结合，无需重新训练。在分子属性预测任务中，仅使用通用大型语言模型（LLM）获得的20个成对比较，RankRefine 在未进行微调的情况下实现了均方绝对误差高达10%的相对降低。由于人类专家或通用大型语言模型提供的排名足以在各种领域改进回归预测，RankRefine 提供了实际可行性和广泛的适用性，尤其是在数据稀缺的环境中。

[No.142] RefLoRA: Refactored Low-Rank Adaptation for Efficient Fine-Tuning of Large Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: RefLoRA：重构低秩适应性调整以提高大型模型高效微调
类别: deep learning
作者: Yilang Zhang;Bingcong Li;Georgios B. Giannakis;
机构: University of Minnesota;VISA;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=zefDc9oi5T
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18877
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) lowers the computational and memory overhead of fine-tuning large models by updating a low-dimensional subspace of the pre-trained weight matrix. Albeit efficient, LoRA exhibits suboptimal convergence and noticeable performance degradation, due to inconsistent and imbalanced weight updates induced by its nonunique low-rank factorizations. To overcome these limitations, this article identifies the optimal low-rank factorization per step that minimizes an upper bound on the loss. The resultant refactored low-rank adaptation (RefLoRA) method promotes a flatter loss landscape, along with consistent and balanced weight updates, thus speeding up stable convergence. Extensive experiments evaluate RefLoRA on natural language understanding, and commonsense reasoning tasks with popular large language models including DeBERTaV3, LLaMA-7B, LLaMA2-7B and LLaMA3-8B. The numerical tests corroborate that RefLoRA converges faster, outperforms various benchmarks, and enjoys negligible computational overhead compared to state-of-the-art LoRA variants.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）通过更新预训练权重矩阵的低维子空间来降低大型模型微调的计算和内存开销。尽管高效，但LoRA表现出次优的收敛性和明显的性能下降，这是由于其非唯一的低秩分解引起的不一致和不平衡的权重更新。为克服这些限制，本文确定了每步中使损失上界最小化的最优低秩分解，从而促进了更平坦的损失景观以及一致和平衡的权重更新，加快了稳定的收敛速度。广泛的实验在包括DeBERTaV3、LLaMA-7B、LLaMA2-7B和LLaMA3-8B在内的流行大型语言模型的自然语言理解和常识推理任务上评估了RefLoRA方法。数值测试表明，RefLoRA收敛速度更快，优于各种基准，并且与最先进的LoRA变体相比，具有可忽略不计的计算开销。

[No.143] GraLoRA: Granular Low-Rank Adaptation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 粒度低秩适应：参数高效微调
类别: deep learning
作者: Yeonjoon Jung;Daehyun Ahn;Hyungjun Kim;Taesu Kim;Eunhyeok Park;
机构: SqueezeBits Inc.;Pohang University of Science and Technology;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=8wvOMQ2Olw
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20355
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a popular method for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of generative models, valued for its simplicity and effectiveness. Despite recent enhancements, LoRA still suffers from a fundamental limitation: overfitting when the bottleneck is widened. It performs best at ranks 32-64, yet its accuracy stagnates or declines at higher ranks, still falling short of full fine-tuning (FFT) performance. We identify the root cause as LoRA's structural bottleneck, which introduces gradient entanglement to the unrelated input channels and distorts gradient propagation. To address this, we introduce a novel structure, Granular Low-Rank Adaptation (GraLoRA) that partitions weight matrices into sub-blocks, each with its own low-rank adapter. With negligible computational or storage cost, GraLoRA overcomes LoRA's limitations, effectively increases the representational capacity, and more closely approximates FFT behavior. Experiments on code generation and commonsense reasoning benchmarks show that GraLoRA consistently outperforms LoRA and other baselines, achieving up to +8.5% absolute gain in Pass@1 on HumanEval+. These improvements hold across model sizes and rank settings, making GraLoRA a scalable and robust solution for PEFT. Code, data, and scripts are available at https://github.com/SqueezeBits/GraLoRA.git
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）是一种流行的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法，因其简单性和有效性而受到青睐。尽管最近有所改进，LoRA仍然存在一个根本性的局限：当瓶颈被扩大时，容易出现过拟合现象。LoRA在秩为32-64时表现最佳，但其准确性在更高秩的情况下停滞不前或下降，仍无法达到全面微调（FFT）的表现。我们发现LoRA的根源在于其结构瓶颈，它引入了梯度纠缠到无关输入通道，并扭曲了梯度传播。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种新颖的结构——粒度低秩适应（GraLoRA），将权重矩阵分割成子块，每个子块都有自己的低秩适配器。GraLoRA几乎不增加计算或存储成本，就能克服LoRA的限制，有效提高表示能力，并更接近FFT的行为。在代码生成和常识推理基准测试上的实验表明，GraLoRA在所有情况下都优于LoRA和其他基线，在HumanEval+上Pass@1绝对增益最高可达+8.5%。这些改进适用于不同规模的模型和不同的秩设置，使GraLoRA成为PEFT的可扩展且稳健的解决方案。相关代码、数据和脚本可在https://github.com/SqueezeBits/GraLoRA.git获取。

[No.144] LaX: Boosting Low-Rank Training of Foundation Models via Latent Crossing
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: LaX：通过潜在交叉提升基础模型的低秩训练
类别: deep learning
作者: Ruijie ZHANG;Ziyue Liu;Zhengyang Wang;Zheng Zhang;
机构: University of California, Santa Barbara;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=pV17ra3AxZ
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21732
英摘: Training foundation models such as ViTs and LLMs requires tremendous computing cost. Low-rank matrix or tensor factorization offers a parameter-efficient alternative, but often downgrades performance due to the restricted parameter space. In this work, we introduce {\textbf{Latent Crossing (LaX)}} -- a simple yet effective plug-and-play module that enhances the capacity of low-rank models by enabling information flow across low-rank subspaces. We extensively validate the benefits of LaX on pre-training tasks with ViT-Base/Large and LLaMA-like models ranging from 60M to 1B parameters. LaX boosts low-rank model performance to match or exceed the full-rank baselines while using 2-3\(\times\) fewer parameters. When equipped with low-rank adapters (i.e., LoRA) for fine-tuning LLaMA-7/13B, LaX consistently improves performance on arithmetic and common sense reasoning tasks with negligible cost.
中摘: 训练ViTs和LLMs这样的基础模型需要巨大的计算成本。低秩矩阵或张量分解提供了一种参数效率高的替代方案，但由于参数空间受限，往往会降低性能。在本工作中，我们引入了**潜空间交叉（LaX）**——一种简单而有效的即插即用模块，通过在低秩子空间之间启用信息流来增强低秩模型的能力。我们广泛验证了LaX在使用ViT-Base/Large和类似LLaMA的模型（参数范围从60M到1B）进行预训练任务时的优势。LaX能够使低秩模型的性能提升至与全秩基线相当甚至超越，同时仅使用2-3倍 fewer参数。当与低秩适配器（即LoRA）结合用于微调LLaMA-7/13B时，LaX在算术和常识推理任务上始终表现出一致的性能提升，且几乎不增加额外的成本。

[No.145] Low Rank Gradients and Where to Find Them
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩梯度与何处寻得它们
类别: theory
作者: Rishi Sonthalia;Michael Murray;Guido Montufar;
机构: Boston College;University of Bath;University of California, Los Angeles;Max Planck Institute for Mathematics in the Sciences;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=de3kwOXQ9e
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01303
英摘: This paper investigates low-rank structure in the gradients of the training loss for two-layer neural networks while relaxing the usual isotropy assumptions on the training data and parameters. We consider a spiked data model in which the bulk can be anisotropic and ill-conditioned, we do not require independent data and weight matrices and we also analyze both the mean-field and neural-tangent-kernel scalings. We show that the gradient with respect to the input weights is approximately low rank and is dominated by two rank-one terms: one aligned with the bulk data-residue , and another aligned with the rank one spike in the input data. We characterize how properties of the training data, the scaling regime and the activation function govern the balance between these two components. Additionally, we also demonstrate that standard regularizers, such as weight decay, input noise and Jacobian penalties, also selectively modulate these components. Experiments on synthetic and real data corroborate our theoretical predictions.
中摘: 本文研究了两层神经网络训练损失梯度中的低秩结构，同时放松了对训练数据和参数通常的各向同性假设。我们考虑了一种尖峰数据模型，其中主体可以是非各向同性和病态条件的，不需要独立的数据和权重矩阵，并且分析了均场和神经 tangent 核缩放两种情况。我们展示了输入权重的梯度大约为低秩，并且主要由两个秩一项支配：一项与主体数据残差对齐，另一项与输入数据中的秩一尖峰对齐。我们还刻画了训练数据特性、缩放区间和激活函数如何控制这两部分之间的平衡。此外，我们还证明了标准正则化器，如权重衰减、输入噪声和雅可比惩罚，也选择性地调节这些部分。实验结果在合成和真实数据上证实了我们的理论预测。

[No.147] DynamicRAG: Leveraging Outputs of Large Language Model as Feedback for Dynamic Reranking in Retrieval-Augmented Generation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 动态RAG：将大型语言模型的输出作为反馈用于检索增强生成中的动态重排序
类别: applications
作者: Jiashuo Sun;Xianrui Zhong;Sizhe Zhou;Jiawei Han;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;Alibaba Group;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=NuCtKoflsV
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07233
英摘: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems combine large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge retrieval, making them highly effective for knowledge-intensive tasks. A crucial but often under-explored component of these systems is the reranker. Since irrelevant documents in RAG systems can mislead the generator, the reranker plays a vital role in refining retrieved documents to enhance generation quality and explainability. However, it is challenging to determine the appropriate number of documents ($k$) that the reranker should select: too few may result in missing critical information, while too many introduce noise and inefficiencies. Although recent studies have explored LLM-based rerankers, they primarily leverage internal model knowledge and overlook the rich supervisory signals that LLMs can provide, such as using response quality as feedback for optimizing reranking decisions. In this paper, we propose DynamicRAG, a novel RAG framework where the reranker dynamically adjusts both the order and number of retrieved documents based on the query. We model the reranker as an agent optimized through reinforcement learning (RL), using rewards derived from LLM output quality. Across seven knowledge-intensive datasets, DynamicRAG demonstrates superior performance, achieving state-of-the-art results among models of same parameter sizes. The model, data and code are available at https://github.com/GasolSun36/DynamicRAG.
中摘: 检索增强生成（RAG）系统将大型语言模型（LLMs）与外部知识检索相结合，使它们在知识密集型任务中非常有效。这些系统中的一个关键但常常被忽视的组件是重排序器。由于RAG系统中的无关文档可能会误导生成器，因此重排序器在细化检索到的文档以提高生成质量和可解释性方面发挥着至关重要的作用。然而，确定重排序器应选择的适当文档数量（$k$）颇具挑战性：选择太少可能导致丢失关键信息，而选择太多则会引入噪音和低效率。尽管最近的研究已经探索了基于LLM的重排序器，但它们主要依赖于内部模型知识，而忽略了LLMs可以提供的丰富监督信号，例如利用响应质量作为反馈来优化重排序决策。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种名为DynamicRAG的新颖RAG框架，在此框架中，重排序器根据查询动态调整检索到的文档的顺序和数量。我们将重排序器建模为一个通过强化学习（RL）优化的代理，并使用来自LLM输出质量的奖励。在七个知识密集型数据集中，DynamicRAG展示了优越的表现，其性能达到了相同参数量模型中的最佳水平。该模型、数据和代码可在https://github.com/GasolSun36/DynamicRAG获取。

[No.148] AltLoRA: Towards Better Gradient Approximation in Low-Rank Adaptation with Alternating Projections
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: AltLoRA：在交替投影低秩适应中的更好梯度近似
类别: deep learning
作者: Xin Yu;Yujia Wang;Jinghui Chen;Lingzhou Xue;
机构: Pennsylvania State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=9YNJ03jYsU
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12455
英摘: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as an effective technique for reducing memory overhead in fine-tuning large language models. However, it often suffers from sub-optimal performance compared with full fine-tuning since the update is constrained in the low-rank space. Recent variants such as LoRA-Pro attempt to mitigate this by adjusting the gradients of the low-rank matrices to approximate the full gradient. However, LoRA-Pro's solution is not unique, and different solutions can lead to significantly varying performance in ablation studies. Besides, to incorporate momentum or adaptive optimization design, approaches like LoRA-Pro must first compute the equivalent gradient, causing a higher memory cost close to full fine-tuning. A key challenge remains in integrating momentum properly into the low-rank space with lower memory cost. In this work, we propose AltLoRA, an alternating projection method that avoids the difficulties in gradient approximation brought by the joint update design, meanwhile integrating momentum without higher memory complexity. Our theoretical analysis provides convergence guarantees and further shows that AltLoRA enables stable feature learning and robustness to transformation invariance. Extensive experiments across multiple tasks demonstrate that AltLoRA outperforms LoRA and its variants, narrowing the gap toward full fine-tuning while preserving superior memory efficiency.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）已成为一种有效的方法，用于减少大规模语言模型微调时的内存开销。然而，与完全微调相比，它通常会遭受性能不佳的问题，因为更新受到低秩空间的限制。最近的一些变体，如LoRA-Pro，试图通过调整低秩矩阵的梯度来近似完整的梯度，从而缓解这一问题。但是，LoRA-Pro的解决方案并非唯一，不同的解决方案在消融研究中会导致显著不同的性能。此外，为了引入动量或自适应优化设计，像LoRA-Pro这样的方法必须首先计算等效梯度，这会导致接近完全微调的更高内存成本。将动量整合到低秩空间中的关键挑战仍然是以较低的内存复杂度实现。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种交替投影方法——AltLoRA，该方法避免了联合更新设计带来的梯度近似难题，同时无需增加更高的内存复杂度即可整合动量。我们的理论分析提供了收敛性保证，并进一步表明AltLoRA能够实现稳定的特征学习和对变换不变性的鲁棒性。在多个任务上的广泛实验表明，AltLoRA在性能上优于LoRA及其变体，同时保留了优越的内存效率，缩小了与完全微调之间的差距。

[No.149] Training-Free Bayesianization for Low-Rank Adapters of Large Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 无训练贝叶斯化在大规模语言模型中的低秩适配器
类别: probabilistic methods

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05723
英摘: Estimating the uncertainty of responses from Large Language Models (LLMs) remains a critical challenge. While recent Bayesian methods have demonstrated effectiveness in quantifying uncertainty through low-rank weight updates, they typically require complex fine-tuning or post-training procedures. In this paper, we propose Training-Free Bayesianization (TFB), a simple yet theoretically grounded framework that efficiently transforms trained low-rank adapters into Bayesian ones without additional training. TFB systematically searches for the maximally acceptable level of variance in the weight posterior, constrained within a family of low-rank isotropic Gaussian distributions. Our theoretical analysis shows that under mild conditions, this search process is equivalent to KL-regularized variational optimization, a generalized form of variational inference. Through comprehensive experiments, we show that TFB achieves superior uncertainty estimation and generalization compared to existing methods while eliminating the need for complex Bayesianization training procedures. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/bayesian-peft.

英摘: This paper addresses the problem of robust estimation in gossip algorithms over arbitrary communication graphs. Gossip algorithms are fully decentralized, relying only on local neighbor-to-neighbor communication, making them well-suited for situations where communication is constrained. A fundamental challenge in existing mean-based gossip algorithms is their vulnerability to malicious or corrupted nodes. In this paper, we show that an outlier-robust mean can be computed by globally estimating a robust statistic. More specifically, we propose a novel gossip algorithm for rank estimation, referred to as \textsc{GoRank}, and leverage it to design a gossip procedure dedicated to trimmed mean estimation, coined \textsc{GoTrim}. In addition to a detailed description of the proposed methods, a key contribution of our work is a precise convergence analysis: we establish an $\mathcal{O}(1/t)$ rate for rank estimation and an $\mathcal{O}(1 / {t})$ rate for trimmed mean estimation, where by $t$ is meant the number of iterations. Moreover, we provide a breakdown point analysis of \textsc{GoTrim}. We empirically validate our theoretical results through experiments on diverse network topologies, data distributions and contamination schemes.

[No.152] StelLA: Subspace Learning in Low-rank Adaptation using Stiefel Manifold
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: StelLA：低秩适应性Stiefel流形子空间学习
类别: deep learning
作者: Zhizhong Li;Sina Sajadmanesh;Jingtao Li;Lingjuan Lyu;
机构: Sony Europe;Sony;Sony Corporation;
类型: Spotlight

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01938
英摘: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has been widely adopted as a parameter-efficient technique for fine-tuning large-scale pre-trained models. However, it still lags behind full fine-tuning in performance, partly due to its insufficient exploitation of the geometric structure underlying low-rank manifolds. In this paper, we propose a geometry-aware extension of LoRA that uses a three-factor decomposition $U\!SV^\top$. Analogous to the structure of singular value decomposition (SVD), it separates the adapter's input and output subspaces, $V$ and $U$, from the scaling factor $S$. Our method constrains $U$ and $V$ to lie on the Stiefel manifold, ensuring their orthonormality throughout the training. To optimize on the Stiefel manifold, we employ a flexible and modular geometric optimization design that converts any Euclidean optimizer to a Riemannian one. It enables efficient subspace learning while remaining compatible with existing fine-tuning pipelines. Empirical results across a wide range of downstream tasks, including commonsense reasoning, math and code generation, image classification, and image generation, demonstrate the superior performance of our approach against the recent state-of-the-art variants of LoRA. Code is available at https://github.com/SonyResearch/stella.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）已被广泛采用作为一种参数高效的大型预训练模型微调技术。然而，它在性能上仍落后于全面微调，部分原因在于其对低秩流形下隐藏的几何结构的不足利用。在本文中，我们提出了一种几何感知增强的LoRA方法，该方法使用三因子分解$U\!SV^\top$。类似于奇异值分解（SVD）的结构，它将适配器的输入子空间和输出子空间$V$和$U$与缩放因子$S$分离。我们的方法约束$U$和$V$位于Stief尔流形上，确保它们在整个训练过程中保持正交性。为了在Stief尔流形上进行优化，我们采用了灵活且模块化的几何优化设计，将任何欧几里得优化器转换为黎曼优化器。这使得高效子空间学习的同时，仍然与现有的微调管道兼容。我们在多种下游任务上的实证结果，包括常识推理、数学和代码生成、图像分类以及图像生成，表明我们的方法在性能上优于最近的LoRA变体。相关代码可在https://github.com/SonyResearch/stella获得。

[No.153] ResponseRank: Data-Efficient Reward Modeling through Preference Strength Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 数据高效奖励建模通过偏好强度学习
类别: general machine learning
作者: Timo Kaufmann;Yannick Metz;Daniel A. Keim;Eyke Hüllermeier;
机构: Ludwig-Maximilians-Universität München;ETH Zurich;Universität Konstanz;
类型: Poster

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.25023
英摘: Binary choices, as often used for reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), convey only the direction of a preference. A person may choose apples over oranges and bananas over grapes, but which preference is stronger? Strength is crucial for decision-making under uncertainty and generalization of preference models, but hard to measure reliably. Metadata such as response times and inter-annotator agreement can serve as proxies for strength, but are often noisy and confounded. We propose ResponseRank to address the challenge of learning from noisy strength signals. Our method uses relative differences in proxy signals to rank responses to pairwise comparisons by their inferred preference strength. To control for systemic variation, we compare signals only locally within carefully constructed strata. This enables robust learning of utility differences consistent with strength-derived rankings while making minimal assumptions about the strength signal. Our contributions are threefold: (1) ResponseRank, a novel method that robustly learns preference strength by leveraging locally valid relative strength signals; (2) empirical evidence of improved sample efficiency and robustness across diverse tasks: synthetic preference learning (with simulated response times), language modeling (with annotator agreement), and RL control tasks (with simulated episode returns); and (3) the Pearson Distance Correlation (PDC), a novel metric that isolates cardinal utility learning from ordinal accuracy.
中摘: 二元选择，如强化学习从人类反馈（RLHF）中常用的方式，只能传达偏好的方向。一个人可能更喜欢苹果而不是橙子，香蕉而不是葡萄，但哪种偏好更强呢？强度对于在不确定性条件下做出决策和偏好模型的一般化至关重要，但却难以可靠地测量。元数据，如响应时间及注释者一致性，可以作为强度的代理指标，但往往噪声较大且受到多种因素的影响。我们提出了一种名为ResponseRank的方法来应对来自嘈杂强度信号的学习挑战。该方法利用局部有效的相对强度信号对成对比较的响应进行排序，以推断其偏好的强度。为了控制系统性变异，我们仅在精心构建的层内局部比较信号。这使得我们可以稳健地学习与强度衍生排名一致的效用差异，同时对强度信号的假设尽可能少。我们的贡献有三个方面：(1) ResponseRank，一种新颖的方法，通过利用局部有效的相对强度信号稳健地学习偏好强度；(2) 在合成偏好学习（使用模拟的响应时间）、语言建模（使用注释者一致性）和RL控制任务（使用模拟的回合回报）等多种任务上，提供了改进的样本效率和稳健性的实证证据；(3) Pearson距离相关性（PDC），一种新颖的度量标准，能够将序数准确性从基数效用学习中分离出来。

[No.156] Shift Before You Learn: Enabling Low-Rank Representations in Reinforcement Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 学习之前移位：启用强化学习中的低秩表示
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Bastien Dubail;Stefan Stojanovic;Alexandre Proutiere;
机构: KTH Royal Institute of Technology;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=loVvFhfsDf
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.05193
英摘: Low-rank structure is a common implicit assumption in many modern reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms. For instance, reward-free and goal-conditioned RL methods often presume that the successor measure admits a low-rank representation. In this work, we challenge this assumption by first remarking that the successor measure itself is not approximately low-rank. Instead, we demonstrate that a low-rank structure naturally emerges in the shifted successor measure, which captures the system dynamics after bypassing a few initial transitions. We provide finite-sample performance guarantees for the entry-wise estimation of a low-rank approximation of the shifted successor measure from sampled entries. Our analysis reveals that both the approximation and estimation errors are primarily governed by a newly introduced quantitity: the spectral recoverability of the corresponding matrix. To bound this parameter, we derive a new class of functional inequalities for Markov chains that we call Type II Poincaré inequalities and from which we can quantify the amount of shift needed for effective low-rank approximation and estimation. This analysis shows in particular that the required shift depends on decay of the high-order singular values of the shifted successor measure and is hence typically small in practice. Additionally, we establish a connection between the necessary shift and the local mixing properties of the underlying dynamical system, which provides a natural way of selecting the shift. Finally, we validate our theoretical findings with experiments, and demonstrate that shifting the successor measure indeed leads to improved performance in goal-conditioned RL.
中摘: 低秩结构在许多现代强化学习（RL）算法中是一种常见的隐式假设。例如，无奖励和目标条件化的RL方法通常假定后续措施可以近似表示为低秩形式。然而，在这项工作中，我们挑战了这一假设，首先指出后续措施本身并不是近似低秩形式。相反，我们证明了一个低秩结构自然地出现在偏移后的后续措施中，这种偏移后的后续措施捕捉了系统动态，绕过了几轮初始过渡。我们提供了从采样条目估计偏移后的后续措施的低秩近似值的有限样本性能保证。我们的分析揭示了近似误差和估计误差主要由一个新的引入量控制：相应矩阵的光谱可恢复性。为了界这个参数，我们推导出一类新的马尔可夫链函数不等式，称为Type II Poincaré不等式，并从中可以量化所需的偏移量以实现有效的低秩近似和估计。这一分析表明，所需的偏移量依赖于偏移后的后续措施的高阶奇异值的衰减，因此在实践中通常是较小的。此外，我们建立了必要的偏移量与底层动力系统的地方混合性质之间的联系，这提供了一种自然选择偏移量的方法。最后，我们通过实验验证了我们的理论发现，并证明了偏移后续措施确实可以在目标条件化的RL中提高性能。

[No.157] Generalization Bounds for Rank-sparse Neural Networks
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 秩稀疏神经网络的一般化界
类别: theory
作者: Antoine Ledent;Rodrigo Alves;Yunwen Lei;

链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=n3M8h9mqDm
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21945
英摘: It has been recently observed in much of the literature that neural networks exhibit a bottleneck rank property: for larger depths, the activation and weights of neural networks trained with gradient-based methods tend to be of approximately low rank. In fact, the rank of the activations of each layer converges to a fixed value referred to as the ``bottleneck rank'', which is the minimum rank required to represent the training data. This perspective is in line with the observation that regularizing linear networks (without activations) with weight decay is equivalent to minimizing the Schatten $p$ quasi norm of the neural network. In this paper we investigate the implications of this phenomenon for generalization. More specifically, we prove generalization bounds for neural networks which exploit the approximate low rank structure of the weight matrices if present. The final results rely on the Schatten $p$ quasi norms of the weight matrices: for small $p$, the bounds exhibit a sample complexity $ \widetilde{O}(WrL^2)$ where $W$ and $L$ are the width and depth of the neural network respectively and where $r$ is the rank of the weight matrices. As $p$ increases, the bound behaves more like a norm-based bound instead.
中摘: 近年来，许多文献观察到神经网络表现出瓶颈秩特性：对于更深的网络，在基于梯度的方法训练时，神经网络的激活和权重倾向于接近低秩。实际上，每一层的激活秩会收敛到一个固定值，称为“瓶颈秩”，这是表示训练数据所需的最小秩。这一观点与正则化线性网络（不包含激活函数）的方法等同于最小化神经网络的Schatten $p$准范数的观察相符。在本文中，我们探讨了这种现象对泛化的含义。更具体地说，我们证明了如果权重矩阵接近低秩结构，则神经网络泛化误差界。最终的结果依赖于权重矩阵的Schatten $p$准范数：当$p$较小时，误差界表现出样本复杂度$\widetilde{O}(WrL^2)$，其中$W$和$L$分别是神经网络的宽度和深度，而$r$是权重矩阵的秩。随着$p$增大，误差界的行为更类似于基于范数的误差界。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[No.159] Language Ranker: A Lightweight Ranking framework for LLM Decoding
来源: NeurIPS 2025

标题: 语言排名器：一种轻量级的LLM解码排序框架

英摘: Conventional research on large language models (LLMs) has primarily focused on refining output distributions, while paying less attention to the decoding process that transforms these distributions into final responses. Recent advances, such as scaling the computation of inference time with reward models, have underscored the importance of decoding, but these methods often suffer from high computational costs and limited applicability. In this paper, we revisit LLM generation through the lens of recommender systems, conceptualizing the decoding process as analogous to the ranking stage in recommendation pipelines. From this perspective, we observe that both traditional decoding methods and reward models exhibit clear limitations such as redundancy. Motivated by this insight, we propose Language Ranker, a novel framework that introduces a lightweight module to rerank candidate responses using features extracted by the base model. Experiments across a wide range of tasks show that Language Ranker achieves performance comparable to large-scale reward models, while requiring only <0.5M additional parameters, significantly reducing the computational overhead during both training and inference stages. This highlights the efficiency and effectiveness of our method, showcasing its potential to fully unlock the capabilities of LLMs.
中摘: 传统的大语言模型（LLMs）研究主要集中在细化输出分布上，而较少关注将这些分布转化为最终响应的解码过程。最近的进展，如通过奖励模型扩展推理时间的计算，强调了解码的重要性，但这些方法往往面临高计算成本和适用性有限的问题。在本文中，我们从推荐系统的角度重新审视LLM生成过程，将解码过程类比于推荐管道中的排序阶段。从这一视角出发，我们观察到传统的解码方法和奖励模型都存在明显的局限性，如冗余问题。基于这一洞察，我们提出了一种名为Language Ranker的新框架，该框架引入了一个轻量级模块，利用基模型提取的特征对候选响应进行重排序。实验结果显示，Language Ranker在多种任务上的性能与大规模奖励模型相当，但仅需增加不到0.5M的额外参数，显著减少了训练和推理阶段的计算开销。这突显了我们方法的高效性和有效性，展示了其潜在能力以完全释放大语言模型的全部功能。

[No.163] MINGLE: Mixture of Null-Space Gated Low-Rank Experts for Test-Time Continual Model Merging
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: MINGLE：混合空域门控低秩专家用于测试时持续模型合并
类别: general machine learning
作者: Zihuan Qiu;Yi Xu;Chiyuan He;Fanman Meng;Linfeng Xu;Qingbo Wu;Hongliang Li;
机构: University of Electronic Science and Technology of China;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=8DCyv8x58O
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11883
英摘: Continual model merging integrates independently fine-tuned models sequentially without access to the original training data, offering a scalable and efficient solution for continual learning. However, existing methods face two critical challenges: parameter interference among tasks, which leads to catastrophic forgetting, and limited adaptability to evolving test distributions. To address these issues, we introduce the task of Test-Time Continual Model Merging (TTCMM), which leverages a small set of unlabeled test samples during inference to alleviate parameter conflicts and handle distribution shifts. We propose MINGLE, a novel framework for TTCMM. MINGLE employs a mixture-of-experts architecture with parameter-efficient, low-rank experts, which enhances adaptability to evolving test distributions while dynamically merging models to mitigate conflicts. To further reduce forgetting, we propose Null-Space Constrained Gating, which restricts gating updates to subspaces orthogonal to prior task representations, thereby suppressing activations on old tasks and preserving past knowledge. We further introduce an Adaptive Relaxation Strategy that adjusts constraint strength dynamically based on interference signals observed during test-time adaptation, striking a balance between stability and adaptability. Extensive experiments on standard continual merging benchmarks demonstrate that MINGLE achieves robust generalization, significantly reduces forgetting, and consistently surpasses previous state-of-the-art methods by 7-9% on average across diverse task orders. Our code is available at: https://github.com/zihuanqiu/MINGLE
中摘: 连续模型合并通过在推理过程中顺序集成独立微调的模型而无需访问原始训练数据，提供了一种可扩展且高效的持续学习解决方案。然而，现有方法面临两个关键挑战：任务参数之间的干扰，导致灾难性遗忘，以及对不断变化的测试分布的有限适应能力。为了解决这些问题，我们引入了Test-Time Continual Model Merging（TTCMM）任务，该任务利用少量未标记的测试样本进行推理，以缓解参数冲突并处理分布偏移。我们提出了MINGLE，一种新的TTCMM框架。MINGLE采用专家混合架构，其中包含参数高效、低秩专家，这增强了对不断变化的测试分布的适应能力，同时动态地合并模型以减轻冲突。为了进一步减少遗忘，我们提出了Null-Space Constrained Gating，该方法限制门控更新仅限于与先前任务表示正交的子空间，从而抑制旧任务上的激活并保留以往的知识。我们还引入了一种自适应松弛策略，在测试时根据观察到的任务干扰信号动态调整约束强度，平衡稳定性和适应性。在标准连续模型合并基准上的广泛实验表明，MINGLE实现了稳健的一般化，显著减少了遗忘现象，并且在不同任务顺序上平均比以前的最佳方法高出7-9%。我们的代码可在以下链接获取：https://github.com/zihuanqiu/MINGLE

[No.164] Dynamical Low-Rank Compression of Neural Networks with Robustness under Adversarial Attacks
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 动态低秩压缩在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性神经网络

作者: Steffen Schotthöfer;H. Lexie Yang;Stefan Schnake;
机构: Oak Ridge National Laboratory;
类型: Oral
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=7AwFJzgIUW
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08022
英摘: Deployment of neural networks on resource-constrained devices demands models that are both compact and robust to adversarial inputs. However, compression and adversarial robustness often conflict. In this work, we introduce a dynamical low-rank training scheme enhanced with a novel spectral regularizer that controls the condition number of the low-rank core in each layer. This approach mitigates the sensitivity of compressed models to adversarial perturbations without sacrificing accuracy on clean data. The method is model- and data-agnostic, computationally efficient, and supports rank adaptivity to automatically compress the network at hand. [No.165] Q3R: Quadratic Reweighted Rank Regularizer for Effective Low-Rank Training
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: Q3R：二次加权秩正则化器，用于有效的低秩训练
类别: deep learning
作者: Ipsita Ghosh;Ethan Nguyen;Christian Kümmerle;
机构: University of North Carolina at Charlotte;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ZtzWvNKOCr

英摘: Parameter-efficient training, based on low-rank optimization, has become a highly successful tool for fine-tuning large deep-learning models. However, these methods fail at low-rank pre-training tasks where maintaining the low-rank structure and the objective remains a challenging task. We propose the Quadratic Reweighted Rank Regularizer dubbed Q3R, which leads to a novel low-rank inducing training strategy inspired by the iteratively reweighted least squares (IRLS) framework. Q3R is based on a quadratic regularizer term which majorizes a smoothed log determinant serving as rank surrogate objective. Unlike other low-rank training techniques, Q3R is able to train weight matrices with prescribed, low target ranks of models that achieve comparable predictive performance as dense models, with small computational overhead, while remaining fully compatible with existing architectures. For example, we demonstrated one experiment where we are able to truncate $60\%$ and $80\%$ of the parameters of a ViT-Tiny model with $~1.3\%$ and $~4\%$ accuracy drop in CIFAR-10 performance respectively. The efficacy of Q3R is confirmed on Transformers across both image and language tasks, including for low-rank fine-tuning.
中摘: 参数效率训练，基于低秩优化，已成为一种高度成功的大型深度学习模型微调工具。然而，这些方法在低秩预训练任务中失败，因为保持低秩结构和目标函数仍然是一个具有挑战性的任务。我们提出了一种名为Q3R的二次重权低秩正则化器，该正则化器借鉴了迭代加权最小二乘（IRLS）框架，导致了一种新颖的低秩诱导训练策略。Q3R基于一个二次正则项，该正则项主要化一个平滑后的对数行列式，作为秩替代目标函数。与其它低秩训练技术不同，Q3R能够在保持与密集模型相当的预测性能的同时，训练具有指定低目标秩的权重矩阵，并且具有较小的计算开销，同时仍然完全兼容现有的架构。例如，在一项实验中，我们能够将一个约有1.3万个参数的ViT-Tiny模型的参数截断60%和80%，分别在CIFAR-10性能上仅损失约1.3%和4%的准确率。Q3R的有效性已在图像和语言任务中的Transformer模型中得到验证，包括低秩微调。

[No.166] Transductive Conformal Inference for Full Ranking
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 全传递性区间预测全面排名
类别: general machine learning
作者: Jean-Baptiste Fermanian;Pierre Humbert;Gilles Blanchard;
机构: University of Montpelier;Sorbonne Université;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=3Bxn5XSgrO
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.11384
英摘: We introduce a method based on Conformal Prediction (CP) to quantify the uncertainty of full ranking algorithms. We focus on a specific scenario where $n+m$ items are to be ranked by some ``black box'' algorithm. It is assumed that the relative (ground truth) ranking of $n$ of them is known. The objective is then to quantify the error made by the algorithm on the ranks of the $m$ new items among the total $(n+m)$. In such a setting, the true ranks of the $n$ original items in the total $(n+m)$ depend on the (unknown) true ranks of the $m$ new ones. Consequently, we have no direct access to a calibration set to apply a classical CP method. To address this challenge, we propose to construct distribution-free bounds of the unknown conformity scores using recent results on the distribution of conformal p-values. Using these scores upper bounds, we provide valid prediction sets for the rank of any item. We also control the false coverage proportion, a crucial quantity when dealing with multiple prediction sets. Finally, we empirically show on both synthetic and real data the efficiency of our CP method for state-of-the-art algorithms such as RankNet or LambdaMart.
中摘: 我们提出了一种基于同构预测（Conformal Prediction，CP）的方法来量化全排序算法的不确定性。我们关注一种特定场景，即由某个“黑盒”算法对$n+m$个项目进行排序。假设其中$n$个项目的相对（真实）排名已知。目标是通过量化算法在$(n+m)$个总项目中对$m$个新项目的排序错误，来评估算法的排序误差。在这种情况下，$n$个原始项目的真正排名依赖于未知的$m$个新项目的真正排名。因此，我们无法直接访问用于校准的经典CP方法所需的校准集。为应对这一挑战，我们建议利用最近关于同构p值分布的结果，构造未知一致性分数的无分布边界。使用这些分数上界，我们可以提供任何项目的排名的有效预测集。同时，我们控制了假覆盖比例，这是处理多个预测集时的一个关键量。最后，我们在合成数据和实际数据上实验证明，对于如RankNet或LambdaMart这类最先进的算法，我们的CP方法具有高效性。

[No.167] Differentially Private Federated Low Rank Adaptation Beyond Fixed-Matrix
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 差分隐私联邦低秩适应超越固定矩阵
类别: deep learning
作者: Ming Wen;Jiaqi Zhu;Yuedong Xu;Yipeng Zhou;DINGDING HAN;
机构: ;Fudan University;Macquarie University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=TecJ926Vgn
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.09990
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) typically require fine-tuning for domain-specific tasks, and LoRA offers a computationally efficient approach by training low-rank adapters. LoRA is also communication-efficient for federated LLMs when multiple users collaboratively fine-tune a global LLM model without sharing their proprietary raw data. However, even the transmission of local adapters between a server and clients risks serious privacy leakage. Applying differential privacy (DP) to federated LoRA encounters a dilemma: adding noise to both adapters amplifies synthetic noise on the model, while fixing one adapter impairs the learnability of fine-tuning. In this paper, we propose FedASK (Differentially Private Federated Low Rank Adaptation with Double Sketching) , a novel federated LoRA framework to enable effective updating of both low-rank adapters with robust differential privacy. Inspired by randomized SVD, our key idea is a two-stage sketching pipeline. This pipeline first aggregates carefully sketched, privacy-preserving local updates, and then reconstructs the global matrices on the server to facilitate effective updating of both adapters. We theoretically prove FedASK's differential privacy guarantee and its exact aggregation property. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that FedASK consistently outperforms baseline methods across a variety of privacy settings and data distributions.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）通常需要针对特定领域进行微调，而LoRA提供了一种计算效率高的方法，通过训练低秩适配器来实现这一目标。当多个用户不共享其专有的原始数据协作微调全局LLM模型时，LoRA还具有通信效率高的特点。然而，即使在服务器与客户端之间传输本地适配器，也存在严重的隐私泄露风险。将差分隐私（DP）应用于联邦LoRA会遇到一个难题：对适配器添加噪声会放大模型上的合成噪声，而固定一个适配器则会损害微调的学习能力。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为FedASK（基于双素描的差分隐私联邦低秩适应）的新颖联邦LoRA框架，以实现对两个低秩适配器的有效更新，并具备稳健的差分隐私保障。该框架受到随机化SVD的启发，采用两阶段素描流水线。首先，该流水线聚合了精心素描的、隐私保护的局部更新；然后，在服务器上重建全局矩阵，以便于两个适配器的有效更新。我们从理论上证明了FedASK的差分隐私保证及其精确聚合特性。全面的实验表明，无论是在不同的隐私设置还是数据分布下，FedASK都能一致地优于基线方法。
