arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.18353
英摘: Direct preference optimization (DPO) methods have shown strong potential in aligning text-to-image diffusion models with human preferences by training on paired comparisons. These methods improve training stability by avoiding the REINFORCE algorithm but still struggle with challenges such as accurately estimating image probabilities due to the non-linear nature of the sigmoid function and the limited diversity of offline datasets. In this paper, we introduce Diffusion Denoising Ranking Optimization (Diffusion-DRO), a new preference learning framework grounded in inverse reinforcement learning. Diffusion-DRO removes the dependency on a reward model by casting preference learning as a ranking problem, thereby simplifying the training objective into a denoising formulation and overcoming the non-linear estimation issues found in prior methods. Moreover, Diffusion-DRO uniquely integrates offline expert demonstrations with online policy-generated negative samples, enabling it to effectively capture human preferences while addressing the limitations of offline data. Comprehensive experiments show that Diffusion-DRO delivers improved generation quality across a range of challenging and unseen prompts, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines in both both quantitative metrics and user studies. Our source code and pre-trained models are available at https://github.com/basiclab/DiffusionDRO.
中摘: 直接偏好优化（DPO）方法通过配对比较进行训练，在调整文本到图像扩散模型与人类偏好之间的对齐方面展现了强大的潜力。这些方法通过避免使用REINFORCE算法来提高训练稳定性，但仍面临挑战，如由于Sigmoid函数的非线性性质和离线数据集的有限多样性，准确估计图像概率的问题。在本文中，我们引入了一种新的偏好学习框架——扩散去噪排名优化（Diffusion-DRO），该框架基于逆强化学习。Diffusion-DRO通过将偏好学习问题转化为排名问题，去除了对奖励模型的依赖，从而将训练目标简化为去噪形式，并克服了先前方法中存在的非线性估计问题。此外，Diffusion-DRO独特地结合了离线专家演示与在线策略生成的负样本，使其能够有效地捕捉人类偏好并解决离线数据的局限性。全面的实验表明，Diffusion-DRO在多种挑战性和未见过的提示下提供了改进的生成质量，在定量指标和用户研究中均优于最先进的基线方法。我们的源代码和预训练模型可在https://github.com/basiclab/DiffusionDRO获取。

[No.170] Perturbation Bounds for Low-Rank Inverse Approximations under Noise
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 噪声条件下低秩逆近似扰动界
类别: optimization
作者: Phuc Tran;Nisheeth K. Vishnoi;
机构: Yale University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=bPNzBXl1n7
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25571
英摘: Low-rank pseudoinverses are widely used to approximate matrix inverses in scalable machine learning, optimization, and scientific computing. However, real-world matrices are often observed with noise, arising from sampling, sketching, and quantization. The spectral-norm robustness of low-rank inverse approximations remains poorly understood. We systematically study the spectral-norm error $\| (\tilde{A}^{-1})_p - A_p^{-1} \|$ for an $n\times n$ symmetric matrix $A$, where $A_p^{-1}$ denotes the best rank-\(p\) approximation of $A^{-1}$, and $\tilde{A} = A + E$ is a noisy observation. Under mild assumptions on the noise, we derive sharp non-asymptotic perturbation bounds that reveal how the error scales with the eigengap, spectral decay, and noise alignment with low-curvature directions of $A$. Our analysis introduces a novel application of contour integral techniques to the \emph{non-entire} function $f(z) = 1/z$, yielding bounds that improve over naive adaptations of classical full-inverse bounds by up to a factor of $\sqrt{n}$. Empirically, our bounds closely track the true perturbation error across a variety of real-world and synthetic matrices, while estimates based on classical results tend to significantly overpredict. These findings offer practical, spectrum-aware guarantees for low-rank inverse approximations in noisy computational environments.
中摘: 低秩伪逆在可扩展机器学习、优化和科学计算中广泛用于近似矩阵逆。然而，现实世界中的矩阵通常受到噪声的影响，这些噪声来源于采样、压缩和量化。低秩逆近似的谱范数鲁棒性尚未得到充分理解。我们系统地研究了$n \times n$对称矩阵$A$的谱范数误差$\| (\tilde{A}^{-1})_p - A_p^{-1} \|$，其中$A_p^{-1}$表示$A^{-1}$的最佳秩-$p$逼近，而$\tilde{A} = A + E$是一个有噪声的观测值。在轻微的噪声假设下，我们推导出精确的非渐近扰动界，揭示了误差如何随特征间隙、谱衰减以及噪声与矩阵$A$低曲率方向的对齐程度而变化。我们的分析引入了一种新的方法，将轮廓积分技术应用于非整函数$f(z) = 1/z$，从而获得了比经典全逆矩阵界改进了最多$\sqrt{n}$倍的界。实验结果显示，我们的界能够紧密跟踪各种实际和合成矩阵的真实扰动误差，而基于经典结果的估计往往严重高估了误差。这些发现为噪声计算环境中低秩逆近似提供了实用且谱感知的保证。

[No.171] Cooperative Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Question Answering: Mutual Information Exchange and Ranking by Contrasting Layers
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 协作检索增强生成问答：对比层间的互信息交换与排名
类别: applications
作者: Youmin Ko;Sung Jong Seo;Hyunjoon Kim;
机构: Hanyang University;
类型: Poster

英摘: Since large language models (LLMs) have a tendency to generate factually inaccurate output, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has gained significant attention as a key means to mitigate this downside of harnessing only LLMs. However, existing RAG methods for simple and multi-hop question answering (QA) are still prone to incorrect retrievals and hallucinations. To address these limitations, we propose CoopRAG, a novel RAG framework for the question answering task in which a retriever and an LLM work cooperatively with each other by exchanging informative knowledge, and the earlier and later layers of the retriever model work cooperatively with each other to accurately rank the retrieved documents relevant to a given query. In this framework, we (i) unroll a question into sub-questions and a reasoning chain in which uncertain positions are masked, (ii) retrieve the documents relevant to the question augmented with the sub-questions and the reasoning chain, (iii) rerank the documents by contrasting layers of the retriever, and (iv) reconstruct the reasoning chain by filling the masked positions via the LLM. Our experiments demonstrate that CoopRAG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art QA methods on three multi-hop QA datasets as well as a simple QA dataset in terms of both the retrieval and QA performances. Our code is available.
中摘: 由于大型语言模型（LLMs）倾向于生成事实性不准确的输出，检索增强生成（RAG）已成为减轻仅使用LLM带来的这一缺点的关键方法。然而，现有的用于简单和多跳问答（QA）的RAG方法仍然容易出现错误检索和幻觉的问题。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种名为CoopRAG的新颖RAG框架，用于问答任务，在该框架中，检索器和LLM通过互相交换信息性的知识进行合作，并且检索器模型的早期层和后期层相互合作，以准确地对与给定查询相关的检索文档进行排名。在这个框架中，我们（i）将问题展开为子问题和推理链，其中不确定的位置被遮掩，（ii）根据子问题和推理链检索与问题相关的重要文档，（iii）通过对比检索器的不同层重新排名文档，（iv）利用LLM填充遮掩位置来重建推理链。我们的实验表明，CoopRAG在三个多跳问答数据集以及一个简单问答数据集上，在检索性能和问答性能方面均能一致地优于最先进的QA方法。我们的代码已开源。

英摘: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have emphasized the critical role of fine-tuning (FT) techniques in adapting LLMs to specific tasks, especially when retraining from scratch is computationally infeasible. However, conventional FT approaches often suffer from catastrophic forgetting and suboptimal data efficiency, limiting their real-world applicability. To address these challenges, this paper proposes \textbf{DEAL}, a novel framework that integrates Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) with a continuous fine-tuning strategy. [No.176] Matching Markets Meet LLMs: Algorithmic Reasoning with Ranked Preferences

英摘: The rise of Large Language Models (LLMs) has driven progress in reasoning tasks -- from program synthesis to scientific hypothesis generation -- yet their ability to handle ranked preferences and structured algorithms in combinatorial domains remains underexplored. [No.177] Scalable In-context Ranking with Generative Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于生成模型的可扩展上下文排名
类别: deep learning
作者: Nilesh Gupta;Chong You;Srinadh Bhojanapalli;Sanjiv Kumar;Inderjit S Dhillon;Felix X. Yu;
机构: University of Texas at Austin;;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=zj45hoQhjD
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05396
英摘: In-context Ranking (ICR) is an emerging paradigm for Information Retrieval (IR), which leverages contextual understanding of LLMs by directly incorporating the task description, candidate documents, and the query into the model's input prompt and tasking the LLM to identify relevant document(s). While it is effective, efficiency is a significant challenge in this paradigm, especially as the candidate list grows due to quadratic/super-linear scaling of attention operation with context length. To this end, this paper first identifies inherent and exploitable structures in the attention of LLMs finetuned for ICR: (1) inter-document block sparsity: attention is dense within each document block but sparse across different documents in the context; and (2) query-document block relevance: the attention scores from certain query tokens to a document block in middle layers strongly correlate with that document's actual relevance. Motivated by these observations, we introduce BlockRank (Blockwise In-context Ranking), a novel method that adapts the attention operation in an LLM by (a) architecturally enforcing the observed inter-document block sparsity, reducing attention complexity from quadratic to linear without loss in performance, and (b) optimizing query-document block relevance for true relevant documents during fine-tuning using an auxiliary contrastive training objective, improving retrieval in attention. Experiments on BEIR, MSMarco and NQ with Mistral-7B demonstrate that BlockRank Mistral matches or outperforms existing SOTA listwise rankers and controlled fine-tuned baseline while being significantly more efficient at inference (4.7x for 100 MSMarco documents in context) and scaling gracefully to long-context shortlists, around 500 documents in-context (approximately 100K context length) within a second, presenting a scalable and effective solution for ICR.
中摘: 在上下文中排名（In-context Ranking, ICR）是一种新兴的信息检索（Information Retrieval, IR）范式，它通过直接将任务描述、候选文档和查询纳入模型的输入提示中，并让大规模语言模型（LLM）识别相关文档来利用LLM的上下文理解能力。尽管这种范式有效，但在这一范式中效率是一个显著的挑战，尤其是在候选文档列表增长时，因为注意力操作随上下文长度呈二次或超线性扩展。为此，本文首先识别出针对ICR微调的LLM中固有的可利用结构：（1）文档块间稀疏性：在每个文档块内部注意力是密集的，但在不同文档之间的注意力是稀疏的；（2）查询-文档块相关性：某些查询标记在中间层对特定文档块的关注分数与该文档的实际相关性高度相关。基于这些观察，我们引入了BlockRank（块级在上下文中排名），这是一种新颖的方法，通过（a）架构上强制执行观察到的文档块间稀疏性，将注意力复杂度从二次降低到线性，同时保持性能不变；（b）在微调过程中使用辅助对比训练目标优化查询-文档块相关性，以提高对真正相关文档的检索效果。在BEIR、MSMarco和NQ数据集上的实验表明，BlockRank Mistral在推理效率上显著优于现有最先进的列表式排名方法和受控微调基线（对于上下文中的100个MSMarco文档，推理效率提高了4.7倍），并且能够平滑地扩展到长上下文短列表，大约在一秒内处理上下文中的500个文档（约10万上下文长度），提供了一种可扩展且有效的ICR解决方案。

[No.178] Aligning Transformers with Continuous Feedback via Energy Rank Alignment
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 通过能量排名对齐调整变压器以实现连续反馈
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Shriram Chennakesavalu;Frank Hu;Sebastian Ibarraran;Grant M. Rotskoff;
机构: Stanford University;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12961
英摘: Searching through chemical space is an exceptionally challenging problem because the number of possible molecules grows combinatorially with the number of atoms. Large, autoregressive models trained on databases of chemical compounds have yielded powerful generators, but we still lack robust strategies for generating molecules with desired properties. This molecular search problem closely resembles the "alignment" problem for large language models, though for many chemical tasks we have a specific and easily evaluable reward function. Here, we introduce an algorithm called energy rank alignment (ERA) that leverages an explicit reward function to produce a gradient-based objective that we use to optimize autoregressive policies. We show theoretically that this algorithm is closely related to proximal policy optimization (PPO) and direct preference optimization (DPO), but has a minimizer that converges to an ideal Gibbs-Boltzmann distribution with the reward playing the role of an energy function. Furthermore, this algorithm is highly scalable, does not require reinforcement learning, and performs well relative to DPO when the number of preference observations per pairing is small. We deploy this approach to align molecular transformers and protein language models to generate molecules and protein sequences, respectively, with externally specified properties and find that it does so robustly, searching through diverse parts of chemical space.
中摘: 搜索化学空间是一个极其具有挑战性的问题，因为可能的分子数量随着原子数量的增加呈组合性增长。大型自回归模型在化学化合物数据库上的训练已经产生了强大的生成器，但我们仍然缺乏生成具有所需性质分子的稳健策略。这种分子搜索问题与大型语言模型的“对齐”问题相似，尽管对于许多化学任务，我们有一个具体且易于评估的奖励函数。在这里，我们引入了一种名为能量排名对齐（ERA）的算法，该算法利用显式的奖励函数来产生基于梯度的目标，用于优化自回归策略。理论上，我们证明该算法与近端策略优化（PPO）和直接偏好优化（DPO）密切相关，但其最小化目标会收敛到理想的吉布斯-玻尔兹曼分布，其中奖励充当能量函数的角色。此外，该算法高度可扩展，不需要强化学习，并且当每对观测的偏好观察较少时，相对于DPO表现出色。我们采用这种方法将分子转换器和蛋白质语言模型对齐，以分别生成具有外部指定性质的分子和蛋白质序列，并发现它能够稳健地进行搜索，探索化学空间的多样化部分。

[No.179] Breaking the Frozen Subspace: Importance Sampling for Low-Rank Optimization in LLM Pretraining
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: rank
标题: 打破冻结子空间：在大语言模型预训练中的低秩优化重要采样
类别: deep learning
作者: Haochen Zhang;Junze Yin;Guanchu Wang;Zirui Liu;Lin Yang;Tianyi Zhang;Anshumali Shrivastava;Vladimir Braverman;
机构: Rice University;University of North Carolina at Charlotte;University of Minnesota;;Workato;ThirdAI Corp.;UT Health;Google;Johns Hopkins University;

链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=ZdmmOAN4h3
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05790
英摘: Low-rank optimization has emerged as a promising approach to enabling memory-efficient training of large language models (LLMs). Existing low-rank optimization methods typically project gradients onto a low-rank subspace, reducing the memory cost of storing optimizer states. A key challenge in these methods is selecting suitable subspaces to ensure an effective optimization trajectory. Most existing approaches select the dominant subspace to preserve gradient information, as this intuitively provides the best approximation. However, we find that in practice, the dominant subspace stops changing during pretraining, thereby constraining weight updates to similar subspaces. In this paper, we propose importance sampling for low-rank optimization in LLM pretraining with a provable convergence guarantee, which the dominant subspace approach does not have. Empirically, we demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms previous methods in LLM pretraining tasks.
中摘: 低秩优化已成为一种有前途的方法，用于实现大型语言模型（LLM）的内存高效训练。现有的低秩优化方法通常将梯度投影到低秩子空间，从而减少存储优化器状态所需的内存成本。这些方法中的一个关键挑战是在选择合适的子空间以确保有效的优化轨迹方面。大多数现有方法选择主导子空间来保留梯度信息，因为这直观上提供了最佳的近似。然而，我们发现，在实践中，主导子空间在预训练过程中停止变化，从而限制了权重更新到相似的子空间。在本文中，我们提出了一种在LLM预训练中使用重要性采样的低秩优化方法，并且该方法具有可证明的收敛保证，而主导子空间方法不具备这一特性。实验结果表明，我们的方法在LLM预训练任务中显著优于以前的方法。

[No.181] DR-Encoder: Encode Low-rank Gradients with Random Prior for Large Language Models Differentially Privately

英摘: The emergence of the Large Language Model (LLM) has shown their superiority in a wide range of disciplines, including language understanding and translation, relational logic reasoning, and even partial differential equations solving. The transformer is the pervasive backbone architecture for the foundation model construction. It is vital to research how to adjust the Transformer architecture to achieve an end-to-end privacy guarantee in LLM fine-tuning. In this paper, we investigate three potential information leakage during a federated fine-tuning procedure for LLM (FedLLM). Based on the potential information leakage, we provide an end-to-end privacy guarantee solution for FedLLM by inserting two-stage randomness. [No.182] Language Ranker: A Metric for Quantifying LLM Performance Across High and Low-Resource Languages
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 语言排名器：一种衡量跨高资源和低资源语言的LLM性能指标

作者: Zihao Li;Yucheng Shi;Zirui Liu;Fan Yang;Ali Payani;Ninghao Liu;Mengnan Du;

By comparing the LLM's internal representation of various languages against a baseline derived from English, we can assess the model's multilingual capabilities in a robust and language-agnostic manner. Our analysis reveals that high-resource languages exhibit higher similarity scores with English, demonstrating superior performance, while low-resource languages show lower similarity scores, underscoring the effectiveness of our metric in assessing language-specific capabilities. [No.185] Privacy-Preserving Low-Rank Adaptation Against Membership Inference Attacks for Latent Diffusion Models
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 隐私保护低秩适应以抵御成员推断攻击的潜在扩散模型
类别: computer vision v
作者: Zihao Luo;Xilie Xu;Feng Liu;Yun Sing Koh;Di Wang;Jingfeng Zhang;
机构: University of Auckland;National University of Singapore;University of Melbourne;King Abdullah University of Science and Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32628
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.11989
英摘: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is an efficient strategy for adapting latent diffusion models (LDMs) on a private dataset to generate specific images by minimizing the adaptation loss. However, the LoRA-adapted LDMs are vulnerable to membership inference (MI) attacks that can judge whether a particular data point belongs to the private dataset, thus leading to the privacy leakage. To defend against MI attacks, we first propose a straightforward solution: Membership-Privacy-preserving LoRA (MP-LoRA). MP-LoRA is formulated as a min-max optimization problem where a proxy attack model is trained by maximizing its MI gain while the LDM is adapted by minimizing the sum of the adaptation loss and the MI gain of the proxy attack model. However, we empirically find that MP-LoRA has the issue of unstable optimization, and theoretically analyze that the potential reason is the unconstrained local smoothness, which impedes the privacy-preserving adaptation. To mitigate this issue, we further propose a Stable Membership-Privacy-preserving LoRA (SMP-LoRA) that adapts the LDM by minimizing the ratio of the adaptation loss to the MI gain. Besides, we theoretically prove that the local smoothness of SMP-LoRA can be constrained by the gradient norm, leading to improved convergence. Our experimental results corroborate that SMP-LoRA can indeed defend against MI attacks and generate high-quality images. Our Code is available at \url{https://github.com/WilliamLUO0/StablePrivateLoRA}.
中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）是一种高效策略，用于通过最小化适应损失来在私人数据集上适应潜在扩散模型（LDMs），从而生成特定图像。然而，LoRA适应的LDMs容易受到成员推理（MI）攻击的影响，这些攻击能够判断某个特定数据点是否属于私人数据集，从而导致隐私泄露。为了防御MI攻击，我们首先提出了一种简单解决方案：成员隐私保护LoRA（MP-LoRA）。MP-LoRA被表述为一个最小最大优化问题，在这个问题中，代理攻击模型通过最大化其MI收益来训练，而LDM则通过最小化适应损失和代理攻击模型的MI收益之和来进行适应。然而，我们实证发现MP-LoRA存在优化不稳定的问题，并从理论上分析其潜在原因是未约束的局部光滑性，这妨碍了隐私保护的适应。为缓解这一问题，我们进一步提出了稳定成员隐私保护LoRA（SMP-LoRA），通过最小化适应损失与MI收益的比例来适应LDM。此外，我们从理论上证明SMP-LoRA的局部光滑性可以通过梯度范数进行约束，从而提高收敛性。我们的实验结果证实，SMP-LoRA确实能够防御MI攻击并生成高质量的图像。我们的代码可在<https://github.com/WilliamLUO0/StablePrivateLoRA> 获取。

[No.186] CaRDiff: Video Salient Object Ranking Chain of Thought Reasoning for Saliency Prediction with Diffusion
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 卡迪夫：视频显著对象排名扩散链推理在显著性预测中的应用

英摘: Video saliency prediction aims to identify the regions in a video that attract human attention and gaze, driven by bottom-up features from the video and top-down processes like memory and cognition. Among these top-down influences, language plays a crucial role in guiding attention by shaping how visual information is interpreted. Existing methods primarily focus on modeling perceptual information while neglecting the reasoning process facilitated by language, where ranking cues are crucial outcomes of this process and practical guidance for saliency prediction. Extensive experiments show the effectiveness of VSOR-CoT in improving the performance of video saliency prediction. 中摘: 视频显著性预测旨在识别视频中吸引人类注意力和注视的区域，这受到视频中的底上特征和记忆、认知等顶下过程的影响。在这类顶下影响中，语言在通过塑造视觉信息的解释方式来引导注意力方面扮演着关键角色。现有方法主要侧重于建模感知信息，而忽视了语言促进的推理过程，在这一过程中，排名线索是至关重要的输出结果，并且为显著性预测提供了实际指导。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为CaRDiff（Caption, Rank, and generate with Diffusion）的框架，该框架通过整合多模态大语言模型（MLLM）、定位模块和扩散模型，来增强视频显著性预测。具体而言，我们引入了一种新颖的提示方法VSOR-CoT（Video Salient Object Ranking Chain of Thought），该方法利用带有定位模块的大语言模型对视频内容进行描述，并推断显著对象及其排名和位置。这个过程生成的排名图可以充分被扩散模型利用，以准确解码给定视频的显著性图。大量实验表明，VSOR-CoT在提高视频显著性预测性能方面非常有效。所提出的CaRDiff在MVS数据集上的表现优于最先进的模型，并且通过零样本评估在DHF1k数据集上展示了跨数据集的能力。

[No.190] Attack-in-the-Chain: Bootstrapping Large Language Models for Attacks Against Black-Box Neural Ranking Models
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 攻击链：为针对黑盒神经排名模型的攻击启动大型语言模型
类别: data mining knowledge management ii
作者: Yu-An Liu;Ruqing Zhang;Jiafeng Guo;Maarten de Rijke;Yixing Fan;Xueqi Cheng;

英摘: Neural ranking models (NRMs) have been shown to be highly effective in terms of retrieval performance. Unfortunately, they have also displayed a higher degree of sensitivity to attacks than previous generation models. To help expose and address this lack of robustness, we introduce a novel ranking attack framework named Attack-in-the-Chain, which tracks interactions between large language models (LLMs) and NRMs based on chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting to generate adversarial examples under black-box settings. Our approach starts by identifying anchor documents with higher ranking positions than the target document as nodes in the reasoning chain. We then dynamically assign the number of perturbation words to each node and prompt LLMs to execute attacks. Finally, we verify the attack performance of all nodes at each reasoning step and proceed to generate the next reasoning step. Empirical results on two web search benchmarks show the effectiveness of our method.
中摘: 神经排名模型（NRMs）在检索性能方面已被证明非常有效。不幸的是，它们也显示出比上一代模型更高的攻击敏感度。为了帮助揭示并解决这一鲁棒性不足的问题，我们引入了一种名为“链式攻击”（Attack-in-the-Chain）的新颖排名攻击框架，该框架基于链式思考（CoT）提示来跟踪大型语言模型（LLMs）和NRMs之间的交互，在黑盒设置下生成对抗样本。我们的方法首先通过识别具有比目标文档更高排名位置的锚文档作为推理链中的节点来开始。然后，动态分配每个节点的扰动词数量，并提示LLMs执行攻击。最后，在每一步推理过程中验证所有节点的攻击性能，并继续生成下一步推理。我们在两个网页搜索基准上的实证结果表明了我们方法的有效性。

[No.191] From Pairwise to Ranking: Climbing the Ladder to Ideal Collaborative Filtering with Pseudo-Ranking
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 从成对比较到排名：攀登理想的协作过滤阶梯——伪排名的方法
类别: data mining knowledge management ii
作者: Yuhan Zhao;Rui Chen;Li Chen;Shuang Zhang;Qilong Han;Hongtao Song;
机构: Harbin Engineering University;Hong Kong Baptist University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33462
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18168
英摘: Intuitively, an ideal collaborative filtering (CF) model should learn from users' full rankings over all items to make optimal top-K recommendations. Due to the absence of such full rankings in practice, most CF models rely on pairwise loss functions to approximate full rankings, resulting in an immense performance gap. In this paper, we provide a novel analysis using the multiple ordinal classification concept to reveal the inevitable gap between a pairwise approximation and the ideal case. However, bridging the gap in practice encounters two formidable challenges: (1) none of the real-world datasets contains full ranking information; (2) there does not exist a loss function that is capable of consuming ranking information. To overcome these challenges, we propose a pseudo-ranking paradigm (PRP) that addresses the lack of ranking information by introducing pseudo-rankings supervised by an original noise injection mechanism. Additionally, we put forward a new ranking loss function designed to handle ranking information effectively. To ensure our method's robustness against potential inaccuracies in pseudo-rankings, we equip the ranking loss function with a gradient-based confidence mechanism to detect and mitigate abnormal gradients. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that PRP significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.
中摘: 直观上，理想的协同过滤（CF）模型应该从用户对所有项目的完整排名中学习，以做出最优的Top-K推荐。但由于实践中缺乏这种完整的排名信息，大多数CF模型依赖于对偶损失函数来近似完整的排名，从而导致了巨大的性能差距。在本文中，我们通过多级分类的概念提供了一种新颖的分析方法，揭示了对偶近似与理想情况之间不可避免的差距。然而，在实践中弥合这一差距面临两大挑战：(1) 实际世界中的数据集都不包含完整的排名信息；(2) 没有损失函数能够处理排名信息。为克服这些挑战，我们提出了一种伪排名范式（PRP），通过引入由原始噪声注入机制监督的伪排名来解决缺乏排名信息的问题。此外，我们还提出了一种新的排名损失函数，旨在有效处理排名信息。为了确保方法在伪排名潜在不准确性方面的鲁棒性，我们将排名损失函数装备了基于梯度的信心机制，用于检测和缓解异常梯度。在四个实际世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，PRP 显著优于最先进的方法。

[No.194] Bayesian Low-Rank Learning (Bella): A Practical Approach to Bayesian Neural Networks
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 贝叶斯低秩学习（Bella）：一种实用的贝叶斯神经网络方法
类别: machine learning i
作者: Bao Gia Doan;Afshar Shamsi;Xiao-Yu Guo;Arash Mohammadi;Hamid Alinejad-Rokny;Dino Sejdinovic;Damien Teney;Damith C. Ranasinghe;Ehsan Abbasnejad;

英摘: Computational complexity of Bayesian learning is impeding its adoption in practical, large-scale tasks. Despite demonstrations of significant merits such as improved robustness and resilience to unseen or out-of-distribution inputs over their non- Bayesian counterparts, their practical use has faded to near insignificance. In this study, we introduce an innovative framework to mitigate the computational burden of Bayesian neural networks (BNNs). Our approach follows the principle of Bayesian techniques based on deep ensembles, but significantly reduces their cost via multiple low-rank perturbations of parameters arising from a pre-trained neural network. Both vanilla version of ensembles as well as more sophisticated schemes such as Bayesian learning with Stein Variational Gradient Descent (SVGD), previously deemed impractical for large models, can be seamlessly implemented within the proposed framework, called Bayesian Low-Rank LeArning (Bella). In a nutshell, i) Bella achieves a dramatic reduction in the number of trainable parameters required to approximate a Bayesian posterior; and ii) it not only maintains, but in some instances, surpasses the performance of conventional Bayesian learning methods and non-Bayesian baselines. 中摘: 贝叶斯学习的计算复杂性阻碍了其在实际大规模任务中的应用。尽管贝叶斯方法在改进鲁棒性和对未见过或分布外输入的抗干扰能力方面表现出显著的优势，但它们的实际应用却几乎退化到了微不足道的程度。在这项研究中，我们提出了一种创新框架，以减轻贝叶斯神经网络（BNNs）的计算负担。我们的方法遵循基于深度ensemble的贝叶斯技术原则，但通过从预训练神经网络产生的参数进行多次低秩扰动，大幅降低了其成本。无论是传统的ensemble版本，还是更复杂的方案如Stein变分梯度下降（SVGD）贝叶斯学习，以前被认为不适合大型模型的应用，现在都可以无缝地在所提出的框架内实现，该框架称为贝叶斯低秩学习（Bella）。简而言之，i) Bella实现了对近似贝叶斯后验所需的可训练参数数量的大幅减少；ii) 它不仅保持了，而且在某些情况下甚至超过了传统贝叶斯学习方法和非贝叶斯基线的性能。我们在ImageNet、CAMELYON17、DomainNet、带有CLIP的VQA以及LLaVA等大规模任务上的结果证明了Bella在构建面向实际应用的高度可扩展和实用的贝叶斯深度模型方面的有效性和灵活性。

[No.195] A Wander Through the Multimodal Landscape: Efficient Transfer Learning via Low-rank Sequence Multimodal Adapter
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank

来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: Rank-Insensitive LoRA-Based量化误差补偿技术以提升2位宽大型语言模型的准确性

作者: Geonho Lee;Janghwan Lee;Sukjin Hong;Minsoo Kim;Euijai Ahn;Du-Seong Chang;Jungwook Choi;

英摘: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become the dominant method for parameter-efficient LLM fine-tuning, with LoRA-based quantization error compensation (LQEC) emerging as a powerful tool for recovering accuracy in compressed LLMs. However, LQEC has underperformed in sub-4-bit scenarios, with no prior investigation into understanding this limitation. We propose RILQ (Rank-Insensitive LoRA-based Quantization Error Compensation) to understand fundamental limitation and boost 2-bit LLM accuracy. 中摘: 低秩适应（LoRA）已成为参数高效大型语言模型（LLM）微调的主要方法，而基于LoRA的量化误差补偿（LQEC）则成为恢复压缩LLM准确性的强大工具。然而，在低于4位的场景中，LQEC的表现不佳，并且在此之前没有对这一局限性进行过研究。我们提出了秩不变LoRA基量化误差补偿（RILQ），以理解基本限制并提升2位量化LLM的准确性。根据基于秩分析揭示出的激活差异损失在模型层面的秩不变特性，RILQ利用此损失在层间合作调整适配器，从而实现使用低秩适配器进行稳健的误差补偿。在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上的评估显示，RILQ能够在各种先进的量化器中一致地提高2位量化推理的准确性，并增强特定任务微调的准确性。RILQ保持与现有LoRA方法相当的计算效率，能够使合并适配器的权重量化LLM推理在准确性方面显著提升，使其成为提升2位LLM性能的有前途的方法。我们的代码可在https://github.com/aiha-lab/RILQ获取。

[No.202] In-depth Analysis of Low-rank Matrix Factorisation in a Federated Setting
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 在联邦学习环境中对低秩矩阵因子分解的深入分析
类别: machine learning v
作者: Constantin Philippenko;Kevin Scaman;Laurent Massoulié;
机构: INRIA Paris;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34192
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08771
英摘: We analyze a distributed algorithm to compute a low-rank matrix factorization on $N$ clients, each holding a local dataset $\mathbf{S}^i \in \mathbb{R}^{n_i \times d}$, mathematically, we seek to solve $min_{\mathbf{U}^i \in \mathbb{R}^{n_i\times r}, \mathbf{V}\in \mathbb{R}^{d \times r} } \frac{1}{2} \sum_{i=1}^N \|\mathbf{S}^i - \mathbf{U}^i \mathbf{V}^\top\|^2_{\text{F}}$. Considering a power initialization of $\mathbf{V}$, we rewrite the previous smooth non-convex problem into a smooth strongly-convex problem that we solve using a parallel Nesterov gradient descent potentially requiring a single step of communication at the initialization step. For any client $i$ in $\{1, \dots, N\}$, we obtain a global $\mathbf{V}$ in $\mathbb{R}^{d \times r}$ common to all clients and a local variable $\mathbf{U}^i$ in $\mathbb{R}^{n_i \times r}$. We provide a linear rate of convergence of the excess loss which depends on $σ_{\max} / σ_{r}$, where $σ_{r}$ is the $r^{\mathrm{th}}$ singular value of the concatenation $\mathbf{S}$ of the matrices $(\mathbf{S}^i)_{i=1}^N$. This result improves the rates of convergence given in the literature, which depend on $σ_{\max}^2 / σ_{\min}^2$. We provide an upper bound on the Frobenius-norm error of reconstruction under the power initialization strategy. We complete our analysis with experiments on both synthetic and real data.
中摘: 我们分析了一个在 \(N\) 个客户端上计算低秩矩阵分解的分布式算法。从数学上讲，我们寻求解决以下问题：\[ \min_{\mathbf{U}^i \in \mathbb{R}^{n_i \times r}, \mathbf{V} \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times r}} \frac{1}{2} \sum_{i=1}^N \|\mathbf{S}^i - \mathbf{U}^i \mathbf{V}^\top\|_{\text{F}}^2 \] 其中每个客户端持有本地数据集 \(\mathbf{S}^i \in \mathbb{R}^{n_i \times d}\)。通过采用幂初始化策略 \(\mathbf{V}\)，我们将之前的平滑非凸问题重写为一个平滑强凸问题，并使用并行的Nesterov梯度下降法求解，可能只需要在初始化步骤进行一次通信。对于任意客户端 \(i \in \{1, \dots, N\}\)，我们获得一个全局变量 \(\mathbf{V} \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times r}\)，它是所有客户端共有的，以及一个局部变量 \(\mathbf{U}^i \in \mathbb{R}^{n_i \times r}\)。我们提供了在幂初始化策略下的损失过剩量的线性收敛速率，该速率依赖于 \(\sigma_{\max} / \sigma_r\)，其中 \(\sigma_r\) 是矩阵序列 \((\mathbf{S}^i)_{i=1}^N\) 的拼接后第 \(r\) 阶奇异值。这一结果改进了文献中给出的收敛速率，这些速率依赖于 \(\sigma_{\max}^2 / \sigma_{\min}^2\)。我们还提供了在幂初始化策略下重构误差的Frobenius范数上界。最后，我们在合成数据和真实数据上进行了实验以完成我们的分析。

[No.203] Enhancing Trustworthiness of Graph Neural Networks with Rank-Based Conformal Training
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于排名的可信性训练增强图神经网络的信任worthiness
类别: machine learning vi
作者: Ting Wang;Zhixin Zhou;Rui Luo;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.02767
英摘: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) has been widely used in a variety of fields because of their great potential in representing graph-structured data. However, lacking of rigorous uncertainty estimations limits their application in high-stakes. Conformal Prediction (CP) can produce statistically guaranteed uncertainty estimates by using the classifier's probability estimates to obtain prediction sets, which contains the true class with a user-specified probability. In this paper, we propose a Rank-based CP during training framework to GNNs (RCP-GNN) for reliable uncertainty estimates to enhance the trustworthiness of GNNs in the node classification scenario. By exploiting rank information of the classifier's outcome, prediction sets with desired coverage rate can be efficiently constructed. The strategy of CP during training with differentiable rank-based conformity loss function is further explored to adapt prediction sets according to network topology information. In this way, the composition of prediction sets can be guided by the goal of jointly reducing inefficiency and probability estimation errors. Extensive experiments on several real-world datasets show that our model achieves any pre-defined target marginal coverage while significantly reducing the inefficiency compared with state-of-the-art methods.
中摘: 图神经网络（GNNs）因其在表示图结构数据方面巨大的潜力而在多个领域得到了广泛应用。然而，缺乏严格的不确定性估计限制了它们在高风险场景中的应用。校准预测（Conformal Prediction，CP）可以通过使用分类器的概率估计来获得包含真实类别的预测集，并且这些预测集具有用户指定的概率保证。在本文中，我们提出了一种在训练过程中基于排名的校准预测框架（RCP-GNN），以增强GNNs在节点分类场景中的可信度，提供可靠的不确定性估计。通过利用分类器结果的排名信息，可以高效地构建具有所需覆盖概率的预测集。进一步探索了基于可微排名一致性损失函数的训练过程中的校准预测策略，使其能够根据网络拓扑信息调整预测集。这样，预测集的组成可以根据同时减少无效性和概率估计误差的目标进行指导。在多个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们的模型能够在显著减少无效性的前提下实现任何预定义的目标边际覆盖概率，优于最先进的方法。

[No.204] MTL-LoRA: Low-Rank Adaptation for Multi-Task Learning
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: MTL-LoRA：多任务学习中的低秩适应
类别: machine learning vi
作者: Yaming Yang;Dilxat Muhtar;Yelong Shen;Yuefeng Zhan;Jianfeng Liu;Yujing Wang;Hao Sun;Weiwei Deng;Feng Sun;Qi Zhang;Weizhu Chen;Yunhai Tong;
机构: Peking University;Nanjing University;Microsoft;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/35509
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09437
英摘: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has been widely employed for domain adaptation, with LoRA being one of the most prominent methods due to its simplicity and effectiveness. However, in multi-task learning (MTL) scenarios, LoRA tends to obscure the distinction between tasks by projecting sparse high-dimensional features from different tasks into the same dense low-dimensional intrinsic space. This leads to task interference and suboptimal performance for LoRA and its variants. To tackle this challenge, we propose MTL-LoRA, which retains the advantages of low-rank adaptation while significantly enhancing MTL capabilities. MTL-LoRA augments LoRA by incorporating additional task-adaptive parameters that differentiate task-specific information and capture shared knowledge across various tasks within low-dimensional spaces. This approach enables pre-trained models to jointly adapt to different target domains with a limited number of trainable parameters. Comprehensive experimental results, including evaluations on public academic benchmarks for natural language understanding, commonsense reasoning, and image-text understanding, as well as real-world industrial text Ads relevance datasets, demonstrate that MTL-LoRA outperforms LoRA and its various variants with comparable or even fewer learnable parameters in MTL setting.
中摘: 参数高效的微调（PEFT）在领域适应中得到了广泛的应用，而LoRA因其简单性和有效性成为其中最突出的方法之一。然而，在多任务学习（MTL）场景中，LoRA往往会通过将来自不同任务的稀疏高维特征投影到同一个密集低维内在空间中，模糊任务之间的区别，导致任务干扰并使得LoRA及其变体的性能不佳。为了解决这一挑战，我们提出了MTL-LoRA，它保留了低秩适应的优势，同时显著增强了多任务学习的能力。MTL-LoRA通过引入额外的任务适应参数来增强LoRA，这些参数能够区分特定于任务的信息，并在低维度空间中捕捉各种任务间的共享知识。这种方法使预训练模型能够在有限的可训练参数下联合适应不同的目标领域。全面的实验结果表明，在多任务学习设置中，MTL-LoRA使用与LoRA及其各种变体相当甚至更少的学习参数，仍能表现出更优的性能。这些结果包括在自然语言理解、常识推理和图像-文本理解的公共学术基准测试上的评估，以及在实际工业文本广告相关性数据集上的评估。

[No.205] OTLRM: Orthogonal Learning-based Low-Rank Metric for Multi-Dimensional Inverse Problems
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 正交学习为基础的低秩度量多维逆问题
类别: machine learning vi
作者: Xiangming Wang;Haijin Zeng;Jiaoyang Chen;Sheng Liu;Yongyong Chen;Guoqing Chao;
机构: Harbin Institute of Technology;Ghent University;University of Electronic Science and Technology of China;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/35427
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11165
英摘: In real-world scenarios, complex data such as multispectral images and multi-frame videos inherently exhibit robust low-rank property. This property is vital for multi-dimensional inverse problems, such as tensor completion, spectral imaging reconstruction, and multispectral image denoising. Existing tensor singular value decomposition (t-SVD) definitions rely on hand-designed or pre-given transforms, which lack flexibility for defining tensor nuclear norm (TNN). The TNN-regularized optimization problem is solved by the singular value thresholding (SVT) operator, which leverages the t-SVD framework to obtain the low-rank tensor. However, it is quite complicated to introduce SVT into deep neural networks due to the numerical instability problem in solving the derivatives of the eigenvectors. In this paper, we introduce a novel data-driven generative low-rank t-SVD model based on the learnable orthogonal transform, which can be naturally solved under its representation. Prompted by the linear algebra theorem of the Householder transformation, our learnable orthogonal transform is achieved by constructing an endogenously orthogonal matrix adaptable to neural networks, optimizing it as arbitrary orthogonal matrices. Additionally, we propose a low-rank solver as a generalization of SVT, which utilizes an efficient representation of generative networks to obtain low-rank structures. Extensive experiments highlight its significant restoration enhancements.
中摘: 在实际应用场景中，如多光谱图像和多帧视频等复杂数据，本身就具有稳健的低秩特性。这种特性对于多维逆问题至关重要，例如张量补全、光谱图像重构和多光谱图像去噪。现有的张量奇异值分解（t-SVD）定义依赖于手工设计或预先给定的变换，这使得定义张量核范数（TNN）缺乏灵活性。张量核范数正则化优化问题通过奇异值阈值（SVT）算子来求解，该算子利用t-SVD框架获得低秩张量。然而，将SVT引入深度神经网络中相当复杂，因为求解特征向量导数时存在数值不稳定的问题。在本文中，我们提出了一种基于可学习正交变换的数据驱动生成低秩t-SVD模型，可以在其表示下自然求解。受豪斯霍尔德变换的线性代数定理启发，我们的可学习正交变换通过构建适用于神经网络的内生正交矩阵实现，并将其优化为任意正交矩阵。此外，我们还提出了一种低秩求解器，作为SVT的一般化，利用生成网络的有效表示来获取低秩结构。大量的实验表明，这种方法能够显著提升图像恢复效果。

[No.207] ScaleOT: Privacy-utility-scalable Offsite-tuning with Dynamic LayerReplace and Selective Rank Compression
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: ScaleOT：隐私与实用性可调节的离线调优，结合动态层替换和选择性秩压缩

作者: Kai Yao;Zhaorui Tan;Tiandi Ye;Lichun Li;Yuan Zhao;Wenyan Liu;Wei Wang;Jianke Zhu;

英摘: Offsite-tuning is a privacy-preserving method for tuning large language models (LLMs) by sharing a lossy compressed emulator from the LLM owners with data owners for downstream task tuning. This approach protects the privacy of both the model and data owners. However, current offsite tuning methods often suffer from adaptation degradation, high computational costs, and limited protection strength due to uniformly dropping LLM layers or relying on expensive knowledge distillation. To address these issues, we propose ScaleOT, a novel privacy-utility-scalable offsite-tuning framework that effectively balances privacy and utility. ScaleOT introduces a novel layerwise lossy compression algorithm that uses reinforcement learning to obtain the importance of each layer. It employs lightweight networks, termed harmonizers, to replace the raw LLM layers. By combining important original LLM layers and harmonizers in different ratios, ScaleOT generates emulators tailored for optimal performance with various model scales for enhanced privacy protection. Additionally, we present a rank reduction method to further compress the original LLM layers, significantly enhancing privacy with negligible impact on utility. Comprehensive experiments show that ScaleOT can achieve nearly lossless offsite tuning performance compared with full fine-tuning while obtaining better model privacy.
中摘: 离线调优是一种保护隐私的方法，通过从大型语言模型（LLM）所有者处共享有损压缩的模拟器来对下游任务进行调优，并将该模拟器与数据所有者共享。这种方法同时保护了模型和数据所有者的隐私。然而，当前的离线调优方法往往面临适应性退化、高计算成本以及由于均匀丢弃LLM层或依赖昂贵的知识蒸馏而导致的保护强度有限等问题。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了ScaleOT，这是一种新颖的可调节隐私与实用性的离线调优框架，能够有效平衡隐私和实用性。ScaleOT引入了一种新颖的分层有损压缩算法，使用强化学习来获取每一层的重要性。它采用轻量级网络，称为谐波器，来替代原始的LLM层。通过以不同的比例组合重要的原始LLM层和谐波器，ScaleOT生成了针对各种模型规模优化性能的定制模拟器，从而增强隐私保护。此外，我们还提出了一种秩减少方法，进一步压缩原始的LLM层，显著增强了隐私保护，同时对实用性的影响几乎可以忽略不计。全面的实验表明，ScaleOT可以在与全量微调几乎无损的情况下实现更好的模型隐私。

[No.208] Gradient Weight-normalized Low-rank Projection for Efficient LLM Training
来源: AAAI 2025

In this work, we introduce Gradient Weight-Normalized Low-Rank Projection (GradNormLoRP), a novel approach that enhances both parameter and memory efficiency while maintaining comparable performance to full fine-tuning. 中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在各种任务中表现出色，但计算资源的需求不断增加也带来了显著挑战，特别是在下游任务中广泛使用全面微调方面。为了解决这一问题，已经开发出了参数高效微调（PEFT）方法，但这些方法往往不如全面微调性能优异，并且在内存效率方面存在问题。在本研究中，我们引入了一种名为梯度权重归一化低秩投影（GradNormLoRP）的新方法，该方法在保持与全面微调相当的性能的同时，提升了参数和内存效率。GradNormLoRP通过归一化权重矩阵来改进梯度条件，有助于优化过程中更好地收敛。此外，它还对权重和梯度矩阵应用了低秩近似，显著减少了训练过程中的内存使用量。大量实验表明，我们的8位GradNormLoRP将优化器内存使用量最多降低了89.5%，并且能够在如NVIDIA RTX 4090这样的消费级GPU上预训练大型LLM，而无需额外的推理成本。此外，GradNormLoRP在微调任务中优于现有的低秩方法。例如，在以8的秩对RoBERTa模型进行GLUE任务的微调时，GradNormLoRP实现了平均得分为80.65，超过了LoRA的得分79.23。这些结果突显了GradNormLoRP作为高效LLM预训练和微调有前途的替代方案的价值。源代码：https://github.com/Jhhuangkay/Gradient-Weight-normalized-Low-rank-Projection-for-Efficient-LLM-Training

[No.210] Is There No Such Thing as a Bad Question? H4R: HalluciBot for Ratiocination, Rewriting, Ranking, and Routing
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 《没有所谓的坏问题吗？》H4R：用于推理、重写、排序和路由的幻觉机器人

英摘: Hallucination continues to be one of the most critical challenges in the institutional adoption journey of Large Language Models (LLMs). HalluciBot can serve as a proxy reward model for query rewriting, offering a general framework to estimate query quality based on accuracy and consensus. In essence, HalluciBot investigates how poorly constructed queries can lead to erroneous outputs - moreover, by employing query rewriting guided by HalluciBot's empirical estimates, we demonstrate that 95.7% output accuracy can be achieved for Multiple Choice questions. The training procedure for HalluciBot consists of perturbing 369,837 queries n times, employing n+1 independent LLM agents, sampling an output from each query, conducting a Multi-Agent Monte Carlo simulation on the sampled outputs, and training an encoder classifier. Therefore, HalluciBot paves the way to ratiocinate (76.0% test F1 score, 46.6% in saved computation on hallucinatory queries), rewrite (+30.2% positive class transition from hallucinatory to non-hallucinatory), rank (+50.6% positive class transition from hallucinatory to non-hallucinatory), and route queries to effective pipelines.

[No.212] Massively Parallel Single-Source SimRanks in O(log N) Rounds
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 大规模并行单源SimRanks在O(log N)轮次内
类别: Data Mining
作者: Siqiang Luo;Zulun Zhu;
机构: Nanyang Technological University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/249
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.04015
英摘: SimRank is one of the most fundamental measures that evaluate the structural similarity between two nodes in a graph and has been applied in a plethora of data management tasks. These tasks often involve single-source SimRank computation that evaluates the SimRank values between a source node $s$ and all other nodes. Due to its high computation complexity, single-source SimRank computation for large graphs is notoriously challenging, and hence recent studies resort to distributed processing. To our surprise, although SimRank has been widely adopted for two decades, theoretical aspects of distributed SimRanks with provable results have rarely been studied.
  In this paper, we conduct a theoretical study on single-source SimRank computation in the Massive Parallel Computation (MPC) model, which is the standard theoretical framework modeling distributed systems such as MapReduce, Hadoop, or Spark. Existing distributed SimRank algorithms enforce either $Ω(\log n)$ communication round complexity or $Ω(n)$ machine space for a graph of $n$ nodes. We overcome this barrier. Particularly, given a graph of $n$ nodes, for any query node $v$ and constant error $ε>\frac{3}{n}$, we show that using $O(\log^2 \log n)$ rounds of communication among machines is almost enough to compute single-source SimRank values with at most $ε$ absolute errors, while each machine only needs a space sub-linear to $n$. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first single-source SimRank algorithm in MPC that can overcome the $Θ(\log n)$ round complexity barrier with provable result accuracy.
中摘: SimRank 是评估图中两个节点结构相似性的一种最基本度量方法，并且已经被广泛应用于各种数据管理任务。这些任务通常涉及单源 SimRank 计算，即评估源节点 \( s \) 与其他所有节点之间的 SimRank 值。由于其高度的计算复杂性，大规模图上的单源 SimRank 计算一直非常具有挑战性，因此最近的研究转向了分布式处理。让我们惊讶的是，尽管 SimRank 已经被广泛采用超过二十年，但具有可证明结果的分布式 SimRank 理论方面却鲜有研究。

在本文中，我们对大规模平行计算（MPC）模型下的单源 SimRank 计算进行了理论研究，这是用于建模诸如 MapReduce、Hadoop 或 Spark 等分布式系统的标准理论框架。现有的分布式 SimRank 算法要么需要 \( Ω(\log n) \) 的通信轮数复杂度，要么需要与节点数 \( n \) 成线性的机器空间。我们突破了这一限制。具体而言，对于包含 \( n \) 个节点的任意查询节点 \( v \) 和常数误差 \( ε > \frac{3}{n} \)，我们展示了使用 \( O(\log^2 \log n) \) 轮通信即可几乎足够地计算出单源 SimRank 值，同时每个机器只需要子线性的空间。据我们所知，这是第一个能够在 MPC 模型下克服 \( Θ(\log n) \) 轮通信复杂度障碍并提供可证明结果准确性的单源 SimRank 算法。

[No.214] Toward Completing the Picture of Control in Schulze and Ranked Pairs Elections
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 完成关于舒尔ZE和分级对选举控制图的拼图
类别: Game Theory and Economic Paradigms
作者: Cynthia Maushagen;David Niclaus;Paul Nüsken;Jörg Rothe;Tessa Seeger;
机构: Heinrich-Heine-Universität Düsseldorf;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/326
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.08956
英摘: Both Schulze and ranked pairs are voting rules that satisfy many natural, desirable axioms. Many standard types of electoral control (with a chair seeking to change the outcome of an election by interfering with the election structure) have already been studied. However, for control by replacing candidates or voters and for (exact) multimode control that combines multiple standard attacks, many questions remain open. We solve a number of these open cases for Schulze and ranked pairs. In addition, we fix a flaw in the reduction of Menton and Singh [IJCAI 2013] showing that Schulze is resistant to constructive control by deleting candidates and re-establish a vulnerability result for destructive control by deleting candidates. In some of our proofs, we study variants of s-t vertex cuts in graphs that are related to our control problems.
中摘: 舒尔茨和 ranked pairs 都是满足许多自然且 desirable 公理的投票规则。许多标准类型的选举控制（主席通过干扰选举结构来改变选举结果）已经被研究过。然而，对于替换候选人或选民的控制以及结合多种标准攻击的 exact 多模式控制，仍有许多问题尚未解决。我们解决了这些开放问题中的若干个案例，针对舒尔茨和 ranked pairs。此外，我们修正了 Menton 和 Singh [IJCAI 2013] 中关于舒尔茨对删除候选人的 constructive 控制具有抗性的减少证明中的缺陷，并重新确立了对删除候选人的 destructive 控制的脆弱性结果。在一些证明过程中，我们研究了与我们的控制问题相关的图上的 s-t 顶点割变体。

[No.218] Rank and Align: Towards Effective Source-free Graph Domain Adaptation
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: rank
标题: 排名与对齐：迈向有效的源无监督图域适应
类别: Machine Learning
作者: Junyu Luo;Zhiping Xiao;Yifan Wang;Xiao Luo;Jingyang Yuan;Wei Ju;Langechuan Liu;Ming Zhang;
机构: Peking University;University of California, Los Angeles;University of International Business and Economics;Anker Innovations;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/520
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.12185
英摘: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have achieved impressive performance in graph domain adaptation. However, extensive source graphs could be unavailable in real-world scenarios due to privacy and storage concerns. To this end, we investigate an underexplored yet practical problem of source-free graph domain adaptation, which transfers knowledge from source models instead of source graphs to a target domain. To solve this problem, we introduce a novel GNN-based approach called Rank and Align (RNA), which ranks graph similarities with spectral seriation for robust semantics learning, and aligns inharmonic graphs with harmonic graphs which close to the source domain for subgraph extraction. In particular, to overcome label scarcity, we employ the spectral seriation algorithm to infer the robust pairwise rankings, which can guide semantic learning using a similarity learning objective. To depict distribution shifts, we utilize spectral clustering and the silhouette coefficient to detect harmonic graphs, which the source model can easily classify. To reduce potential domain discrepancy, we extract domain-invariant subgraphs from inharmonic graphs by an adversarial edge sampling process, which guides the invariant learning of GNNs. Extensive experiments on several benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed RNA.
中摘: 图神经网络（GNNs）在图域适应领域取得了令人印象深刻的性能。然而，在实际场景中，由于隐私和存储方面的顾虑，大量的源图可能不可用。为了解决这一问题，我们探讨了一个尚未充分研究但具有实际意义的源图无监督图域适应问题，该问题通过从源模型而非源图中转移知识来适应目标域。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种新颖的基于GNN的方法——Rank and Align（RNA），该方法利用谱序列化对图相似性进行排序以实现稳健的语义学习，并通过将接近源域的谐波图与不谐波图对齐来进行子图提取。特别是，为了克服标签稀缺的问题，我们采用谱序列化算法推断稳健的成对排序，这可以指导使用相似性学习目标的语义学习。为了描绘分布偏移，我们利用谱聚类和轮廓系数来检测谐波图，这些图源模型可以轻松分类。为了减少潜在的域差异，我们通过对抗边采样过程从不谐波图中提取域不变子图，从而引导GNN的不变学习。在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明了我们提出的RNA的有效性。

[No.221] A LLM-based Ranking Method for the Evaluation of Automatic Counter-Narrative Generation
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于LLM的排名方法用于自动反叙事生成的评估
类别: 
作者: Irune Zubiaga;Aitor Soroa;Rodrigo Agerri;
机构: University of the Basque Country;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.559/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.15227
英摘: This paper proposes a novel approach to evaluate Counter Narrative (CN) generation using a Large Language Model (LLM) as an evaluator. We show that traditional automatic metrics correlate poorly with human judgements and fail to capture the nuanced relationship between generated CNs and human perception. To alleviate this, we introduce a model ranking pipeline based on pairwise comparisons of generated CNs from different models, organized in a tournament-style format. The proposed evaluation method achieves a high correlation with human preference, with a $ρ$ score of 0.88. As an additional contribution, we leverage LLMs as zero-shot CN generators and provide a comparative analysis of chat, instruct, and base models, exploring their respective strengths and limitations. Through meticulous evaluation, including fine-tuning experiments, we elucidate the differences in performance and responsiveness to domain-specific data. We conclude that chat-aligned models in zero-shot are the best option for carrying out the task, provided they do not refuse to generate an answer due to security concerns.
中摘: 本文提出了一种新的方法，使用大型语言模型（LLM）作为评估器来评价反叙事（CN）生成。我们展示了传统自动指标与人类判断的相关性较差，并且无法捕捉生成的反叙事与人类感知之间的微妙关系。为了解决这一问题，我们引入了一种基于不同模型生成的反叙事两两比较的模型排名管道，组织成类似淘汰赛的形式。所提出的评估方法与人类偏好高度相关，相关系数达到0.88。此外，我们利用LLM作为零样本反叙事生成器，并提供了聊天、指令和基础模型的对比分析，探讨了它们各自的优缺点。通过细致的评估，包括微调实验，我们阐明了这些模型在性能和对特定领域数据的响应方面的差异。我们得出结论，在没有因安全问题拒绝生成答案的情况下，零样本聊天对齐模型是最适合执行该任务的选择。

[No.222] A Study of Implicit Ranking Unfairness in Large Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 大型语言模型中的隐含排名不公平性研究
类别: 
作者: Chen Xu;Wenjie Wang;Yuxin Li;Liang Pang;Jun Xu;Tat-Seng Chua;
机构: Renmin University of China;NExT;National University of Singapore;Chinese Academy of Sciences;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.467/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.07054
英摘: Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated a superior ability to serve as ranking models. However, concerns have arisen as LLMs will exhibit discriminatory ranking behaviors based on users' sensitive attributes (\eg gender). Worse still, in this paper, we identify a subtler form of discrimination in LLMs, termed \textit{implicit ranking unfairness}, where LLMs exhibit discriminatory ranking patterns based solely on non-sensitive user profiles, such as user names. Such implicit unfairness is more widespread but less noticeable, threatening the ethical foundation. To comprehensively explore such unfairness, our analysis will focus on three research aspects: (1) We propose an evaluation method to investigate the severity of implicit ranking unfairness. (2) We uncover the reasons for causing such unfairness. (3) To mitigate such unfairness effectively, we utilize a pair-wise regression method to conduct fair-aware data augmentation for LLM fine-tuning. The experiment demonstrates that our method outperforms existing approaches in ranking fairness, achieving this with only a small reduction in accuracy. Lastly, we emphasize the need for the community to identify and mitigate the implicit unfairness, aiming to avert the potential deterioration in the reinforced human-LLMs ecosystem deterioration.
中摘: 最近，大型语言模型（LLMs）展示了作为排名模型的优越能力。然而，随着LLMs将基于用户的敏感属性（例如性别）表现出歧视性排名行为，人们开始产生担忧。更糟糕的是，在本文中，我们识别出了一种更为隐蔽的歧视形式，称为“隐含排名不公平”，即LLMs仅基于非敏感用户资料（如用户名）表现出歧视性排名模式。这种隐含不公平现象更为普遍但不易察觉，威胁着伦理基础。为了全面探讨这种不公平现象，我们的分析将集中在三个方面：（1）我们提出了一种评估方法来调查隐含排名不公平的程度；（2）我们揭示了导致这种不公平的原因；（3）为了有效减轻这种不公平现象，我们利用一对一回归方法进行公平感知的数据增强，以改进LLM微调。实验表明，我们的方法在排名公平性方面优于现有方法，仅在准确性上略有降低。最后，我们强调社区需要识别并减轻隐含不公平现象，以防止人类与LLMs生态系统中的潜在恶化。

[No.223] Adaptive Feature-based Low-Rank Compression of Large Language Models via Bayesian Optimization
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于自适应特征的贝叶斯优化低秩压缩大型语言模型
类别: 
作者: Yixin Ji;Yang Xiang;Juntao Li;Qingrong Xia;Zi Ye;Xinyu Duan;Zhefeng Wang;Kehai Chen;Min Zhang;
机构: Soochow University;Huawei;Harbin Institute of Technology;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.240/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.10616
英摘: In recent years, large language models (LLMs) have driven advances in natural language processing. Still, their growing scale has increased the computational burden, necessitating a balance between efficiency and performance. Low-rank compression, a promising technique, reduces non-essential parameters by decomposing weight matrices into products of two low-rank matrices. Yet, its application in LLMs has not been extensively studied. The key to low-rank compression lies in low-rank factorization and low-rank dimensions allocation. To address the challenges of low-rank compression in LLMs, we conduct empirical research on the low-rank characteristics of large models. We propose a low-rank compression method suitable for LLMs. This approach involves precise estimation of feature distributions through pooled covariance matrices and a Bayesian optimization strategy for allocating low-rank dimensions. Experiments on the LLaMA-2 models demonstrate that our method outperforms existing strong structured pruning and low-rank compression techniques in maintaining model performance at the same compression ratio.
中摘: 近年来，大规模语言模型（LLMs）推动了自然语言处理的进步。然而，它们不断增加的规模也增加了计算负担，需要在效率和性能之间找到平衡。低秩压缩是一种有前景的技术，通过将权重矩阵分解为两个低秩矩阵的乘积来减少不必要的参数。尽管如此，其在LLMs中的应用尚未得到广泛研究。低秩压缩的关键在于低秩因子分解和低秩维度分配。为了应对LLMs中低秩压缩的挑战，我们对大型模型的低秩特性进行了实证研究，并提出了一种适合LLMs的低秩压缩方法。该方法通过聚类协方差矩阵精确估计特征分布，并利用贝叶斯优化策略进行低秩维度分配。实验结果显示，在相同的压缩比下，我们的方法优于现有的强结构剪枝和低秩压缩技术，能够保持模型性能。

[No.224] AGRaME: Any-Granularity Ranking with Multi-Vector Embeddings
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: AGRaME: 多向量嵌入的任意粒度排名
类别: 
作者: Revanth Gangi Reddy;Omar Attia;Yunyao Li;Heng Ji;Saloni Potdar;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;Apple;Adobe;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.490/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15028
英摘: Ranking is a fundamental and popular problem in search. However, existing ranking algorithms usually restrict the granularity of ranking to full passages or require a specific dense index for each desired level of granularity. Such lack of flexibility in granularity negatively affects many applications that can benefit from more granular ranking, such as sentence-level ranking for open-domain question-answering, or proposition-level ranking for attribution. In this work, we introduce the idea of any-granularity ranking, which leverages multi-vector embeddings to rank at varying levels of granularity while maintaining encoding at a single (coarser) level of granularity. We propose a multi-granular contrastive loss for training multi-vector approaches, and validate its utility with both sentences and propositions as ranking units. Finally, we demonstrate the application of proposition-level ranking to post-hoc citation addition in retrieval-augmented generation, surpassing the performance of prompt-driven citation generation.
中摘: 排名是搜索中的一个基本且流行的问题。然而，现有的排名算法通常限制了排名的粒度级别，只能对整个段落进行排名，或者需要为每个期望的粒度级别构建特定的密集索引。这种粒度级别的缺乏灵活性对许多可以从更精细的排名中受益的应用程序产生了负面影响，例如开放域问答中的句子级排名，或归因中的命题级排名。在本工作中，我们引入了任意粒度排名的概念，该概念利用多向量嵌入在保持编码在单一（较粗）粒度级别的情况下，在不同的粒度级别上进行排名。我们提出了一种多粒度对比损失用于训练多向量方法，并使用句子和命题作为排名单位验证其有效性。最后，我们展示了命题级排名在检索增强生成中的后处理引文添加应用，超越了基于提示的引文生成性能。

[No.225] Centrality-aware Product Retrieval and Ranking
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于中心性的产品检索与排序
类别: 
作者: Hadeel Saadany;Swapnil Bhosale;Samarth Agrawal;Diptesh Kanojia;Constantin Orasan;Zhe Wu;
机构: University of Surrey;eBay Inc.;
类型: Industry
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-industry.17/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15930
英摘: This paper addresses the challenge of improving user experience on e-commerce platforms by enhancing product ranking relevant to users' search queries. Ambiguity and complexity of user queries often lead to a mismatch between the user's intent and retrieved product titles or documents. Recent approaches have proposed the use of Transformer-based models, which need millions of annotated query-title pairs during the pre-training stage, and this data often does not take user intent into account. To tackle this, we curate samples from existing datasets at eBay, manually annotated with buyer-centric relevance scores and centrality scores, which reflect how well the product title matches the users' intent. We introduce a User-intent Centrality Optimization (UCO) approach for existing models, which optimises for the user intent in semantic product search. To that end, we propose a dual-loss based optimisation to handle hard negatives, i.e., product titles that are semantically relevant but do not reflect the user's intent. Our contributions include curating challenging evaluation sets and implementing UCO, resulting in significant product ranking efficiency improvements observed for different evaluation metrics. Our work aims to ensure that the most buyer-centric titles for a query are ranked higher, thereby, enhancing the user experience on e-commerce platforms.
中摘: 本文探讨了通过改进与用户搜索查询相关的商品排名以提升电子商务平台用户体验的挑战。用户的查询往往存在模糊性和复杂性，这导致检索到的商品标题或文档与用户意图之间存在不匹配。最近的方法提出了使用基于Transformer的模型，这些模型在预训练阶段需要数百万条标注过的查询-标题对，但这些数据通常没有考虑到用户的意图。为了解决这一问题，我们从eBay现有的数据集中精心挑选样本，并手动标注买家为中心的相关性和中心性评分，这些评分反映了商品标题与用户意图的匹配程度。我们提出了一种用户意图中心性优化（UCO）方法，用于现有模型，在语义商品搜索中优化用户意图。为此，我们提出了一种基于双损失的优化方法来处理硬负例，即那些在语义上相关但未能反映用户意图的商品标题。我们的贡献包括构建具有挑战性的评估集和实现UCO，观察到不同的评估指标下商品排名效率显著提高。我们的工作旨在确保查询中最符合买家意图的商品标题被优先展示，从而提升电子商务平台的用户体验。

[No.226] Clustering and Ranking: Diversity-preserved Instruction Selection through Expert-aligned Quality Estimation
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 聚类和排序：专家导向的质量评估 preserved 多样性的指令选择
类别: 
作者: Yuan Ge;Yilun Liu;Chi Hu;Weibin Meng;Shimin Tao;Xiaofeng Zhao;Mahong Xia;Zhang Li;Boxing Chen;Hao Yang;Bei Li;Tong Xiao;JingBo Zhu;
机构: Northeastern University;Huawei;NiuTrans Research;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.28/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.18191
英摘: With contributions from the open-source community, a vast amount of instruction tuning (IT) data has emerged. Given the significant resource allocation required for training and evaluating models, it is advantageous to have an efficient method for selecting high-quality IT data. However, existing methods for instruction data selection have limitations such as relying on fragile external APIs, being affected by biases in GPT models, or reducing the diversity of the selected instruction dataset. In this paper, we propose an industrial-friendly, expert-aligned and diversity-preserved instruction data selection method: Clustering and Ranking (CaR). CaR employs a two-step process: first, it ranks instruction pairs using a high-accuracy (84.25%) scoring model aligned with expert preferences; second, it preserves dataset diversity through clustering. In our experiment, CaR efficiently selected a mere 1.96% of Alpaca's IT data, yet the resulting AlpaCaR model surpassed Alpaca's performance by an average of 32.1% in GPT-4 evaluations. Moreover, we find that data selecting is a consistent paradigm whether the pre-trained model is more capable or the model parameters scaling up. Our approach employs compact models with 550M parameters and incurs just 11.2% of the financial outlay of current methods, enhancing its industrial deployability.
中摘: 得益于开源社区的贡献，大量的指令调优（IT）数据已经涌现出来。鉴于训练和评估模型所需的资源较多，拥有一个高效的方法来选择高质量的IT数据是有优势的。然而，现有的指令数据选择方法存在一些局限性，如依赖脆弱的外部API、受到GPT模型偏见的影响，或者降低了所选指令数据集的多样性。在本文中，我们提出了一种工业友好型、专家对齐且保留多样性的指令数据选择方法：聚类和排名（Clustering and Ranking, CaR）。CaR采用两步过程：首先，使用与专家偏好高度一致（准确率84.25%）的评分模型对指令对进行排序；其次，通过聚类保留数据集的多样性。在我们的实验中，CaR仅高效地选择了Alpaca IT数据的1.96%，但在GPT-4评估中，AlpaCaR模型的性能平均优于Alpaca 32.1%。此外，我们发现，无论是预训练模型更强大还是模型参数增加，数据选择都是一个一致的范式。我们的方法使用了包含550M参数的紧凑模型，并且仅需当前方法财务支出的11.2%，从而增强了其实用性。

[No.227] Consolidating Ranking and Relevance Predictions of Large Language Models through Post-Processing
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 通过后处理 consolidating 大型语言模型的排名和相关性预测
类别: 
作者: Le Yan;Zhen Qin;Honglei Zhuang;Rolf Jagerman;Xuanhui Wang;Michael Bendersky;Harrie Oosterhuis;

类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.25/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11791
英摘: The powerful generative abilities of large language models (LLMs) show potential in generating relevance labels for search applications. Previous work has found that directly asking about relevancy, such as ``How relevant is document A to query Q?", results in sub-optimal ranking. Instead, the pairwise ranking prompting (PRP) approach produces promising ranking performance through asking about pairwise comparisons, e.g., ``Is document A more relevant than document B to query Q?". Thus, while LLMs are effective at their ranking ability, this is not reflected in their relevance label generation. In this work, we propose a post-processing method to consolidate the relevance labels generated by an LLM with its powerful ranking abilities. Our method takes both LLM generated relevance labels and pairwise preferences. The labels are then altered to satisfy the pairwise preferences of the LLM, while staying as close to the original values as possible. Our experimental results indicate that our approach effectively balances label accuracy and ranking performance. Thereby, our work shows it is possible to combine both the ranking and labeling abilities of LLMs through post-processing.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）的强大生成能力在为搜索应用生成相关标签方面显示出潜力。以往的研究发现，直接询问关于相关性的内容，例如“文档A与查询Q的相关性如何？”会导致排名效果不佳。相反，通过询问两两比较问题，如“文档A相对于文档B而言，对查询Q的相关性更高吗？”的对偶排名提示（PRP）方法能够产生令人满意的排名性能。因此，尽管LLMs在其排名能力上表现有效，但这种效果并未体现在它们生成的相关标签上。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种后处理方法，以整合LLM生成的相关标签与其强大的排名能力。该方法同时考虑了LLM生成的相关标签和两两偏好。标签随后被调整以满足LLM的两两偏好，同时尽可能保持原始值的准确性。我们的实验结果表明，我们的方法有效地平衡了标签准确性和排名性能。因此，我们的工作展示了可以通过后处理将LLM的排名能力和标签能力结合起来的可能性。

[No.228] DL-QAT: Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Quantization-Aware Training for Large Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: DL-QAT：分解权重的低秩量化感知训练用于大型语言模型
类别: 
作者: Wenjing Ke;Zhe Li;Dong Li;Lu Tian;Emad Barsoum;
机构: Advanced Micro Devices, Inc.;
类型: Industry
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-industry.10/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09223
英摘: Improving the efficiency of inference in Large Language Models (LLMs) is a critical area of research. Post-training Quantization (PTQ) is a popular technique, but it often faces challenges at low-bit levels, particularly in downstream tasks. Quantization-aware Training (QAT) can alleviate this problem, but it requires significantly more computational resources. To tackle this, we introduced Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Quantization-Aware Training (DL-QAT), which merges the advantages of QAT while training only less than 1% of the total parameters. Specifically, we introduce a group-specific quantization magnitude to adjust the overall scale of each quantization group. Within each quantization group, we use LoRA matrices to update the weight size and direction in the quantization space. We validated the effectiveness of our method on the LLaMA and LLaMA2 model families. The results show significant improvements over our baseline method across different quantization granularities. For instance, for LLaMA-7B, our approach outperforms the previous state-of-the-art method by 4.2% in MMLU on 3-bit LLaMA-7B model. Additionally, our quantization results on pre-trained models also surpass previous QAT methods, demonstrating the superior performance and efficiency of our approach.
中摘: 提高大型语言模型（LLMs）推理效率是研究中的一个重要领域。后训练量化（PTQ）是一种流行的技术，但在低位数级别时往往会面临下游任务中的挑战。量化感知训练（QAT）可以缓解这一问题，但需要显著更多的计算资源。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了分解权重的低秩量化感知训练（DL-QAT），它结合了QAT的优势，同时仅训练不到1%的总参数量。具体来说，我们在每个量化组中引入了一个特定于组的量化幅度来调整每个量化组的整体规模。在每个量化组内部，我们使用LoRA矩阵来更新量化空间中的权重大小和方向。我们在LLaMA和LLaMA2模型家族上验证了该方法的有效性。结果显示，在不同的量化粒度下，我们的方法相对于基线方法有显著改进。例如，对于LLaMA-7B模型，我们的方法在3位量化LLaMA-7B模型上的MMLU基准测试中比之前最先进的方法提高了4.2%。此外，我们在预训练模型上的量化结果也超过了之前的QAT方法，证明了我们方法的优越性能和效率。

[No.229] Efficient Pointwise-Pairwise Learning-to-Rank for News Recommendation
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 高效的点对点学习排序技术在新闻推荐中的应用
类别: 
作者: Nithish Kannen;Yao Ma;Gerrit J.j. Van Den Burg;Jean Baptiste Faddoul;
机构: Amazon;Zalando SE;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.723/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17711
英摘: News recommendation is a challenging task that involves personalization based on the interaction history and preferences of each user. Recent works have leveraged the power of pretrained language models (PLMs) to directly rank news items by using inference approaches that predominately fall into three categories: pointwise, pairwise, and listwise learning-to-rank. While pointwise methods offer linear inference complexity, they fail to capture crucial comparative information between items that is more effective for ranking tasks. Conversely, pairwise and listwise approaches excel at incorporating these comparisons but suffer from practical limitations: pairwise approaches are either computationally expensive or lack theoretical guarantees, and listwise methods often perform poorly in practice. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for PLM-based news recommendation that integrates both pointwise relevance prediction and pairwise comparisons in a scalable manner. We present a rigorous theoretical analysis of our framework, establishing conditions under which our approach guarantees improved performance. Extensive experiments show that our approach outperforms the state-of-the-art methods on the MIND and Adressa news recommendation datasets.
中摘: 新闻推荐是一项具有挑战性的任务，涉及根据每位用户的交互历史和偏好进行个性化推荐。最近的研究利用预训练语言模型（PLMs）的力量，通过三种主要的学习排序方法——点wise、pairwise 和 listwise 方法——直接对新闻项目进行排名：点wise 方法提供线性推理复杂度，但无法捕捉对排名任务更为有效的项目之间关键的比较信息。相比之下，pairwise 和 listwise 方法擅长整合这些比较，但在实践中存在实际限制：pairwise 方法要么计算成本高昂，要么缺乏理论保证，而 listwise 方法在实践中往往表现不佳。在本文中，我们提出了一种新的基于 PLMs 的新闻推荐框架，该框架以可扩展的方式整合了点wise 相关性预测和 pairwise 比较。我们对这一框架进行了严格的理论分析，并建立了确保我们的方法能够提高性能的条件。广泛的实验表明，我们的方法在 MIND 和 Adressa 新闻推荐数据集上优于最先进的方法。

[No.230] Eigen Attention: Attention in Low-Rank Space for KV Cache Compression
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: Eigen 注意力：低秩空间中的KV缓存压缩注意力机制
类别: 
作者: Utkarsh Saxena;Gobinda Saha;Sakshi Choudhary;Kaushik Roy;
机构: Purdue University;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.899/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.05646
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) represent a groundbreaking advancement in the domain of natural language processing due to their impressive reasoning abilities. Recently, there has been considerable interest in increasing the context lengths for these models to enhance their applicability to complex tasks. However, at long context lengths and large batch sizes, the key-value (KV) cache, which stores the attention keys and values, emerges as the new bottleneck in memory usage during inference. To address this, we propose Eigen Attention, which performs the attention operation in a low-rank space, thereby reducing the KV cache memory overhead. Our proposed approach is orthogonal to existing KV cache compression techniques and can be used synergistically with them. Through extensive experiments over OPT, MPT, and Llama model families, we demonstrate that Eigen Attention results in up to 40% reduction in KV cache sizes and up to 60% reduction in attention operation latency with minimal drop in performance. Code is available at https://github.com/UtkarshSaxena1/EigenAttn.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在自然语言处理领域代表了一项革命性的进展，得益于它们出色的推理能力。最近，人们越来越关注增加这些模型的上下文长度，以增强其在复杂任务中的适用性。然而，在长上下文长度和大批次大小的情况下，关键值（KV）缓存——用于存储注意力键和值——成为了推理过程中内存使用的新瓶颈。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种Eigen Attention方法，它在低秩空间中执行注意力操作，从而减少KV缓存的内存开销。我们的方法与现有的KV缓存压缩技术互不干扰，并且可以与它们协同使用。通过在OPT、MPT和Llama模型家族上进行广泛的实验，我们证明了Eigen Attention可以使KV缓存的大小最多减少40%，同时使注意力操作的延迟最多减少60%，且性能下降最小。相关代码可在https://github.com/UtkarshSaxena1/EigenAttn获取。

[No.231] Enhancing Alignment using Curriculum Learning & Ranked Preferences
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 使用课程学习与排序偏好增强对齐
类别: 
作者: Pulkit Pattnaik;Rishabh Maheshwary;Kelechi Ogueji;Vikas Yadav;Sathwik Tejaswi Madhusudhan;
机构: ServiceNow;

英摘: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is an effective technique that leverages pairwise preference data (usually one chosen and rejected response pair per user prompt) to align LLMs to human preferences. In practice, multiple responses can exist for a given prompt with varying quality relative to each other. With availability of such quality ratings for multiple responses, we propose utilizing these responses to create multiple preference pairs for a given prompt. Our work focuses on systematically using the constructed multiple preference pair in DPO training via curriculum learning methodology. In particular, we order these multiple pairs of preference data from easy to hard (emulating curriculum training) according to various criteria. We show detailed comparisons of our proposed approach to the standard single-pair DPO setting. Our method, which we call Curry-DPO consistently shows increased performance gains on MTbench, Vicuna, WizardLM, and the UltraFeedback test set, highlighting its effectiveness. More specifically, Curry-DPO achieves a score of 7.43 on MT-bench with Zephy-7B model outperforming majority of existing LLMs with similar parameter size. Curry-DPO also achieves the highest adjusted win rates on Vicuna, WizardLM, and UltraFeedback test datasets (90.7%, 87.1%, and 87.9% respectively) in our experiments, with notable gains of upto 7.5% when compared to standard DPO technique. We release the preference pairs used in alignment at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/ServiceNow-AI/Curriculum_DPO_preferences
中摘: 直接偏好优化（DPO）是一种有效的技术，它利用成对偏好数据（通常每个用户提示包含一个选择和一个拒绝的响应配对）来使语言模型（LLM）与人类偏好相一致。在实践中，对于给定的提示，可能存在多个不同质量的响应。有了这些响应的质量评分，我们建议利用这些评分创建多个成对偏好数据，以供给定提示使用。我们的工作集中在通过课程学习方法系统地利用构建的多个成对偏好数据进行DPO训练。特别是，我们根据各种标准将这些多个成对偏好数据从简单到复杂排序（模拟课程训练）。我们在标准单一成对DPO设置中详细比较了我们提出的方法。我们的方法，我们称之为Curry-DPO，在MTbench、Vicuna、WizardLM和UltraFeedback测试集上表现出持续的性能提升，突显了其有效性。具体来说，Curry-DPO在使用Zephy-7B模型时在MT-bench上的得分为7.43，显著优于其他具有相似参数量的大多数现有语言模型。在我们的实验中，Curry-DPO在Vicuna、WizardLM和UltraFeedback测试数据集上的调整后胜率最高（分别为90.7%、87.1%和87.9%），并且与标准DPO技术相比，性能提高了高达7.5%。我们发布了用于对齐的偏好配对，可在以下链接获取：https://huggingface.co/datasets/ServiceNow-AI/Curriculum_DPO_preferences

[No.233] Exploring Automated Keyword Mnemonics Generation with Large Language Models via Overgenerate-and-Rank
来源: EMNLP 2025

标题: 通过过生成和排序来探索大规模语言模型在自动生成关键词助记词方面的应用
类别: 
作者: Jaewook Lee;Hunter McNichols;Andrew Lan;

英摘: In this paper, we study an under-explored area of language and vocabulary learning: keyword mnemonics, a technique for memorizing vocabulary through memorable associations with a target word via a verbal cue. Typically, creating verbal cues requires extensive human effort and is quite time-consuming, necessitating an automated method that is more scalable. We propose a novel overgenerate-and-rank method via prompting large language models (LLMs) to generate verbal cues and then ranking them according to psycholinguistic measures and takeaways from a pilot user study. To assess cue quality, we conduct both an automated evaluation of imageability and coherence, as well as a human evaluation involving English teachers and learners. 中摘: 在本文中，我们研究了语言和词汇学习的一个未充分探索的领域：关键词记忆法，这是一种通过目标词汇与口头提示的有纪念意义的关联来记忆词汇的技术。通常，创建口头提示需要大量的人力投入，并且耗时较长，因此需要一种更易于扩展的自动化方法。我们提出了一种新颖的“过生成和排序”方法，通过促使大型语言模型（LLMs）生成口头提示，然后根据心理语言学指标和一项试点用户研究的收获对其进行排名。为了评估提示的质量，我们进行了自动化的图像性和连贯性评价，以及由英语教师和学习者参与的人工评价。结果显示，LLM生成的记忆法在图像性、连贯性和感知实用性方面与人工生成的记忆法相当，但由于语言学习者背景和偏好的多样性，仍有很大的改进空间。

来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题:  Fantastical 序列生成与查找：通过状态跟踪约束解码和重排序实现的忠实高效 API 调用生成
类别: 
作者: Zhuoer Wang;Leonardo F. R. Ribeiro;Alexandros Papangelis;Rohan Mukherjee;Tzu-Yen Wang;Xinyan Zhao;Arijit Biswas;James Caverlee;Angeliki Metallinou;

英摘: API call generation is the cornerstone of large language models' tool-using ability that provides access to the larger world. However, existing supervised and in-context learning approaches suffer from high training costs, poor data efficiency, and generated API calls that can be unfaithful to the API documentation and the user's request. To address these limitations, we propose an output-side optimization approach called FANTASE. 中摘: API调用生成是大型语言模型具备工具使用能力的基础，能够提供访问更广阔世界的能力。然而，现有的监督学习和上下文学习方法存在高训练成本、数据效率低以及生成的API调用可能与API文档和用户请求不一致的问题。为解决这些限制，我们提出了一种名为FANTASE的输出侧优化方法。FANTASE的两个独特贡献是其状态跟踪约束解码（SCD）和重排序组件。SCD动态地以Token Search Trie的形式纳入适当的API约束，以实现高效且有保证的生成忠实度，符合API文档的要求。重排序组件通过利用轻量级模型作为鉴别器来重新排列大型语言模型的束搜索候选生成，从而有效地引入监督信号。我们在DSTC8和API Bank数据集中展示了FANTASE在API调用生成准确性、推理效率和上下文效率方面的优越性能。

[No.235] Fast Forwarding Low-Rank Training
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 低秩训练快进
类别: 
作者: Adir Rahamim;Naomi Saphra;Sara Kangaslahti;Yonatan Belinkov;
机构: Technion – Israel Institute of Technology;Harvard University;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.535/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.04206
英摘: Parameter efficient finetuning methods like low-rank adaptation (LoRA) aim to reduce the computational costs of finetuning pretrained Language Models (LMs). Enabled by these low-rank settings, we propose an even more efficient optimization strategy: Fast Forward, a simple and effective approach to accelerate large segments of training. In a Fast Forward stage, we repeat the most recent optimizer step until the loss stops improving on a tiny validation set. By alternating between regular optimization steps and Fast Forward stages, Fast Forward provides up to an 87\% reduction in FLOPs and up to an 81\% reduction in train time over standard SGD with Adam. We validate Fast Forward by finetuning various models on different tasks and demonstrate that it speeds up training without compromising model performance. Additionally, we analyze when and how to apply Fast Forward.
中摘: 像低秩适应（LoRA）这样的参数高效微调方法旨在减少预训练语言模型（PLMs）微调的计算成本。得益于这些低秩设置，我们提出了一种更加高效的优化策略：快速前馈（Fast Forward），这是一种简单而有效的加速大规模训练段落的方法。在快速前馈阶段，我们重复最近的优化步骤，直到在小型验证集上损失不再改善为止。通过交替使用常规优化步骤和快速前馈阶段，快速前馈相比标准的Adam与SGD方法，可以提供高达87%的FLOPs（浮点运算次数）减少和高达81%的训练时间减少。我们通过在不同任务上微调各种模型来验证快速前馈的有效性，并证明它可以在不牺牲模型性能的情况下加速训练。此外，我们还分析了何时以及如何应用快速前馈。

[No.236] Few-shot Prompting for Pairwise Ranking: An Effective Non-Parametric Retrieval Model
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题:  few-shot 提示在对偶排名中的应用：一种有效的非参数检索模型
类别: 
作者: Nilanjan Sinhababu;Andrew Parry;Debasis Ganguly;Debasis Samanta;Pabitra Mitra;

英摘: A supervised ranking model, despite its advantage of being effective, usually involves complex processing - typically multiple stages of task-specific pre-training and fine-tuning. This has motivated researchers to explore simpler pipelines leveraging large language models (LLMs) that are capable of working in a zero-shot manner. However, since zero-shot inference does not make use of a training set of pairs of queries and their relevant documents, its performance is mostly worse than that of supervised models, which are trained on such example pairs. Motivated by the existing findings that training examples generally improve zero-shot performance, in our work, we explore if this also applies to ranking models. More specifically, given a query and a pair of documents, the preference prediction task is improved by augmenting examples of preferences for similar queries from a training set. [No.237] FIRST: Faster Improved Listwise Reranking with Single Token Decoding
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: FIRSTR：使用单令牌解码的更快改进的列表级重排序
类别: 
作者: Revanth Gangi Reddy;JaeHyeok Doo;Yifei Xu;Md Arafat Sultan;Deevya Swain;Avirup Sil;Heng Ji;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;Lapis Labs;IBM;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.491/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.15657
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced the field of information retrieval, particularly for reranking. Listwise LLM rerankers have showcased superior performance and generalizability compared to existing supervised approaches. However, conventional listwise LLM reranking methods lack efficiency as they provide ranking output in the form of a generated ordered sequence of candidate passage identifiers. Further, they are trained with the typical language modeling objective, which treats all ranking errors uniformly--potentially at the cost of misranking highly relevant passages. Addressing these limitations, we introduce FIRST, a novel listwise LLM reranking approach leveraging the output logits of the first generated identifier to directly obtain a ranked ordering of the candidates. Further, we incorporate a learning-to-rank loss during training, prioritizing ranking accuracy for the more relevant passages. Empirical results demonstrate that FIRST accelerates inference by 50% while maintaining a robust ranking performance with gains across the BEIR benchmark. Finally, to illustrate the practical effectiveness of listwise LLM rerankers, we investigate their application in providing relevance feedback for retrievers during inference. Our results show that LLM rerankers can provide a stronger distillation signal compared to cross-encoders, yielding substantial improvements in retriever recall after relevance feedback.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在信息检索领域取得了显著进展，特别是在重排序方面。列表级LLM重排序器在性能和泛化能力上优于现有的监督方法。然而，传统的列表级LLM重排序方法缺乏效率，因为它们以生成的候选段落标识符有序序列的形式提供排名输出。此外，这些方法在训练时采用的是典型的语言建模目标，这可能导致对高度相关的段落进行错误的排名。为了解决这些限制，我们提出了FIRST，这是一种新颖的列表级LLM重排序方法，利用第一个生成的标识符的输出logits直接获得候选者的排名顺序。在此基础上，我们在训练过程中引入了学习排序损失，优先提高更相关段落的排名准确性。实验结果显示，FIRST在保持鲁棒排名性能的同时，将推理速度提升了50%，并在BEIR基准测试中表现出色。最后，为了展示列表级LLM重排序器的实际效果，我们研究了它们在检索器推理过程中提供相关性反馈的应用。我们的结果显示，LLM重排序器相较于交叉编码器能提供更强的精炼信号，从而在相关性反馈后显著提高了检索器的召回率。

[No.240] HyQE: Ranking Contexts with Hypothetical Query Embeddings
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 基于假设查询嵌入的上下文排名
类别: 
作者: Weichao Zhou;Jiaxin Zhang;Hilaf Hasson;Anu Singh;Wenchao Li;
机构: Boston University;Intuit;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.761/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15262
英摘: In retrieval-augmented systems, context ranking techniques are commonly employed to reorder the retrieved contexts based on their relevance to a user query. A standard approach is to measure this relevance through the similarity between contexts and queries in the embedding space. However, such similarity often fails to capture the relevance. Alternatively, large language models (LLMs) have been used for ranking contexts. However, they can encounter scalability issues when the number of candidate contexts grows and the context window sizes of the LLMs remain constrained. Additionally, these approaches require fine-tuning LLMs with domain-specific data. In this work, we introduce a scalable ranking framework that combines embedding similarity and LLM capabilities without requiring LLM fine-tuning. Our framework uses a pre-trained LLM to hypothesize the user query based on the retrieved contexts and ranks the context based on the similarity between the hypothesized queries and the user query. Our framework is efficient at inference time and is compatible with many other retrieval and ranking techniques. Experimental results show that our method improves the ranking performance across multiple benchmarks. The complete code and data are available at https://github.com/zwc662/hyqe
中摘: 在检索增强系统中，常用的上下文排名技术是根据上下文与用户查询的相关性重新排序检索到的上下文。一种标准方法是通过嵌入空间中的相似度来衡量这种相关性。然而，这种相似度往往无法准确捕捉相关性。作为替代方案，大语言模型（LLM）也被用于排名上下文，但当候选上下文的数量增加时，如果LLM的上下文窗口大小仍然受到限制，它们可能会遇到可扩展性问题。此外，这些方法需要使用领域特定的数据对LLM进行微调。在这项工作中，我们介绍了一种无需对LLM进行微调即可结合嵌入相似度和LLM能力的可扩展排名框架。该框架使用预训练的LLM基于检索到的上下文来假设用户查询，并根据假设查询与用户查询之间的相似度对上下文进行排名。该框架在推理时间上非常高效，并且兼容许多其他检索和排名技术。实验结果表明，我们的方法在多个基准测试中提高了排名性能。完整的代码和数据可在https://github.com/zwc662/hyqe获取。

[No.242] Learning to Rank Salient Content for Query-focused Summarization
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 查询导向摘要中重要内容的学习排序
类别: 
作者: Sajad Sotudeh;Nazli Goharian;
机构: Georgetown University;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.838/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00324
英摘: This study examines the potential of integrating Learning-to-Rank (LTR) with Query-focused Summarization (QFS) to enhance the summary relevance via content prioritization. Using a shared secondary decoder with the summarization decoder, we carry out the LTR task at the segment level. Compared to the state-of-the-art, our model outperforms on QMSum benchmark (all metrics) and matches on SQuALITY benchmark (2 metrics) as measured by Rouge and BertScore while offering a lower training overhead. Specifically, on the QMSum benchmark, our proposed system achieves improvements, particularly in Rouge-L (+0.42) and BertScore (+0.34), indicating enhanced understanding and relevance. While facing minor challenges in Rouge-1 and Rouge-2 scores on the SQuALITY benchmark, the model significantly excels in Rouge-L (+1.47), underscoring its capability to generate coherent summaries. Human evaluations emphasize the efficacy of our method in terms of relevance and faithfulness of the generated summaries, without sacrificing fluency. A deeper analysis reveals our model's superiority over the state-of-the-art for broad queries, as opposed to specific ones, from a qualitative standpoint. We further present an error analysis of our model, pinpointing challenges faced and suggesting potential directions for future research in this field.
中摘: 这项研究探讨了将学习排序（Learning-to-Rank，LTR）与查询聚焦摘要（Query-focused Summarization，QFS）相结合，以通过内容优先级提升摘要的相关性。我们使用共享的二级解码器与摘要解码器来在段落级别执行LTR任务。与当前最先进的方法相比，我们的模型在QMSum基准测试中（所有指标）表现出色，在SQuALITY基准测试中（两个指标）则与之相当，同时训练开销更低。具体来说，在QMSum基准测试中，我们提出的系统在Rouge-L（+0.42）和BertScore（+0.34）上取得了改进，表明其理解能力和相关性有所增强。尽管在SQuALITY基准测试中的Rouge-1和Rouge-2分数面临一些挑战，但该模型在Rouge-L（+1.47）上的表现显著优于其他方法，突显了其生成连贯摘要的能力。人类评估强调了我们方法在生成摘要的相关性和忠实性方面的有效性，而不牺牲流畅性。深入分析显示，从定性的角度来看，我们的模型在处理广泛查询方面优于最先进的方法，而在处理具体查询时则相对逊色。我们还对模型进行了错误分析，指出了所面临的挑战，并提出了未来研究方向的潜在建议。
