[No.243] Leveraging Estimated Transferability Over Human Intuition for Model Selection in Text Ranking
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 利用估计的转移性而非人类直觉进行文本排序模型选择
类别: 
作者: Jun Bai;Zhuofan Chen;Zhenzi Li;Hanhua Hong;Jianfei Zhang;Chen Li;Chenghua Lin;Wenge Rong;
机构: Beihang University;University of Manchester;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.688/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16198
英摘: Text ranking has witnessed significant advancements, attributed to the utilization of dual-encoder enhanced by Pre-trained Language Models (PLMs). Given the proliferation of available PLMs, selecting the most effective one for a given dataset has become a non-trivial challenge. As a promising alternative to human intuition and brute-force fine-tuning, Transferability Estimation (TE) has emerged as an effective approach to model selection. However, current TE methods are primarily designed for classification tasks, and their estimated transferability may not align well with the objectives of text ranking. To address this challenge, we propose to compute the expected rank as transferability, explicitly reflecting the model's ranking capability. Furthermore, to mitigate anisotropy and incorporate training dynamics, we adaptively scale isotropic sentence embeddings to yield an accurate expected rank score. Our resulting method, Adaptive Ranking Transferability (AiRTran), can effectively capture subtle differences between models. On challenging model selection scenarios across various text ranking datasets, it demonstrates significant improvements over previous classification-oriented TE methods, human intuition, and ChatGPT with minor time consumption.
中摘: 文本排名见证了显著的进步，这归功于预训练语言模型（PLMs）增强的双编码器的应用。鉴于可用的PLMs数量激增，选择最适合给定数据集的模型已成为一个非平凡的挑战。作为人类直觉和暴力微调的有前途的替代方案，转移性估计（TE）已经发展成为一种有效的模型选择方法。然而，当前的TE方法主要针对分类任务设计，它们估计的转移性可能与文本排名的目标不完全一致。为了解决这一挑战，我们提出将预期排名计算为转移性，明确反映模型的排名能力。此外，为了缓解各向异性并结合训练动态，我们适应性地缩放了各向同性的句子嵌入，以获得准确的预期排名分数。我们的方法Adaptive Ranking Transferability（AiRTran）能够有效捕捉模型之间的微妙差异。在各种文本排名数据集上面对具有挑战性的模型选择场景时，它在时间和性能方面都优于之前的分类导向的TE方法、人类直觉以及ChatGPT。

[No.244] LINKAGE: Listwise Ranking among Varied-Quality References for Non-Factoid QA Evaluation via LLMs
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 链路：通过大规模语言模型（LLMs）对各种质量的参考进行列表式排名以评估非事实型问答（Non-Factoid QA）
类别: 
作者: Sihui Yang;Keping Bi;Wanqing Cui;Jiafeng Guo;Xueqi Cheng;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.410/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14744
英摘: Non-Factoid (NF) Question Answering (QA) is challenging to evaluate due to diverse potential answers and no objective criterion. The commonly used automatic evaluation metrics like ROUGE or BERTScore cannot accurately measure semantic similarities or answers from different perspectives. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been resorted to for NFQA evaluation due to their compelling performance on various NLP tasks. Common approaches include pointwise scoring of each candidate answer and pairwise comparisons between answers. Inspired by the evolution from pointwise to pairwise to listwise in learning-to-rank methods, we propose a novel listwise NFQA evaluation approach, that utilizes LLMs to rank candidate answers in a list of reference answers sorted by descending quality. Moreover, for NF questions that do not have multi-grade or any golden answers, we leverage LLMs to generate the reference answer list of various quality to facilitate the listwise evaluation. Extensive experimental results on three NFQA datasets, i.e., ANTIQUE, the TREC-DL-NF, and WebGLM show that our method has significantly higher correlations with human annotations compared to automatic scores and common pointwise and pairwise approaches.
中摘: 非事实性（NF）问答评估具有挑战性，因为可能的答案多样且缺乏客观标准。常用的自动评估指标如ROUGE或BERTScore无法准确衡量语义相似度或从不同角度给出的答案。最近，由于大型语言模型（LLMs）在各种自然语言处理（NLP）任务上的出色表现，LLMs被用于NFQA评估。常见的方法包括为每个候选答案进行点wise评分以及对答案进行pairwise比较。受学习排序方法从点wise到pairwise再到listwise演变的启发，我们提出了一种新颖的listwise NFQA评估方法，该方法利用LLMs对参考答案列表（按质量降序排列）中的候选答案进行排序。此外，对于没有多级或任何标准答案的NF问题，我们利用LLMs生成不同质量级别的参考答案列表，以促进listwise评估。在三个NFQA数据集，即ANTIQUE、TREC-DL-NF和WebGLM上的广泛实验结果显示，我们的方法与人工注释的相关性显著高于自动评分及常见的pointwise和pairwise方法。

[No.249] Make Large Language Model a Better Ranker
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: rank
标题: 提升大型语言模型的排名能力
类别: 
作者: Wen-Shuo Chao;Zhi Zheng;Hengshu Zhu;Hao Liu;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;University of Science and Technology of China;Chinese Academy of Sciences;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.51/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19181
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate robust capabilities across various fields, leading to a paradigm shift in LLM-enhanced Recommender System (RS). Research to date focuses on point-wise and pair-wise recommendation paradigms, which are inefficient for LLM-based recommenders due to high computational costs. However, existing list-wise approaches also fall short in ranking tasks due to misalignment between ranking objectives and next-token prediction. Moreover, these LLM-based methods struggle to effectively address the order relation among candidates, particularly given the scale of ratings. To address these challenges, this paper introduces the large language model framework with Aligned Listwise Ranking Objectives (ALRO). ALRO is designed to bridge the gap between the capabilities of LLMs and the nuanced requirements of ranking tasks. Specifically, ALRO employs explicit feedback in a listwise manner by introducing soft lambda loss, a customized adaptation of lambda loss designed for optimizing order relations. This mechanism provides more accurate optimization goals, enhancing the ranking process. Additionally, ALRO incorporates a permutation-sensitive learning mechanism that addresses position bias, a prevalent issue in generative models, without imposing additional computational burdens during inference. Our evaluative studies reveal that ALRO outperforms both existing embedding-based recommendation methods and LLM-based recommendation baselines.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在各个领域展示了强大的能力，这导致了基于LLM的推荐系统（RS）范式的转变。迄今为止的研究主要集中在点式和对式推荐范式上，这些方法对于基于LLM的推荐器来说效率低下，因为它们需要大量的计算成本。然而，现有的列表式方法在排序任务中也存在不足，主要是因为排名目标与下一个词预测之间存在不匹配。此外，这些基于LLM的方法难以有效地处理候选项目之间的顺序关系，尤其是在面对评分规模时更为困难。为了解决这些挑战，本文引入了带有对齐列表式排名目标（ALRO）的大规模语言模型框架。ALRO旨在弥合LLM能力和排名任务复杂需求之间的差距。具体而言，ALRO通过引入软λ损失机制，以列表式方式利用显式反馈，这是一种针对优化顺序关系定制的λ损失适应方法。这一机制提供了更准确的优化目标，增强了排序过程。此外，ALRO还整合了一种敏感于排列的学习机制，该机制解决了生成模型中的位置偏差问题，而不会在推理过程中增加额外的计算负担。我们的评估研究表明，ALRO在性能上优于现有的基于嵌入的推荐方法以及基于LLM的基本推荐基准。
